Without massive pre-training or specialized data infrastructure, our framework achieves strong performance in both simulation and real-world environments. Extensive experiments further reveal several key properties of robot ICL, including action, semantic, composition, and affordance discrimination. We will fully open-source our data and training pipeline to facilitate systematic and reproducible research on robot ICL. The project page can be found at this https URL .

</details>


### [35] [Counterfactual Video Generation Enables Scalable Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38172)
*Zihan Wang,Zhen Wu,Pieter Abbeel,Rocky Duan,Jitendra Malik,Carmelo Sferrazza,C. Karen Liu,Guanya Shi,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PRISM将少量真实人-物交互视频放大为多样化训练集，并通过real-to-sim-to-real迁移，使人形机器人无需真机微调即可仅凭机载深度完成多类物体的抓取、搬运与放置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过视觉模仿学习人形机器人的全身移动操作技能前景广阔，但采集多样、高质量且能清晰展示全身与物体无遮挡交互的视频成本高，成为规模化训练的实际瓶颈。

Method: 提出PRISM，一种real-to-sim-to-real框架：首先基于少量真实示例，通过视频到视频生成合成数百个多样化的反事实人-物交互视频；随后采用接触锚定的real-to-sim管线重建人与物体运动，将不完美的视频数据重定向为物理合理的轨迹；最后利用反事实视频的类内多样性训练单一策略，使其泛化到同类未见物体。

Result: 在真实机器人上部署该策略，无需任何真实世界微调。仅使用机载深度观测，人形机器人即可拾取、搬运和放下箱子、桶、储物箱和球等物体，并适应新实例、新尺寸和新初始构型。

Conclusion: PRISM缓解了真实交互视频采集瓶颈，验证了以少量真实视频驱动real-to-sim-to-real视觉模仿，可学习具有泛化能力的人形机器人移动操作技能。

Abstract: Teaching humanoids loco-manipulation skills, such as carrying diverse objects, via visual imitation is a promising path toward generalist robots. However, collecting diverse, high-quality interaction videos, such as clips that clearly show a person's full body and unoccluded interactions with objects, poses a practical barrier to scaling this approach. We propose PRISM, a real-to-sim-to-real framework that overcomes this limitation by amplifying a handful of real videos into a large, diverse training set. PRISM first generates hundreds of diverse "counterfactual" human-object interaction videos via video-to-video (V2V) generation from a few exemplar real videos. Our contact-anchored real-to-sim pipeline then reconstructs both human and object motions, retargeting this imperfect video data into physically plausible trajectories. The intra-class variability across these counterfactual videos lets us train a single policy that generalizes to unseen objects within each category. We demonstrate the full pipeline by deploying this policy on a real robot without any real-world fine-tuning. Using only onboard depth observations, our humanoid picks up, carries, and drops objects, including boxes, barrels, bins, and balls, across novel instances, sizes, and initial configurations.

</details>


### [36] [Rho: A Foundation for Efficiently Adaptable VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38164)
*Rho Team,Simran Bagaria,Daphne Chen,Dean Fortier,Jianlong Fu,Michael Harrison,Tess Hellebrekers,Neel Joshi,Andrey Kolobov,Dalton Moore,Galen Mullins,Michael Murray,Eduardo Salinas,Reuben Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Rho 是一系列开源 VLA 模型，面向双臂操作，在三种代表性机器人本体上实现数据高效的任务自适应，并具备在线适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用物理 AI 模型需兼具广泛的视觉-语言能力、跨机器人本体的精确控制以及对下游任务的高效适应。

Method: 提出 Rho 系列开源 VLA 模型，用于双臂操作；系统消融动作专家架构与训练配方，在仿真和真机实验中验证本体中期训练可提升下游适应；内置潜在策略通过纠正反馈在线学习，为冻结的流匹配动作专家选择观测条件噪声输入。

Result: Rho 在 YAM Box、UR AI Trainer、FR3 Duo 上匹配或超越现有开源 VLA，在评估的任务、本体和基线中取得最强整体性能；仅需 15 个纠正回合即可适应离线微调分布边缘的任务情况。

Conclusion: Rho 既是强大的通用机器人操作模型，也是实用的适应基础；发布基础模型和本体特定检查点，以促进研究和工业部署。

Abstract: General-purpose physical AI models must combine broad visual and linguistic capabilities with precise control across robot embodiments and efficient adaptation to downstream tasks. We introduce Rho, a family of open-weights VLA models for bimanual manipulation designed for data-light task adaptation on 3 embodiments representative of dual-arm robots across research labs and the industry -- YAM Box, UR AI Trainer, and FR3 Duo. We systematically ablate Rho's action-expert architecture and training recipe, and show in controlled simulation and physical-robot experiments that embodiment midtraining improves downstream adaptation. The resulting Rho variants for YAM Box, UR AI Trainer, and FR3 Duo match or outperform existing open-weights VLAs and achieve the strongest overall performance across the tasks, embodiments, and baselines evaluated in this report. We further demonstrate the Rho model family's built-in capacity for online adaptation: an internal latent policy learns from corrective feedback to select observation-conditioned noise inputs for the frozen flow-matching action expert. With as few as 15 corrected episodes, adapting this lightweight module enables Rho to handle task situations at the fringe of its offline finetuning distribution. Together, these results position Rho as both a strong general-purpose robotic manipulation model and a practical foundation for adaptation. We release the base Rho model and the embodiment-specific checkpoints to facilitate Rho's deployment in research experiments and practical industrial use cases.

</details>


### [37] [FORM: Robot Manipulation through Direct Material Law Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38105)
*Stepan Tretiakov,Ruihan Zhao,Cheng-Hsi Hsiao,Xingjian Li,Adam Thorpe,Hassan Iqbal,Sandeep Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu,Krishna Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FORM，通过单次机器人交互快速辨识可变形材料参数，并直接复用辨识模型规划新动作与新几何下的操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人面对未知可变形材料时缺乏其物理属性及受力运动响应先验，可靠操作需要快速在线辨识材料特性。

Method: FORM利用弱形式动量平衡，将观测到的材料运动与接触力转化为关于未知材料参数的线性方程；这些方程采用与正向仿真器一致的物质点法（MPM）离散化组装，因此辨识归结为线性最小二乘求解，所得解可直接用于预测，无需重新拟合或转换。

Result: 在四类材料上，FORM将辨识时间从迭代基线的约10–25分钟降至2–5秒，并在新运动、新初始条件和新几何上保持有竞争力的精度；在仿真与硬件上完成弹性杆插入、弹性球杆高尔夫推杆、弹塑性成形和目标体积倾倒四项任务。弹性属性估计误差在3.4%以内，弹塑性属性误差在2%以内，面团成形达到72.4–77.8% IoU，在60–160 mL目标体积下平均倾倒误差为3.8 mL。

Conclusion: 从单次交互辨识出的材料模型可复用于规划新运动或操作不同几何，实现未知可变形材料快速、可靠的机器人操作。

Abstract: When interacting with an unfamiliar deformable material, a robot lacks prior knowledge of its physical properties and how it will respond to applied forces and motion. Rapid online identification is therefore essential for reliable manipulation. We present FORM (From Observed Response to Material laws), which identifies material properties from a single robot interaction and reuses the recovered model to plan manipulation under new actions and geometries. We use weak-form momentum balance to convert observed material motion and contact forces into linear equations in the unknown material parameters. These equations are assembled using the same material point method discretization as the forward simulator, so identification reduces to linear least-squares solves whose solutions can be used directly for prediction without refitting or conversion. Across four material classes, FORM reduces identification time from roughly 10--25 minutes for iterative baselines to 2--5 seconds, while maintaining competitive accuracy on new motions, initial conditions, and geometries. We demonstrate our approach in simulation and on hardware across four manipulation tasks: elastic rod insertion, golf putting with an elastic club, elastoplastic shaping, and target-volume pouring. In each task, the model identified from a single interaction is reused to plan new motions or manipulate a different geometry. FORM estimates elastic properties within 3.4% and elastoplastic properties within 2%, achieves 72.4--77.8% IoU in dough shaping, and keeps mean pouring error at 3.8 mL across target volumes of 60--160 mL.

</details>


### [38] [CrossBFM: Distilling a Shared Latent Behavior Space Across Humanoid Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38087)
*Tan-Dzung Do,Tuan Dat Phuong,Nico Bohlinger,Cuc T. Trinh,Siwei Ju,Vien Anh Ngo,Jan Peters,Xinchao Wang,An T. Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CrossBFM，将行为基础模型的行为潜在空间视为跨具身可迁移资产，通过无机器人特定参数的统一编码器蒸馏行为空间，并用潜在条件 PPO 跟踪器实现全身控制，在多个及未见过的人形机器人上迁移三种提示模式，且大幅降低训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为基础模型虽能用单一潜在向量表示模仿运动、到达姿态或最大化奖励，但 Forward-Backward 表示训练单个机器人需数百 GPU 小时；为第二个机器人重复训练会得到互不相关的具身特定潜在空间，无法统一或迁移。

Method: 利用 retargeting 提供的帧级跨具身对应，设计无机器人特定参数的统一编码器，将行为空间同时蒸馏到所有训练具身，耗时少于 1 GPU 小时；随后训练潜在条件跟踪器，以常规 PPO 将蒸馏潜在转为全身控制，额外约 10 GPU 小时。

Result: 在三个蒸馏人形机器人上三种提示模式均可迁移：运动跟踪相比关节条件策略仅损失 0.025 rad；姿态间平滑目标到达无摔倒；41 个奖励提示均可优化。仅用四分之一运动语料回归编码器只损失 5% 跟踪性能；在机器人子集训练、未见机器人评估可恢复高达 89% 的已见机器人跟踪性能，显示对形态相似机器人的跨具身泛化。真实机器人上三种提示模式及基于流生成的潜变量也得到验证。

Conclusion: CrossBFM 表明潜在行为空间可作为跨具身可迁移资产，以极低训练成本统一多机器人行为模型，并具备对未见但形态相似机器人的泛化能力及真实部署可行性。

Abstract: Behavior Foundation Models (BFMs) give humanoids a promptable policy over a latent behavior space, enabling one single vector to represent a motion to imitate, a pose to reach, or a reward to maximize. Forward-Backward representations successfully produce such spaces, but at the cost of hundreds of GPU-hours for a single robot. Moreover, when the training process is repeated for a second robot, it produces a second space unrelated to the first, resulting in embodiment-specific latents that do not unify or transfer. We address these problems with CrossBFM, treating the latent space as the transferable asset for various embodiments. As retargeting provides frame-level cross-embodiment correspondence, we propose a unified encoder architecture with no robot-specific parameters for distilling the behavior space to address all training embodiments simultaneously in less than a GPU-hour. Following this encoder, latent-conditioned trackers turn the distilled latent into whole-body control in a conventional PPO training manner in just 10 more GPU-hours. On three distilled humanoids, all three prompting modes transfer: motion tracking with latent-conditioned policy losing only $0.025$ rad to its joint-conditioned counterpart, smooth goal reaching between poses with no falls, and reward optimization for all $41$ reward prompts. Our experiments further reveal that 1) regressing the encoder on a quarter of the motion corpus costs only $5\%$ of tracking performance and 2) training the encoder on a subset of robots and evaluating on an unseen one recovers up to $89\%$ of the tracking performance of seen robots, demonstrating cross-embodiment generalization to morphologically similar robots. We also verify the pipeline on real robots across all three prompting modes and with flow-based generated latents. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [39] [MotorMind: Scaffolding General Vision Language Models for Zero-Shot Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38078)
*Bingxuan Li,Siqi Song,Yizhuo Wu,Jiarui Yao,Tong Zhang,Huan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MotorMind：一种将通用 VLM 提出的中层动作连接到确定性机器人控制与反馈的机器人操作执行框架，无需任务专用策略训练、编码智能体或额外 grounding 工具，即可实现零样本机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型虽推动机器人操作发展，但零样本泛化仍有限且依赖专用训练，难以直接受益于快速进步的通用 VLM；现有 agentic 机器人系统常依赖外部模型与工具，增加复杂度与成本。作者希望验证：通用 VLM 能否像人类遥操作者一样，直接从观测推理、发出动作并根据执行反馈持续调整，而不依赖外部动作专家、编码智能体或 SAM3 等 grounding 工具。

Method: 提出 MotorMind 机器人操作 harness：由 VLM 生成中层动作，并接入确定性机器人控制与反馈；同时采用异步监控和后台记忆更新。该方法不进行任务专用策略训练，也不使用编码智能体或额外 grounding 工具。

Result: 在 LIBERO-PRO 基础套件上达到 66.7% 成功率，扰动设置下为 53.8%，而所评估的先前零样本方法分别最高仅为 13.3% 和 19.2%。在真实 xArm6 机器人上，同一接口在直接操作和人类扰动设置中平均成功率达 95%。更换更强 VLM 主干可进一步提升性能；剩余失败主要来自视觉 grounding、具身推理和动作知识，并随 VLM 能力提升而减少。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通用 VLM 在配备合适的中层动作表示与异步执行 harness 后，可有效完成零样本机器人操作，无需依赖外部专用模型或工具。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced robotic manipulation, but their zero-shot generalization in new tasks and environments remains limited, and their reliance on specialized training keeps them from benefiting directly from rapidly advancing general-purpose vision-language models (VLMs). In parallel, recent agentic robotic systems leverage VLMs for high-level reasoning or coding agents for robot control, but often depend on extensive external models and tools, introducing additional complexity and cost. This motivates us to ask: Can a general-purpose VLM itself operate a robot more like the human teleoperator by reasoning directly from observations, issuing actions, and continuously adapting to execution feedback, without relying on external models such as learned action experts, coding agents or grounding tools like SAM3? In this work, we introduce MotorMind, a robot manipulation harness that connects VLM-proposed mid-level actions to deterministic robot control and feedback, with asynchronous monitoring and background memory updates. Without task-specific policy training, coding agents, or additional grounding tools such as SAM3, MotorMind achieves 66.7% success on the base LIBERO-PRO suites and 53.8% under perturbations, compared with at most 13.3% and 19.2%, respectively, for the prior zero-shot methods we evaluate. The same interface reaches 95% average success on a real xArm6 robot across direct manipulation and human-perturbation settings. Replacing the backbone with a stronger VLM further improves performance, while the remaining failures - primarily due to visual grounding, embodied reasoning, and action knowledge - decrease as VLM capability improves. These results show that a general-purpose VLM, when equipped with an appropriate mid-level action representation and asynchronous execution harness, can perform effective zero-shot robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [40] [WorldLine: Action-Driven Visual Simulation for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38059)
*Shenghe Zheng,Wenbo Li,Jiyao Zhang,Bin Xia,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan,Jiaya Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 WorldLine——一种动作驱动的视觉模拟器，将可迁移的动力学学习与异构动作对齐解耦，利用超一万小时无动作机器人视频学习操作动力学，并用超两千小时、跨十余种具身的动作轨迹进行接地，从而实现高效因果推演，用于策略评估与具身规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人学习受限于经验采集与候选行为评估的高成本；视频生成模型虽可作为可扩展的视觉模拟器，但往往偏重视觉逼真度，牺牲动作跟随准确性与机器人—物体动力学的连贯性，而动作条件模拟器又依赖稀缺且难以跨不兼容控制空间共享的具身特定数据。

Method: WorldLine 将可迁移动力学学习与异构动作接地解耦：从超过 10,000 小时无动作机器人视频中学习操作动力学，并利用超过 2,000 小时、覆盖十余种具身的动作轨迹进行接地；采用图像空间动作表示作为跨具身共享控制接口；通过多视角、含失败样本的训练与关系正则化提升交互敏感预测；并使用面向机器人的少步蒸馏实现高效因果推演，同时保留动作关键运动。

Result: 在留出与域外设置下，WorldLine 保持较强视觉质量与机器人运动一致性；在失败轨迹上，机器人掩码 IoU 较最强基线提升 0.1626；在 RoboTwin 与 AgiBot 上以 74% 平均准确率预测轨迹成功，较最强基线高 1 个百分点；在未使用 RoboTwin 训练或适配的情况下，其推演使任务成功率较直接策略执行提升最多 21.4 个百分点。

Conclusion: WorldLine 是一个可扩展且高效的视觉模拟器，可用于策略评估与具身规划，兼具强视觉质量、动作一致性与跨具身泛化能力。

Abstract: Real-world robot learning is constrained by the cost of collecting experience and evaluating candidate behaviors. Video generation models offer a scalable foundation for visual simulators that predict action outcomes before physical execution. Yet they often favor visual plausibility over accurate action following and coherent robot--object dynamics, while action-conditioned simulators depend on scarce, embodiment-specific data that are difficult to share across incompatible control spaces. We introduce WorldLine, an action-driven visual simulator that decouples transferable dynamics learning from heterogeneous action grounding. WorldLine learns manipulation dynamics from more than 10,000 hours of action-free robot videos and grounds them using over 2,000 hours of action trajectories across more than ten embodiments. An image-space action representation provides a shared control interface across embodiments, while multi-view and failure-enriched training with relational regularization improves interaction-sensitive prediction. Robot-focused few-step distillation enables efficient causal rollout while preserving action-critical motion. Across held-out and out-of-domain settings, WorldLine maintains strong visual quality and robot-motion agreement; on failed trajectories, it improves robot-mask IoU by 0.1626 over the strongest baseline. It predicts trajectory success with 74% mean accuracy across RoboTwin and AgiBot, one percentage point above the strongest baseline. Without RoboTwin training or adaptation, its rollouts improve task success by up to 21.4 percentage points over direct policy execution. Together, these capabilities make WorldLine a scalable and efficient visual simulator for policy evaluation and embodied planning. More results are available at \href{ this https URL }{project page}.

</details>


### [41] [Pow3R-SLAM: Real-Time RGB-D SLAM with 3D Reconstruction Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38054)
*Christopher Kolios,Ishaan Mehta,Sasa Janjic,Yeganeh Bahoo,Sajad Saeedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Pow3R-SLAM 是一个实时 RGB-D SLAM 系统，将深度作为先验而非仅用于几何融合，在跟踪与建图中取得优于 MASt3R-SLAM 和 ORB-SLAM3 的精度、速度与地图密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 RGB-D SLAM 受深度图像稀疏性困扰；受 MASt3R-SLAM 启发，作者希望将深度作为网络预测的先验，以改善双视图 3D 重建 SLAM 的点图条件化与空洞深度推断。

Method: 提出 Pow3R-SLAM，使用 Pow3R 进行跟踪与建图；将可用深度作为先验生成条件更好的 pointmap，并利用双视图光度、深度和内参数据推断空白区域深度，而非把深度当作几何量直接融合；同时提出混合变体以提升速度。

Result: 在 TUM、7-Scenes、Replica 的 24 条序列上，相比 MASt3R-SLAM：墙钟时间快 1.6 倍，平均轨迹误差低 15%，未缩放误差低 3.1 倍，地图更稠密且 Chamfer 距离低 30%；混合变体快 2.1 倍，达 25.3 FPS，并保持更高跟踪与建图精度；相比 RGB-D 模式 ORB-SLAM3，在 TUM、7-Scenes、ETH3D-SLAM 上更准确，且完成所有 TUM 序列；在少量自相似场景中可能表现不佳。

Conclusion: 整体结果表明，将深度作为双视图 3D 重建 SLAM 的先验可以带来显著收益。

Abstract: We present Pow3R-SLAM, a real-time RGB-D simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) system that uses Pow3R for tracking and mapping. Inspired by MASt3R-SLAM, a recent work on monocular SLAM using two-view 3D reconstruction priors, we extend the work to incorporate depth as a prior on the network's prediction, rather than as geometry to fuse. Where traditional RGB-D SLAM systems struggle with sparsity in the depth images, Pow3R utilizes the available depth to give a better-conditioned pointmap, while inferring the depths in empty regions from the two-view photometric, depth, and intrinsic data. Evaluated against MASt3R-SLAM following its protocol on 24 sequences from TUM, 7-Scenes, and Replica, Pow3R-SLAM runs 1.6x faster in wall time, has 15% lower mean trajectory error, a 3.1x lower unscaled error, and produces denser maps, with a 30% lower Chamfer distance. We also introduce a hybrid variant that runs 2.1x faster than MASt3R-SLAM at 25.3 frames per second (FPS), while maintaining improved tracking and mapping accuracy. Against ORB-SLAM3 in RGB-D mode, Pow3R-SLAM is more accurate on TUM, 7-Scenes, and ETH3D-SLAM, and completes every TUM sequence. While Pow3R-SLAM can struggle on a small set of self-similar scenes, its overall performance shows that adding depth as a prior for two-view 3D reconstruction SLAM can be beneficial. A project webpage is available at: this https URL , and code will be made open-source upon acceptance.

</details>


### [42] [A QCQP-Representable IMU Pre-Integration Factor for Certifiable State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38048)
*Utkarsh Rai,Zhexin Xu,Bang-Shien Chen,David Rosen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可表示为QCQP的IMU预积分因子，首次将IMU预积分直接引入可认证估计，并在GNSS-IMU平滑中实现紧松弛与全局最优性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 惯性传感是机器人中常见且可靠的模态，将其纳入可认证估计可拓宽实际应用范围；但标准IMU预积分依赖指数映射，无法精确多项式表示，且QCQP所需辅助提升变量会导致标准SDP松弛较松。

Method: 基于Cayley映射推导IMU预积分因子，并引入显式冗余约束以收紧所得凸松弛，从而获得QCQP可表示的预积分因子；将其应用于可认证GNSS-IMU平滑。

Result: 在合成和真实世界数据上评估，所提公式产生紧松弛，并能将估计问题求解到经验证的全局最优性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现将IMU预积分直接用于可认证估计，为预积分惯性测量提供可认证估计框架，并扩大可认证估计的实用范围。

Abstract: We propose a QCQP-representable IMU pre-integration factor that enables certifiable estimation with pre-integrated inertial measurements. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to directly incorporate IMU pre-integration into certifiable estimation. Inertial sensing is a common and reliable modality in robotics, and incorporating it broadens the practical scope of certifiable estimation. The main challenges are obtaining the required algebraic structure and a sufficiently tight convex relaxation. Standard IMU pre-integration relies on the exponential map, which does not admit an exact polynomial representation. Moreover, obtaining a QCQP formulation requires auxiliary lifting variables, for which the standard semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxation can be loose. We address these issues by deriving an IMU pre-integration factor based on the Cayley map and an explicit set of redundant constraints that tighten the resulting relaxation. To validate the proposed factor, we apply it to certifiable GNSS-IMU smoothing and evaluate it on synthetic and real-world data. The results show that the proposed formulation yields tight relaxations and solves the resulting estimation problems to verified global optimality.

</details>


### [43] [EgoAlign: Bridging the Human-Humanoid Gap for Long-Range Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38046)
*Yiming Jiang,Chen Jin,Chongyang Xu,Yilun Chen,Aimin Hao,Yisheng He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoAlign 将第一视角人类演示转化为与通用连续全身控制器兼容的动作与状态监督，无需采集真实机器人演示；通过目标机器人模型与仿真器、尺度对齐、控制器闭环精修和因果回放重建机器人状态与运动 token 标签，用于微调 VLA 模型并零样本部署到实体人形机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一视角人类演示是易获取的任务经验来源，但人体尺度与控制器响应差异、机器人状态缺失，使其难以直接作为人形机器人训练监督；目标是在不收集实体机器人演示的情况下利用这些人类数据。

Method: EgoAlign 使用目标机器人模型和仿真器，通过执行反馈引导演示采集；保留用于视觉引导周期性迈步的运动参考，并通过尺度对齐与控制器在环精修来适配上半身交互几何；最后用因果回放重建对应机器人状态和运动 token 标签，与人类观测配对用于训练。随后仅用适配后的人类演示微调视觉-语言-动作模型，并零样本部署到实体人形机器人。

Result: 所得策略可完成长距离物体搬运、导航至未见目标位置，并独立评估足部交互。相比仅做运动学对齐，精修提升了仿真中手部对齐和实体抓取成功率；相比遥操作，人类采集减少了现场采集时间。

Conclusion: EgoAlign 表明，通过数据构建与控制器在环适配，可有效利用第一视角人类演示训练通用人形全身 VLA 策略，避免真实机器人演示，并提升对齐与任务成功率、降低数据采集成本。

Abstract: Egocentric human demonstrations offer an accessible source of task experience, but differences in body scale and controller response, together with missing robot states, limit their value as humanoid training supervision. We present EgoAlign, a data-construction framework that converts these demonstrations into action and state supervision compatible with a general-purpose, continuous whole-body controller, without collecting physical-robot demonstrations. Using the target-robot model and simulator, EgoAlign guides demonstration collection through execution feedback. It preserves locomotion references for visually guided periodic stepping while adapting upper-body interaction geometry through scale alignment and controller-in-the-loop refinement. A final causal replay reconstructs the corresponding robot states and motion-token labels for training with the human observations. We assess the resulting supervision by fine-tuning a vision--language--action model solely on adapted human demonstrations and deploying it zero-shot on a physical humanoid. The resulting policies perform long-range object relocation, navigation to unseen goal positions, and independently evaluated foot interaction. Refinement improves simulated hand alignment and physical pickup success over kinematic alignment alone, while human collection reduces on-site acquisition time relative to teleoperation. this https URL

</details>


### [44] [doPlan: A Variable-Horizon Dataset for Multi-Stage Language-Conditioned Planning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38028)
*Parthib Roy,Yash Tandon,Marcus Blennemann,Giovanni Tapia Lopez,Angel Martinez-Sanchez,Mohan M. Trivedi,Ross Greer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: doPlan 是首个公开、人工标注的真实世界数据集，用于研究乘客语言作为持续任务上下文，揭示语言敏感性与长期意图驱动行为之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆通过自然语言与乘客交互时，不能只理解即时命令；乘客意图可能跨多个行为阶段、依赖未来事件、指代周围主体或地标，并随驾驶条件演变。现有语言驾驶数据集多关注短时、局部交互，长期乘客意图研究不足。

Method: 提出 doPlan，基于 nuPlan 构建，包含 5,154 条人工书写的乘客指令，覆盖 169.1 小时累计指令对齐上下文和 50.9 小时唯一驾驶数据，标注窗口为 30.0 至 508.8 秒。标注涵盖即时、延迟、事件条件、持续和多阶段乘客意图，并公开数据集、标注界面及配套资源。评估四个语言条件驾驶模型。

Result: 对乘客语言的敏感性并不能可靠转化为符合请求方向的行为。在 2,161 个具有匹配未来机动示例中，首个相关机动发生在评估点后中位数 24.6 秒，仅 9.8% 落在模型常见的 5 秒预测时域内。

Conclusion: 需要将持续乘客意图与连续规划决策相连接。doPlan 提供了研究未解决目标如何被保留、如何在演变场景中接地、如何跨多阶段跟踪，以及规划器如何判断未来目标何时与当前计划相关的实验环境。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles interacting with passengers through natural language must reason beyond immediate commands. Passenger intent may span multiple stages of behavior, depend on future events, refer to surrounding agents or landmarks, and remain relevant as driving conditions evolve. Existing language-enabled driving datasets largely focus on short, localized interactions, leaving these longer-horizon forms of passenger intent comparatively underexplored. We introduce doPlan, to our knowledge the first publicly available, human-annotated real-world dataset designed to study passenger language as persistent task context. Built on nuPlan, doPlan contains 5,154 human-written passenger instructions spanning 169.1 hours of cumulative instruction-aligned context over 50.9 hours of unique driving, with annotation windows ranging from 30.0 to 508.8 s. The annotations capture immediate, deferred, event-conditioned, persistent, and multi-stage passenger intent. The dataset, annotation interface, and supporting resources are publicly available at this https URL . We evaluate four language-conditioned driving models and find that sensitivity to passenger language does not reliably translate into behavior consistent with the requested direction. More broadly, among 2,161 examples with a matched future maneuver, the first associated maneuver occurs a median of 24.6 s after the evaluation point, and only 9.8% occur within the models' common 5 s prediction horizon. These findings highlight the need to connect persistent passenger intent with successive planning decisions. doPlan provides a setting for studying how unresolved goals can be retained, grounded in evolving scenes, and tracked across multiple stages, including how a planner determines when a future goal becomes relevant to the current plan.

</details>


### [45] [Comparing Utility of Inertial, Occupancy, Semantic, and Intent Information in Human Motion Prediction During Daily Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37971)
*Max Burns,Maisha Khanum,Monroe Kennedy III,Steven H. Collins*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究基于Meta Aria眼镜采集的室内人体运动数据集，使用人体运动扩散模型评估不同信息源对室内运动预测的重要性，发现显式意图信息贡献最大，相比恒速基线提升42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需在复杂室内环境中与人共处，准确预测人类运动至关重要；但室内场景高度歧义，需明确身体运动、场景表示、眼动、语义和意图等信息的相对贡献。

Method: 招募9名无先验受试者佩戴Meta Aria眼镜执行模拟室内日常活动，覆盖大学校园10栋建筑、238分钟任务间导航数据；采用人体运动扩散模型，并逐步引入身体运动、场景表示、眼动注视、语义信息与显式意图信息进行对比评估。

Result: 相对恒速基线，预测误差降低42%；身体运动、场景表示和眼动注视均显著降低预测误差；语义信息对室内运动预测有用但弱于户外导航；显式意图信息带来的误差降低最大；眼动注视作为意图指标之一，对预测减速尤其有用，并在缺少占据地图时提供基础空间信息。

Conclusion: 显式意图估计或用户输入是更精细室内运动预测的关键；该工作为高度歧义的室内场景中人体运动预测迈出第一步，代码将在接收后公开。

Abstract: Accurate human motion prediction is crucial for robotic systems operating around people, particularly in complex indoor spaces. In this study, we assess the relative importance of different sources of information in indoor motion prediction with a human motion diffusion model. We collected a dataset of nine naive human subjects conducting simulated indoor daily activities while wearing a pair of Meta Aria glasses. This dataset includes ten buildings from a university campus, and encompasses 238 minutes of navigation between daily tasks. Overall, we demonstrate a 42% improvement beyond a constant velocity baseline. Including body motion, scene representation, and eye gaze fixation data significantly reduced prediction error. Semantic information was found to be useful for indoor motion prediction, but to a lesser degree than in outdoor navigation. Providing explicit intent information reduced error beyond any other addition, suggesting that incorporating explicit intent estimation or user input are fundamental for finer prediction of indoor motion. One of the few indicators of intent, eye gaze fixation, was found to be especially useful in predicting deceleration, and provided basic spatial information to the model in the absence of an occupancy map. These results are a first step towards predicting human motion in highly ambiguous indoor scenarios. Code will be made public upon acceptance. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [46] [PhysWAM: Physically Consistent World Action Model for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37970)
*Dhruv Parikh,Fengcheng Yu,Quankai Gao,Jiawei Yang,Junjie Ye,Maulik Bhatt,Thang Vu,Charles Ochoa,Rowan McAllister,Igor Vasiljevic,Rajgopal Kannan,Viktor Prasanna,Vitor Guizilini,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PhysWAM：在单个 flow-matching transformer 中联合去噪多视图视频、度量深度与自车运动，并通过耦合点投影（CPP）用 LiDAR 几何约束世界与动作生成，实现自动驾驶规划与零样本闭环迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 world-action 模型虽能联合预测场景演化与动作，但仅靠联合生成未必对二者施加共享的几何约束，难以保证预测与真实场景几何一致。

Method: PhysWAM 将多视图视频、度量深度和 ego motion 统一于单个 flow-matching transformer 中共同去噪；引入 Coupled Point Projection（CPP）：将生成深度反投影为 3D 点，用生成的 SE(3) ego motion 变换这些点，并最小化其与用记录 ego motion 变换后的 LiDAR 点之间的距离。该几何约束与标准 flow-matching 目标联合监督生成深度与运动。推理时仅使用简单的无标签一致性规则进行轨迹选择，无需学习评分器或模拟器反馈。

Result: 在 NAVSIM v1 与 v2 规划、零样本闭环迁移、未来视频与度量深度预测上评估：尽管选择过程简单，PhysWAM 仍取得强规划性能，并可零样本迁移到未见驾驶环境；同时生成准确的度量深度与时间一致的视频，CPP 能同时提升规划与深度预测。

Conclusion: 结果表明，场景深度与 ego motion 之间的几何关系为在简单统一模型中耦合世界生成与动作生成提供了直接途径。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) jointly predict how a scene will evolve and how an agent should act, however joint generation alone does not necessarily impose a shared geometric constraint on these predictions. We present PhysWAM, a unified world-action model for autonomous driving that co-denoises multiview video, metric depth, and ego motion within a single flow-matching transformer. To ground world and action generation in measured scene geometry, we introduce Coupled Point Projection (CPP) that unprojects the generated depth into 3D points, transforms them using the generated $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ ego motion, and minimizes their distance to LiDAR points transformed using the recorded ego motion. This geometric constraint promotes physical consistency with the measured scene by jointly supervising generated depth and motion alongside their standard flow-matching objectives. At inference, trajectory selection relies only on a simple label-free consensus rule, with no learned scorer or simulator feedback. We evaluate PhysWAM across NAVSIM v1 and v2 planning, zero-shot closed-loop transfer, and future video and metric-depth prediction. Despite PhysWAM's simple selection procedure, it achieves strong planning performance and transfers zero-shot to unseen driving environments. It also generates accurate metric depth and temporally coherent video, with CPP improving both planning and depth prediction. Together, these results demonstrate that the geometric relationship between scene depth and ego motion provides a direct way to couple world and action generation within a simple unified model.

</details>


### [47] [WayFinder: Hierarchical Visual-Language-Action for Zero-Shot Waypoint Generation and Low-Level Kinematic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37922)
*Timothy K Johnsen,Marco Levorato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WayFinder 是一个端到端闭环分层 VLA 框架，通过解耦高层任务推理与低层运动控制，无需微调即可提升机器人导航可靠性并降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型泛化能力强，但实际部署受限于执行不可靠以及针对特定机器人本体和任务进行微调的高昂计算成本。

Method: WayFinder 采用零样本、离板 MLLM 策略处理语言上下文和状态地图以生成战略航点；异步地，轻量级在板策略基于连续传感器反馈以高频执行实时运动控制。在 Microsoft AirSim 中，对四个不同复杂度环境和三种 MLLM 规模进行评估。

Result: 与基线低层策略相比，WayFinder 实现了更优的导航可靠性；仅在导航失败时查询高层 MLLM，无需微调，最小化昂贵推理，并将导航成功率最多提升 27.45%。

Conclusion: WayFinder 通过分层异步架构和按需调用 MLLM，为 VLA 模型的实际部署提供了无需微调、高效且可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Visual Language Action (VLA) models offer unprecedented generalization for autonomous robots; however, their real-world deployment is frequently bottlenecked by unreliable execution and the prohibitive computational cost of fine-tuning for specific robot embodiments and tasks. To bridge this gap, we propose WayFinder, an end-to-end, closed-loop hierarchical VLA framework that circumvents the need for fine-tuning by decoupling high-level task reasoning from low-level kinematic control. WayFinder utilizes a zero-shot, offboard Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) policy to process linguistic context and state maps for strategic waypoint generation. Asynchronously, a lightweight, onboard policy executes real-time kinematic control at high frequency based on continuous sensor feedback. We evaluate WayFinder in Microsoft AirSim, testing on four environments of varying complexity and three MLLM scales to balance prediction efficacy with computational efficiency. Our results demonstrate that WayFinder achieves superior navigation reliability compared to baseline low-level policies. By querying the high-level MLLM only during navigation failures, WayFinder eliminates the need for fine-tuning, minimizes expensive inferences, and significantly increases navigation success rates by up to 27.45%.

</details>


### [48] [ExceptionDrive: A Planning-Oriented Counterfactual Corner-Case Benchmark for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37871)
*Ziyi Luo,Zhe Sun,Yehao Lu,Lei Zhou,Lisheng Wu,Xuewei Li,Zequn Qin,Xi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ExceptionDrive 反事实规划基准，通过将危险场景注入真实 nuScenes 数据，评估规划器在罕见安全关键风险下的可靠性，并设计 Reminder Agent 提升零样本危险应对策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规驾驶基准上的平均性能无法证明规划器在罕见且安全攸关的危险场景中可靠，因此需要能够保留真实上下文并系统注入危险的反事实评测方法。

Method: 采用 VLM 辅助筛选、局部多视角编辑和质量审计，将危险注入 nuScenes 场景；构建 21 个任务，覆盖六类安全族，定义危险/冲突区域、局部安全约束和可接受响应。由于危险注入可能使原始人类轨迹失效，提出无参考评估协议，使用 Unsafe Rate (UR)、Hazard Clearance Compliance (HCC)、Hazard Proximity Response (HPR) 和 Counterfactual Trajectory Shift (CTS) 衡量核心区域侵入、间隙合规、相对于规定余量的间隙以及反事实轨迹变化。还开发 Reminder Agent，在无样本特定任务标签下，将视觉证据和共享分类体系转化为危险存在性、类型及高层建议策略的结构化记录，该记录用于引导基于 VLM 的决策智能体，但不预测轨迹也不控制车辆。

Result: 七种代表性规划器频繁侵入危险区域或提供不足间隙。零样本实验中，Reminder Agent 提供的提醒提升了策略准确率并减少了警告不足。

Conclusion: ExceptionDrive 揭示了现有规划器在安全关键危险场景中的可靠性缺口，并表明通过结构化危险提醒引导决策智能体可改善零样本危险应对策略。

Abstract: Average performance on routine driving benchmarks does not establish planner reliability under rare, safety-critical hazards. We proposed ExceptionDrive, a counterfactual planning benchmark that uses VLM-assisted screening, localized multi-view editing, and quality auditing to insert hazards into real nuScenes scenes while preserving their context. Its 21 tasks span six safety families and define hazard or conflict regions, local safety constraints, and acceptable responses. Because hazard insertion can invalidate the recorded human trajectory, our reference-free protocol evaluates edited predictions using Unsafe Rate (UR), Hazard Clearance Compliance (HCC), Hazard Proximity Response (HPR), and Counterfactual Trajectory Shift (CTS), which measure core-region intrusion, clearance compliance, clearance relative to a prescribed margin, and counterfactual trajectory change. Seven representative planners frequently intrude into hazard regions or provide insufficient clearance. We also develop a Reminder Agent that, without sample-specific task labels, converts visual evidence and the shared taxonomy into structured records of hazard presence, type, and a recommended high-level strategy. The agent neither predicts trajectories nor controls the vehicle; its records guide a VLM-based decision agent. In zero-shot experiments, the reminders improve strategy accuracy and reduce under-warning.

</details>


### [49] [Explore, Execute, Evolve: A Skill Acquisition and Reuse Loop for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37810)
*Sicheng Xie,Yitong Chen,Haidong Cao,Shunlin Lu,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboSkill 是一个通过“探索-执行-演化”闭环连接技能获取与复用的框架，旨在降低通用多模态智能体解决机器人任务的高执行成本，并提升对未见任务的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作模型和世界动作模型在机器人领域表现突出，但泛化到未见任务仍具挑战；通用多模态智能体虽能零样本解决机器人任务，却常需从零推理和探索物理世界，导致执行成本高。

Method: RoboSkill 构建 Explore、Execute、Evolve 闭环：智能体探索以收集任务相关信息，执行任务并根据反馈调整，基于执行记录演化技能库；随后复用技能指导下一轮的探索与执行。为提升效率，方法用触觉反馈补充视觉以减少物理交互不确定性，并用可复用代码增强文本指导以减少技能复用时的推理开销。

Result: 在 LIBERO-10 上，RoboSkill 将首回合成功率提升 12.5--25.0 个百分点，并在四种智能体上降低平均运行时间 7.6--72.4%；在真实机器人上，成功率提升 8.3 个百分点，成功试验的平均运行时间至少降低 14.4%。

Conclusion: RoboSkill 通过闭环技能获取与复用、触觉增强感知和可复用代码指导，有效降低机器人任务执行成本，同时提升成功率和运行效率。

Abstract: Vision-language-action and world-action models have demonstrated impressive capabilities in robotics, yet generalization to unseen tasks remains challenging. More recently, general-purpose multimodal agents have shown great potential for zero-shot robotic task solving. However, they often incur high execution costs by reasoning and exploring the physical world from scratch. To reduce these costs, we introduce RoboSkill, a framework that connects skill acquisition and reuse through an Explore, Execute, Evolve loop. Within this loop, the agent explores to gather task-relevant information, executes tasks while adapting to feedback, and evolves its skill library based on execution records. It then reuses these skills to guide exploration and execution in the next cycle, closing the loop. To improve loop efficiency, we complement vision with tactile feedback to reduce uncertainty during physical interaction. We further augment textual guidance with reusable code to reduce reasoning overhead during skill reuse. On LIBERO-10, RoboSkill improves first-episode success rates by 12.5--25.0 percentage points and reduces average runtime by 7.6--72.4% across four agents. On real robots, it improves success rates by 8.3 percentage points and reduces average runtime for successful trials by at least 14.4%.

</details>


### [50] [MVG-WAM: Multiple View Geometry-Aware World-Action Modeling for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37793)
*Wenbo Chen,Tianfu Li,Haoxuan Xu,Zhihao Cao,Zhenghan Chen,Zhengming Zhu,Zizhou Luo,Guosheng Yang,Yuan Liu,Lujia Wang,Wen Chen,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MVG-WAM，将多视角观测建模为同一物理世界的关联投影，显式引入几何约束与相机感知路由，在 LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0 及真实机器人任务上取得高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 World-Action Models 虽结合视觉动态与动作预测，但其多视角接口通常仅拼接图像或 token，导致同步相机间的几何关系隐式化，难以同时利用全局场景上下文与交互所需的局部几何信息。

Method: MVG-WAM 将多视角观测组织为同一物理世界的关系投影；联合推断由极线约束的全局状态与按视角索引的几何状态；通过相机感知路由为每个视频区域提供对应几何上下文与共享全局状态，从而显式结构化动作预测表示；并利用多时间跨度未来深度监督将几何表示锚定到度量尺度，且动作 rollout 时无需深度解码。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上平均成功率达 99.1%，在 RoboTwin 2.0 上达 92.07%；在 Cobot Magic 真实实验中，覆盖三类操作任务、150 次试验，成功率为 91.3%。

Conclusion: MVG-WAM 通过显式多视角几何建模与相机感知表示，提升了机器人操作中的动作预测性能，并在仿真与真实任务中展现出竞争性表现。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) couple visual dynamics with action prediction, bringing the rich priors of pretrained video models to robotic manipulation. However, their multi-view interfaces typically tile images or concatenate tokens, leaving the geometric relationships among synchronized cameras implicit. This makes it harder to connect global scene context with the local geometry required for interaction. We introduce the Multi-View Geometry-Aware World-Action Model (MVG-WAM), which organizes these observations as related projections of one physical world rather than separate images on a canvas. Our model combines an epipolar-constrained global state with view-indexed geometric states jointly inferred from synchronized observations. Camera-aware routing supplies each video region with its corresponding geometric context and the shared global state, explicitly structuring the representation used for action prediction. We further ground the geometry-aware representation in metric scale through multi-horizon future-depth supervision, without requiring depth decoding during action rollout. MVG-WAM achieves average success rates of 99.1% on LIBERO and 92.07% on RoboTwin 2.0, demonstrating competitive performance across both benchmarks. Real-world experiments on Cobot Magic further demonstrate a 91.3% success rate across 150 trials spanning three manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [51] [Geometry-Preserving Human-to-Robot Upper-Body Motion Retargeting from Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37776)
*Xiaoyu Yang,Sen Han,Da Li,Nan Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种几何保持的运动重定向框架，将单目RGB视频中的人体与手部统一重建，并映射到双臂灵巧机器人上，实现协调的上肢动作复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目RGB视频是获取机器人上肢演示的便捷来源，但人体到机器人的视频驱动迁移面临困难：身体与手部运动在不同空间尺度上恢复、人体与机器人运动学差异显著，且精细远端手部动作难以跨本体保持。

Method: 提出几何保持的运动重定向框架，集成统一身体—手部重建与形态无关的几何迁移。利用逐帧身体估计、视频级观测和详细手部证据共同约束单一可微MHR状态，并在参数空间修复瞬态手部伪影。重建运动由手臂段方向、肘部构型、相对手掌朝向和双侧腕部关系表示，通过多阶段逆运动学和机器人特定手部适配在目标机器人上实现。系统内，Across-VAM负责视频生成，Across-WAM执行人—机器人运动映射。

Result: 在16段单目视频（共1769帧源帧，包括10段手语和6段抓取接近序列）上评估。统一重建将平均手部重投影误差从SAM 3D Body的22.36像素降至7.21像素。全部16条重定向轨迹均完成运动学仿真回放，代表性手语和抓取接近动作还在物理机器人上演示。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该统一流程能够将单目人体视频转化为双臂灵巧机器人上的协调上肢运动。

Abstract: Monocular RGB video provides an accessible source of human demonstrations for upper-body robot motion, yet video-driven human-to-robot transfer remains challenging because body and hand motion are recovered at different spatial scales, human and robot kinematics differ substantially, and fine distal motion is difficult to preserve across embodiments. We present a geometry-preserving motion-retargeting framework that integrates unified body--hand reconstruction with morphology-independent geometric transfer. Frame-wise body estimates, video-level observations, and detailed hand evidence jointly constrain a single differentiable Momentum Human Rig (MHR) state, while transient hand artifacts are repaired in parameter space. The reconstructed motion is represented by arm-segment directions, elbow configuration, relative palm orientation, and bilateral wrist relations, and is realized on the target robot through multi-stage inverse kinematics and robot-specific hand adaptation. Within the broader system, Across-VAM provides video generation, whereas Across-WAM performs human-to-robot motion mapping. The method is evaluated on 16 monocular videos comprising 1,769 source frames, including 10 signing and six reach-to-grasp sequences. Unified reconstruction reduces mean hand reprojection error from 22.36 to 7.21 pixels relative to SAM 3D Body. All 16 retargeted trajectories completed kinematic simulation playback, and representative signing and reach-to-grasp motions were further demonstrated on a physical robot. The results demonstrate a unified pipeline from monocular human video to coordinated upper-body motion on a dual-arm dexterous robot.

</details>


### [52] [Urgent Actions Go First: Urgency-Aware Denoising for Real-Time VLA Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37772)
*Zibo Wang,Haochen Han,Pengzhen Ren,Mingtong Dai,Fangming Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出紧急感知去噪（UAD）推理框架，利用动作块联合生成但顺序执行的紧迫性异质性，按需分配去噪计算，实现最高1.89倍动作可用延迟加速且保持成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散与流匹配VLA策略通过迭代去噪生成动作块，推理延迟高，严重限制实时机器人控制。现有加速方法将动作块视为整体计算单元，忽略了滚动时域控制中动作联合生成但顺序执行所导致的执行紧迫性异质性。

Method: UAD在推理时根据每个动作的物理需求时间分配去噪计算：紧急动作少步释放，尾部动作后台继续细化并与物理执行重叠。通过轨迹协调（Trajectory Reconciliation）重建统一内部状态演化以恢复联合去噪一致性且不增加模型评估；通过幽灵动作校正（Ghost Action Correction）利用未执行的幽灵延续动态补偿早期释放误差。

Result: 在多种VLA架构、仿真基准和真实操作任务中，UAD平均动作可用延迟最高加速1.89倍，成功率与最优去噪预算的原始推理相当，成功-延迟权衡优于最先进的VLA加速基线。

Conclusion: UAD有效利用动作执行紧迫性异质性，为VLA策略提供更优的成功-延迟权衡，提升实时机器人控制可行性。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-matching Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies generate action chunks through iterative denoising, incurring substantial inference latency that severely limits real-time robotic control. Existing acceleration methods treat an action chunk as a monolithic computational unit, ignoring a crucial physical reality of receding-horizon control: actions are generated jointly but consumed sequentially, resulting in inherently heterogeneous execution urgencies. We exploit this asymmetry to introduce Urgency-Aware Denoising (UAD), a novel inference-time framework that allocates denoising computation according to when each action is physically needed. UAD releases time-critical urgent actions after fewer denoising steps while overlapping the continued background refinement of tail actions with physical execution. However, heterogeneous denoising introduces two key challenges: early-release errors in urgent actions and trajectory inconsistency in tail actions. UAD elegantly resolves both through two core mechanisms: Trajectory Reconciliation, which reconstructs unified internal state evolution to restore joint denoising coherence without additional model evaluations, and Ghost Action Correction, which leverages non-executed ghost continuations to dynamically compensate for early-release errors across remaining executable actions. Extensive evaluations across multiple VLA architectures, simulation benchmarks, and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that UAD achieves up to a 1.89x speedup in average action availability latency while maintaining comparable success rates to vanilla inference with optimal denoising budget, offering a more favorable success-latency trade-off than state-of-the-art VLA acceleration baselines.

</details>


### [53] [Faster and Better? Benchmark Bugs and Design Limitations Distort the Evaluation of Vision-Language-Action Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37771)
*Qiwei Chen,Kaijun Zhou,Nuohui Shi,Zhiyang Li,Yuxuan Feng,Jinyu Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作发现，在模拟操作基准上，一些免训练VLA加速方法成功率异常高于基线，原因包括基准bug和设计局限；审计7个基准后识别22个bug和4个设计局限，修复可逆转排名并消除异常增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅看成功率无法判断VLA加速方法的增益来自更好的任务执行，还是评测缺陷；因此需要审计基准中的实现bug与设计局限，以支持可信评估。

Method: 从出现异常增益的任务出发，通过绘制物体轨迹并与检查器接受区域对比来定位根因，将bug分类为任务一致性、初始化和可复现性；并将审计扩展到RoboTwin、LIBERO-Plus、VLABench等7个基准，识别22个bug和4个设计局限，同时修正宽松成功检查器、不真实物体质量，并加入偏好平滑动作的运动感知分数。

Result: 修复bug可逆转方法排名，使基线在某任务上从最后升至第一；修正设计局限也能消除异常增益，在另一任务上使基线从落后加速方法21个百分点变为领先5个百分点。

Conclusion: 加速方法所表现出的增益可能只是基准伪影，而非更好的任务执行；作者发布bug修复和修订后的基准设置，以支持对VLA加速的可信评估。

Abstract: Simulated manipulation benchmarks are the standard tool for evaluating vision-language-action (VLA) policies and the acceleration methods that reduce their inference latency for on-robot deployment. On these benchmarks, we observe that some training-free acceleration methods, which approximate the baseline policy's computation, achieve higher measured success rates than the baseline itself. Success rates alone cannot establish whether such gains come from better task execution or from evaluation flaws. We therefore investigate two kinds of benchmark flaws behind these gains: bugs, where the implementation does not match the intended task or evaluation protocol, and design limitations, where success criteria and simulation settings do not fully capture how acceleration affects task execution. Starting from tasks with anomalous gains, we localize root causes by plotting object trajectories against checker acceptance regions, and classify the resulting bugs into task consistency, initialization, and reproducibility. Extending this audit to seven benchmarks, including RoboTwin, LIBERO-Plus, and VLABench, we identify 22 bugs of these types and 4 design limitations. For the latter, we revise permissive success checkers, correct unrealistic object masses, and add a motion-aware score that favors smoother actions. Experiments show that bug fixes can reverse method rankings, moving the baseline from last to first on one task. Addressing design limitations can likewise remove anomalous gains: on another task, the baseline moves from 21 percentage points behind an accelerated method to 5 points ahead. Gains attributed to acceleration can therefore be artifacts of the benchmark rather than better task execution. We release our bug fixes and revised benchmark settings to support trustworthy evaluation of VLA acceleration.

</details>


### [54] [Recompositional Robotics: Cross-Domain, Open-set, and Lifelong Modularity Beyond Morphology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37734)
*Steven Swanbeck,Jonathan Salfity,Corrie Van Sice,Robert Blake Anderson,Mitch Pryor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出“重组机器人学”（recompositional robotics）这一新问题：不同于仅改变结构的模块化机器人，目标是在硬件、软件、计算、行为等异构模块间进行跨域重配置，以最大化集成跨度并最小化集成惯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模块化机器人多聚焦形态在线变化，但仅在一个域（结构）降低重配置阻力；为提升机器人根本能力，需跨运动动力学、感知、计算与高层协调行为等多域重配置，因此需要统一抽象与整体推理框架。

Method: 作者定义集成跨度（跨域可重配置性）与集成惯性（抗重配置性），提出重组机器人学问题：将硬件、软件、计算、行为等异构模块按所需/提供的接口抽象，从而整体推理与自适应重组；并基于两个已部署系统和在研工作进行问题界定。

Result: 论文指出当前模块化机器人在结构域已降低集成惯性，但在贡献集成跨度的其他域仍较高；能最大化跨度并最小化惯性的系统在实际中通过重配置提供最大效用。

Conclusion: 重组机器人学被形式化并扎根于实际系统与研究，作者提出关于其未来的开放问题，呼吁跨域模块化与接口抽象方向的研究。

Abstract: Research in modular robotics has produced capable approaches allowing a robot's morphology to change online, with recent efforts also developing approaches to decide which morphology to assume and automatically propagate that decision into the robot's motion planning and control. These approaches are powerful and increase adaptability in the field. However, an alternative objective is not to build robots whose structures can change, but robots whose fundamental capabilities can change, where capability is a joint function across several domains, including kino-dynamics, perception, compute, and high-level coordinating behaviors. A robot designed to be reconfigured across these domains has a greater capacity to alter its capability than one that can be reconfigured in a single domain. We refer to this cross-domain reconfigurability as integration span, and recognize a complementary measure of the resistance to reconfiguration, which we refer to as integration inertia. Current modular robots have reduced integration inertia in the structural domain while it remains high in the other domains that contribute to integration span. We assert that the systems that can provide the most utility through reconfiguration in practice are those maximizing span and minimizing inertia and call this general problem recompositional robotics: adaptation over a heterogeneous set of modules including hardware, software, compute, and behavior that abstracts each component by the interfaces it requires and provides such that they can be reasoned over holistically. We define the problem, ground it in two deployed systems and active research efforts, and pose open questions about the future of recompositional robotics.

</details>


### [55] [CogWAM: Aligning Semantic Cognition with World Action Modeling via Event-Driven Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37721)
*Sen Wang,Liu Liu,Xinjiang Wang,Zequn Chen,Haoyi Jiang,Taojun Ding,Tingyang Xiao,Zhizhong Su,Jie Wang,Sanping Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CogWAM 提出一种认知引导的世界-动作模型，通过持久化语义状态在任务推理与世界-动作学习之间建立显式语义接口，使局部预测与动作持续对齐任务进度，并在多个基准和真实双臂操作中取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略虽融合语义推理与未来世界预测，但二者简单结合并不能保证局部预测和动作始终与任务进度保持一致。

Method: CogWAM 引入持久化 Semantic State，存储已完成任务事件和当前子任务，仅在观测表明语义转换时更新，从而让任务级上下文跨多个动作块持续存在；采用进度条件化的 WORLD 与 ACTION 查询，选择性提取任务相关信息用于未来世界预测和动作生成；训练时 Semantic State 为两分支提供共享任务进度上下文，推理时移除未来预测分支，直接从观测和维护状态生成动作；此外提出语义训练策略以改进转换学习和闭环条件化。

Result: 无需额外机器人动作预训练，CogWAM 在 RoboDojo 上取得 15.56/11.70% Score/SR，在 BiCoord 上达到最先进性能；真实世界实验展示闭环双臂操作，且相比逐步更新减少了 16.4 次 Semantic State 重新生成。

Conclusion: CogWAM 通过显式语义状态桥接任务推理与物理预测控制，在保持任务进度上下文的同时提升动作生成性能与效率，并展现出良好的真实世界操作能力。

Abstract: Robot policies increasingly incorporate semantic reasoning and future-world prediction, yet combining these capabilities does not guarantee that local predictions and actions remain aligned with task progress. We introduce CogWAM, a cognition-guided world-action model that establishes an explicit semantic interface between task reasoning and world-action learning through a persistent Semantic State, which stores completed task events and the active subtask. CogWAM updates this state only when observations indicate semantic transitions, allowing task-level context to persist across multiple action chunks. To bridge semantic context with physical prediction and control, CogWAM employs progress-conditioned WORLD and ACTION queries that selectively extract task-relevant information for future-world prediction and action generation. During training, the Semantic State provides shared task-progress context for both branches, while inference removes the future-prediction branch and directly generates actions from observations and the maintained state. We further introduce semantic training strategies to improve transition learning and closed-loop conditioning. Without additional robot-action pretraining, CogWAM achieves 15.56 / 11.70 % Score/SR on RoboDojo and state-of-the-art performance on BiCoord, while real-world experiments demonstrate closed-loop dual-arm manipulation with 16.4 fewer Semantic State regenerations than step-wise updating.

</details>


### [56] [ProAct-VLM: Pre-Failure Vision-Language Task Replanning with Continuous Perception Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37681)
*Ahmed Nader Ahmed,Omar Moured,Mughni Irfan Mohammed Abdul,Muhayy Ud Din,Irfan Hussain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ProAct-VLM，一个将VLM嵌入实时感知-反馈闭环的自适应任务规划框架，能在环境变化导致失败前主动重规划，提升动态长时程操作任务的成功率与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人任务易受意外环境变化影响，传统规则式决策脆弱且泛化差。VLM具备世界知识与视觉-文本推理能力，但需嵌入能处理不确定性与环境变化的框架。现有方法多为反应式，仅在执行失败或任务后检查时重规划，或在动作前做离散条件检查，无法捕捉执行过程中发生的变化。

Method: ProAct-VLM将VLM集成到实时感知-反馈闭环中，持续监控环境，一旦检测到相关变化便立即重规划，实现物理接地且自适应的任务规划，从而在失败发生前完成动作调整。

Result: 在多种基线和不同VLM骨干网络上的评估表明，该框架在动态、长时程操作任务中同时提升了成功率和效率。

Conclusion: ProAct-VLM通过实时感知-反馈与主动重规划有效应对动态环境变化，验证了将VLM嵌入闭环框架用于长时程机器人任务的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic tasks are vulnerable to unexpected environmental changes that can render planned actions ineffective or unsafe. To address this, robots must detect such changes as they occur, interpret their impact, and adjust their actions accordingly. Traditional rule-based decision-making pipelines are brittle in open-world conditions, as they are hand-tuned for specific scenarios and lack generalization. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) offer a promising alternative as they combine broad world knowledge with unified visual--text reasoning, enabling them to generalize across diverse scenarios and generate accurate, grounded task plans. However, for effective deployment in dynamic real-world settings, VLMs must be embedded into frameworks capable of handling uncertainty and environmental changes. Existing frameworks broadly address this reactively, triggering replanning only after execution failures or post-task checks, risking failed actions. Some methods verify conditions before actions, but these discrete checks miss changes occurring during execution. To address this, we present ProAct-VLM, an adaptive, physically grounded task planning framework that integrates VLMs within a real-time perception--feedback loop. ProAct-VLM continuously monitors the environment and re-plans as soon as relevant changes are detected, enabling adaptation before failure occurs. Evaluations against multiple baselines and across different VLM backbones show that our framework improves both success rates and efficiency in dynamic, long-horizon manipulation tasks. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [57] [Learning Expressive and Compositional Motion Representation via Spectral Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37677)
*Feiyang Wu,Chenxiao Gao,Chen Yang,Ye Zhao,Bo Dai,Anqi Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 spectral skills，一种用于人形机器人分层控制的潜在命令接口，通过预测式表征学习获得；在29自由度人形上显著降低跟踪误差，并支持技能链接与组合，在Unitree G1硬件上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型常采用分层架构，但规划器与控制器之间的命令接口至关重要：需兼顾精确执行、易预测性，并支持从已有技能组合新行为。现有接口难以同时满足这些要求。

Method: 提出 spectral skills，紧凑编码短运动片段；采用预测式表征学习，通过预测后续运动而非重构编码器输入来学习该潜在接口。控制器以 spectral skills 为条件进行控制。

Result: 在29自由度人形上，全局跟踪误差相对当前最优方法降低62%；同一冻结控制器无需单独过渡策略即可链接独立编码的技能；通过向兼容基础技能添加正交方向组合出新行为，产生训练数据中未见的组合；在Unitree G1硬件上展示跟踪、链接、组合以及语言条件规划器控制。

Conclusion: spectral skills 作为规划器-控制器接口，可提升执行精度、支持技能链接与组合，是实现通用人形机器人控制的有效接口表征，并具备真实硬件可行性。

Abstract: Robotic foundation models offer a promising path toward general-purpose humanoid robot control, often through hierarchical architectures. However, their effectiveness depends on the command interface between the planner and the controller, which must support accurate execution while remaining easy to predict, and ideally allow new behaviors to be composed from prior ones. In this work, we introduce spectral skills, a latent representation of this interface that meets these requirements through predictive representation learning. By design, spectral skills compactly encode short motion segments and are learned by predicting subsequent motion rather than reconstructing the encoder input. On a 29-DoF humanoid, a controller conditioned on spectral skills reduces global tracking error by 62\% relative to the state of the art. The same frozen controller chains independently encoded skills without a separate transition policy. It also composes new behaviors by adding orthogonal directions to any compatible base skill, producing combinations unseen in the training data. We demonstrate tracking, chaining, and composition, as well as control through a language-conditioned planner, on Unitree G1 hardware. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [58] [Semantic Map Sharing and Capability-Aware Coverage Planning for AI-Native 6G Robotic Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37666)
*Abdulqader Dhafer,Qi Wang,Zhou Daniel Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向异构空地机器人搜救的边端语义感知覆盖规划框架，将航空地形感知转化为紧凑语义栅格地图，并进行机器人可达性约束的区域分解与能力感知覆盖路径规划；通过仅共享语义修正量降低通信负载，并在基准场景中优于LS-MCPP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统覆盖方法通常未将感知地形转化为平台特定的可达性，且持续图像交换导致高通信成本；搜救中异构空地机器人团队需要兼顾能力约束、覆盖效率与通信效率。

Method: 以边缘为中心、语义感知的覆盖规划：航空观测生成紧凑语义栅格地图，推理各机器人可通行性，进行可达性约束区域分解，并将区域分配给满足能力剖面的机器人；形成感知-共享-规划闭环，将语义修正反馈至可通行性推理与重规划，仅传输语义修正而非完整地图，契合AI原生6G中AI使能的目标导向通信。

Result: 高更新场景下，传输语义修正相比周期性全地图共享将应用负载降低约82倍；匹配基准场景中，所提方法达到91.5%覆盖率且无能力不可行分配，而LS-MCPP为78.8%覆盖率与21.5%能力不可行分配率。

Conclusion: 语义修正可在不重复传输完整地图的情况下更新共享规划状态，实现通信高效、能力可行的异构机器人覆盖规划，验证了AI原生6G目标导向通信在搜救应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Search and Rescue (SAR) operations increasingly deploy heterogeneous teams of aerial and ground robots. However, conventional coverage methods typically do not translate perceived terrain into platform-specific reachability, while continuous image exchange imposes a high communication cost. We propose an edge-centric, semantic-aware coverage planning framework that integrates aerial terrain perception, robot-specific traversability reasoning, and payload-efficient semantic state sharing. Aerial observations are converted into compact semantic grid maps, enabling reachability-constrained area decomposition and capability-aware coverage paths that assign only regions admitted by each robot's capability profile. The resulting perception-sharing-planning loop feeds semantic corrections into traversability reasoning and replanning, forming an application-level mechanism motivated by AI-enabled goal-oriented communication envisioned for AI-native 6G networks. For the high-update case, transmitting semantic corrections reduces the application payload by a factor of approximately $82$ relative to periodic full-map sharing. Across matched benchmark scenarios, the proposed method achieved $91.5\%$ coverage with no capability-infeasible allocations, compared with $78.8\%$ coverage and a $21.5\%$ capability-infeasible allocation rate for LS-MCPP. Semantic corrections update the shared planning state without requiring repeated transmission of the complete map.

</details>


### [59] [Cascaded consensus splitting for multi-branch contingency games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37612)
*Bastien Lechardoy,Pau de las Heras Molins,Thibault Lahire,Laurent Pautet,David Filliat,David Fridovich-Keil,Georgios Bakirtzis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种多分支 contingency 博弈架构，使不同意图不确定性可在不同分支时刻逐步揭示，并给出基于 ADMM 的场景并行求解器；在三智能体紧耦合交互中优于单分支方法且降低滚动时域平均求解时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 contingency 博弈仅采用单一分支时刻，无法刻画不同智能体意图在不同时间揭示的异步交互，且随智能体与意图数量增长，所有场景相关最优响应需联合求解，计算代价快速上升。

Method: 引入多分支 contingency 架构，将意图不确定性来源按不同分支时刻逐步解析，使规划轨迹适应意图的异步揭示；并开发基于 ADMM 的求解器，利用场景级并行性。

Result: 在紧耦合三智能体交互实验中，所提架构优于传统单分支方案，并获得更低的滚动时域平均求解时间。

Conclusion: 多分支异步 contingency 规划能更真实地建模意图揭示时序，并通过 ADMM 并行求解提升性能与计算效率。

Abstract: Contingency games enable agents to anticipate and plan for other agents' hypothetical intents by constructing trajectories with a shared prefix and intent-dependent branches. While contingency games capture intent uncertainty, existing formulations rely on a single branching time, oversimplifying interactions in which different agents' intentions are revealed at different times. Moreover, the computational cost of such problems grows rapidly with the number of agents and intents, as all scenario-dependent best responses must be solved jointly. We introduce a multi-branch contingency architecture in which sources of intent uncertainty can be resolved progressively at different branching times, allowing the planned trajectories to adapt to the asynchronous revelation of intents. We also develop an ADMM-based solver that exploits scenario-level parallelism. Experiments on tightly coupled three-agent interactions support that the proposed architecture outperforms the conventional single-branch while achieving a lower mean receding-horizon solve time.

</details>


### [60] [When to Adapt: Multi-Signal Domain Shift Detection for Efficient Training-Free Adaptation in Open-Vocabulary Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37602)
*Michele Antonazzi,Alejandra C. Hernandez,José Araujo,Olov Andersson,Patric Jensfelt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于开放词汇分割的多信号域漂移检测方法，以支持免训练持续测试时自适应，仅在需要时触发自适应，从而在长期机器人部署中保持精度并显著减少自适应开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人在长期任务中会因环境变化遭遇域漂移；视觉基础模型虽提升了开放词汇语义分割，但若不适配当前环境，性能会显著下降。现有免训练域自适应方法通常逐帧适配，在资源受限的机器人硬件上不切实际。

Method: 提出面向免训练持续测试时自适应（TF-CTTA）的多信号域漂移检测方法。该方法利用连续帧的时间一致性，监测并融合视觉变化、适配器不匹配和语义漂移三类互补信号，以判断是否触发自适应。

Result: 在包含室内与室外环境的基准以及真实机器人数据上验证，方法在保持分割精度的同时大幅减少了自适应次数。

Conclusion: 该工作使免训练自适应更实用可行，可支持长期、真实世界场景中的机器人部署。

Abstract: Robust and reliable perception is essential for autonomous robots operating in real-world environments, particularly in long-term missions where environmental conditions may change significantly over time. Although recent advances in Visual Foundation Models (VFMs) have improved open-vocabulary semantic segmentation, these models can still suffer from domain shift, which can significantly degrade performance if they are not adapted to the current environment. Training-free domain adaptation is a relevant paradigm for adaptation, consisting of adjusting the model online using lightweight adapters. Recent approaches apply this on a per-frame basis, which is impractical for deployments on resource-constrained robotic hardware. To tackle this, we propose a multi-signal domain shift detection method for training-free continual test-time adaptation (TF-CTTA) in open-vocabulary segmentation. Our method leverages temporal coherence across consecutive frames by monitoring and combining complementary aspects of domain shift (visual change, adapter mismatch, and semantic drift) to trigger adaptation only when needed. We validate our approach on a benchmark including indoor and outdoor environments and using real robotic data. We demonstrate that our approach maintains segmentation accuracy while substantially reducing adaptations, making training-free adaptation practical and feasible for long-term, real-world robotic deployments.

</details>


### [61] [BlenDAgger: Blended Shared Control for Interactive Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37599)
*Cailyn Smith,Geoffrey Sun,Henny Admoni,Zackory Erickson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Blended DAgger（BlenDAgger），在人类干预时将策略动作与示教者动作通过共享控制融合，以更高效地收集模仿学习数据并提升机器人操作策略的自主性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略常依赖人类纠正数据训练，但遥操作提供纠正负担重，且人类示教者并非总是最优；因此需要一种更高效且能提升自主性能的干预数据收集方法。

Method: BlenDAgger 在干预阶段使用共享控制，将策略输出与人类示教者动作进行混合，而非传统 HG-DAgger 那样完全由人类遥操作纠正；在 5 个操作任务（2 个真实世界、3 个仿真）中验证，并分析过渡平滑性与轨迹相似性，另开展 n=14 的两项真实任务用户研究。

Result: 与典型人类门控纠正方法 HG-DAgger 相比，BlenDAgger 在两项真实任务上自主性能提升 30 个百分点以上；策略控制与人类干预之间的过渡平滑度提高 57%，与训练数据的轨迹相似度提高 14%；用户研究中数据收集更快（BF=13.32），主观感知无显著差异。

Conclusion: 混合共享控制能比完全遥操作干预微调机器人策略的典型方法带来更高自主性能，表明 BlenDAgger 是更有效的干预数据收集与策略微调方案。

Abstract: Robot policies are frequently trained from human corrections, yet teleoperating a robot to provide corrections is burdensome, and human demonstrators are not always optimal. We propose Blended DAgger (BlenDAgger), an approach for collecting data to train imitation learning policies by using shared control to blend the policy's and demonstrator's actions during interventions. By blending human and policy actions, we aim to improve the autonomous performance of manipulation policies. We validate our approach across five manipulation tasks, two in the real world and three in simulation. Our approach achieves higher autonomous performance by 30 or more percentage points on two real-world tasks compared to a typical human-gated correction approach (HG-DAgger). We also investigate the advantages of BlenDAgger that allow for higher autonomous performance, finding that BlenDAgger results in 57% smoother transitions between policy control and human interventions, and 14% higher trajectory similarity to the training data. In a user study (n=14) on two real-world tasks, we find that BlenDAgger results in faster data collection (BF=13.32), and we do not find a difference in subjective perceptions. These results show that blended shared control leads to higher autonomous performance compared to typical methods for fine-tuning robot policies from fully teleoperated interventions.

</details>


### [62] [Credit-Guided Policy Improvement for Test-time Adaptive Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37591)
*Yang Li,Sijia Zhang,Yihan Li,Aming WU,Zihao Zhang,Ziju Han,Yahong Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Credit-Guided Policy Improvement (CGPI)，利用动作引起的观测转移恢复带符号、参考相对的决策信用，在冻结预训练策略下进行可验证的轻量测试时适应，从而提升视觉语言导航在分布偏移下的鲁棒性并支持零样本仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTA-VLN方法依赖预测不确定性、轨迹级反馈或累积适应经验，但这些信号不能直接反映已执行动作是否有助于遵循指令向目标推进；且即使存在看似合理的纠正信号，也不能保证策略更新可靠。核心挑战在于识别支持目标导向改进的交互，并判断更新是否值得保留。

Method: 观察到每个执行动作会产生即时观测转移，可提供其局部后果的证据。基于此提出CGPI：从动作引起的观测转移中恢复带符号、相对于参考的决策信用，无需外部结果反馈。冻结预训练导航策略，用该信用提出轻量适应更新，并依据先前信用支持的交互进行验证；仅当更新得到支持时保留，否则回滚。

Result: 在评估的VLN基准和导航骨干网络上取得一致性能提升；定性机器人试验进一步表明零样本仿真到现实迁移的可行性。

Conclusion: CGPI为TTA-VLN提供了一种无需外部反馈、以动作局部后果为依据且可验证的策略改进机制，能有效缓解分布偏移导致的偏离航向决策，并具备实际机器人迁移潜力。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation for vision-language navigation (TTA-VLN) enables pretrained policies to adapt online to unseen environments using only test-time observations and interaction history. However, distribution shifts can distort local action preferences and lead to off-course decisions. Existing methods rely on predictive uncertainty, trajectory-level feedback, or accumulated adaptation experience to correct such deviations. These signals, however, do not directly reveal whether an executed action supports instruction-guided progress toward the goal. Moreover, a plausible corrective signal does not guarantee a reliable policy update. The key challenge is thus twofold: identifying interactions that support goal-directed improvement and determining whether the resulting updates are worth retaining. We observe that each executed action induces an immediate observation transition, providing evidence of its local consequences. Based on this insight, we propose Credit-Guided Policy Improvement (CGPI), which recovers signed, reference-relative decision credit from action-induced observation transitions without external outcome feedback. With the pretrained navigation policy frozen, CGPI uses this credit to propose lightweight adaptation updates and verifies them against prior credit-supported interactions. Updates are retained only when supported and rolled back otherwise. CGPI achieves consistent gains across the evaluated VLN benchmarks and navigation backbones, while qualitative robot trials further illustrate the feasibility of zero-shot sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [63] [RoboHarn-Evo: Evolving Hierarchical Physical Knowledge for Self-Improving Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37583)
*Shifeng Bao,Fanding Huang,Yihan Lin,Youhe Feng,Guanlin Li,Chen Zhao,Yang Li,Jiawei He,Cheng Chi,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboHarn-Evo，一种双环路 harness，通过物理交互进化分层物理知识 HPK，在不更新基座模型的情况下提升长时序机器人操作的任务推理与执行成功率，并实现知识纠错与零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型可协调长时序机器人操作，但任务推理成功仍取决于局部物理交互是否产生预期效果；研究旨在不更新基座模型的前提下，利用重复交互提升这一能力。

Method: RoboHarn-Evo 采用双环路 harness，从物理经验中进化 HPK。HPK 耦合两层可复用知识：Task Knowledge 判断应执行哪个子任务及其完成条件；Action Knowledge 表示物体相对几何策略及其物理效果。执行时，agent 在对应决策层级检索知识，并在任务目标下将其接地到当前场景；跨回合利用物理反馈修正历史知识、更新适用性，并组织可复用条目以供后续检索。

Result: 在 RMBench 上，HPK 使不同 agent 模型平均成功率最高提升 24.2 个百分点。经过 80 次交互 rollout，held-out 成功率从 48.3% 升至 75.0%（GPT-5.5），从 70.0% 升至 88.3%（GPT-6）。RoboHarn-Evo 修正超过 83% 的历史知识错误，同时保留 95.8% 的有效知识，并从 RMBench 零样本迁移到 RoboDojo，分别提升 35.0 和 25.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: 物理交互可被积累为可复用知识，从而提升后续机器人操作表现；结果证明了在免更新基座模型条件下，通过经验进化分层物理知识来增强长时序操作推理的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language models can coordinate long-horizon robot manipulation, yet successful task reasoning still depends on whether local physical interactions produce the intended effects. We study how repeated interaction can improve this capability without updating the base model. We introduce RoboHarn-Evo, a dual-loop harness that evolves Hierarchical Physical Knowledge (HPK) from physical experience. HPK couples two levels of reusable knowledge: Task Knowledge captures which subtask should be executed and when it is complete, while Action Knowledge captures object-relative geometric strategies and their physical effects. During execution, the agent retrieves knowledge at the corresponding decision level and grounds it in the current scene under the task goal. Across episodes, physical feedback is used to revise historical knowledge, update its applicability, and organize reusable entries for subsequent retrieval. Experiments on RMBench show that HPK improves average success by up to 24.2 percentage points across different agent models. With 80 interaction rollouts, held-out success rises from 48.3% to 75.0% for GPT-5.5 and from 70.0% to 88.3% for GPT-6. RoboHarn-Evo also resolves over 83% of historical knowledge errors while retaining 95.8% of valid knowledge, and transfers zero-shot from RMBench to RoboDojo with gains of 35.0 and 25.0 percentage points. These results demonstrate that physical interaction can be accumulated into reusable knowledge for improving subsequent manipulation.

</details>


### [64] [RoboFin3D: A Sim-to-Real Platform for Robotic Surface Finishing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37560)
*Haowei Wen,Shangtao Li,Vaibhav Sanjay,Philip Huang,Jiaoyang Li,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboFin3D，一个基于 Isaac Sim 与 Newton 物理引擎的 sim-to-real 平台，用于低成本、可重复的机器人打磨与砂磨仿真实验；通过 SDF 与独立表面场建模工件几何和外观变化，并用 WeldGen 扩充仿真资产，校准后还可提升感知模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器人表面精加工中的打磨和砂磨实验通常依赖物理试验，成本高且消耗工件，导致实验难以可重复；因此需要一个廉价、可重复的物理仿真平台来支持机器人表面精加工研究。

Method: 构建 RoboFin3D，基于 Isaac Sim 和 Newton 物理引擎提供物理级打磨与砂磨仿真。该方法使用 signed distance field (SDF) 建模工件动态变化的几何形状，支持接触计算、实时更新与渲染，且无需中间网格；使用独立表面场建模砂磨过程中表面外观的渐进变化；引入 WeldGen 焊缝采样模块，在 8,918 个真实工件网格上生成焊缝焊道，以提供多样化的仿真资产；仿真参数基于真实实验结果进行校准。

Result: 评估表明该仿真对真实世界具有保真度；仿真生成数据可用于提升感知模型性能。仅使用仿真数据微调 SAM2，可将未打磨区域分割的 IoU 从 77.15% 提升至 84.47%；合成数据与真实数据联合训练时达到 97.41%。

Conclusion: RoboFin3D 能为机器人打磨与砂磨提供可重复、低成本的仿真实验环境，并有效支持 sim-to-real 迁移及下游感知任务性能提升。

Abstract: Grinding and sanding are fundamental processes in industrial robotic surface finishing. However, physical trials are expensive and consume workpieces, making reproducible experiments difficult. We present RoboFin3D, a sim-to-real platform built on Isaac Sim and the Newton physics engine, that provides physics-based grinding and sanding simulation for cheap and repeatable robotic surface finishing experiments. RoboFin3D utilizes a signed distance field (SDF) to model the changing geometry of the workpiece, enabling contact computation, live updates and rendering without an intermediate mesh. It additionally uses a separate surface field to model progressive surface appearance change during sanding. We also introduce WeldGen, a weld sampling module, to generate weld beads on 8,918 real-world workpiece meshes for providing diverse simulation assets. The simulation parameters are calibrated on real experimental results and our evaluation demonstrates our simulation's fidelity against the real world. We also demonstrate that simulation-generated data can be used to improve the performance of perception models. Simulation-only fine-tuning of SAM2 improves IoU for segmentation of unsanded regions from 77.15% to 84.47%, while combined synthetic and real training reaches 97.41%.

</details>


### [65] [Risk-Aware Semantic Grounding for Trustworthy LLM-Based Robot Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37554)
*Łukasz Sobczak,Nur Keleşoğlu,Sławomir Piotr Nowak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向可信LLM机器人规划的风险感知语义接地框架，将语义接地可靠性建模为多维风险估计问题，并引入TRUST-NAV基准评估歧义、幻觉与语义冲突等风险场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正被广泛用作机器人导航的高层规划器，但当指令存在歧义、环境不支持或语义不一致时，其输出可能不可靠。现有LLM规划器主要优化规划生成，忽视了语义接地的可靠性评估。

Method: 提出风险感知语义接地框架，将语义接地可靠性形式化为多维风险估计问题；在规划前显式建模歧义、幻觉和语义冲突风险，使系统能够决定执行指令、请求澄清或拒绝指令。同时构建TRUST-NAV基准，包含标准导航任务和风险诱导指令场景。

Result: 实验表明，传统LLM规划器在有效导航任务上表现较强，而所提框架在歧义检测和语义冲突拒绝方面有显著提升。

Conclusion: 可信机器人规划不应仅以任务完成度评价，还应评估系统识别“不应执行”情况的能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as high-level planners in robot navigation, but their outputs may become unreliable when instructions are ambiguous, unsupported by the environment, or semantically inconsistent. This paper presents a Risk-Aware Semantic Grounding framework for trustworthy LLM-based robot planning. Unlike existing LLM-based planners that primarily optimize plan generation, we formulate semantic grounding reliability as a multi-dimensional risk estimation problem. The proposed architecture explicitly models grounding uncertainty through ambiguity, hallucination and semantic-conflict risks before planning occurs, enabling the system to decide whether to execute the instruction, request clarification, or reject it. To evaluate the approach, we introduce TRUST-NAV, a benchmark containing both standard navigation tasks and risk-inducing instruction scenarios. Experimental results show that while conventional LLM planners achieve strong performance on valid navigation tasks, the proposed framework substantially improves ambiguity detection and semantic conflict rejection. These findings suggest that trustworthy robot planning should be evaluated not only by task completion, but also by the ability to recognize when execution should not occur.

</details>


### [66] [Wrench-ACT: Enhancing Robot Policies for Contact Rich Behavior Using Direct Wrench Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37552)
*Johannes Hechtl,Yannik Blei,Simon Ball,Reihaneh Mirjalili,Michael Krawez,Seongjin Bien,Philipp Schmitt,Wolfram Burgard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种以 wrench 为唯一动作输出的模仿学习策略，直接由纯力控制器执行，用于接触丰富操作，并通过力反馈遥操作数据提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作需要主动调节交互力，但现有机器人操作学习多将动作表示为目标位置或位姿；即使使用力传感，也常仅作观测，或在预测力时依赖混合力控制器。

Method: 基于 ACT，在双边 wrench 遥操作演示上训练单任务模仿学习策略，直接预测 wrench 作为唯一动作输出并交由纯力控制器执行；通过跨条件消融分析数据采集接口与动作空间的作用。

Result: 在五个接触丰富操作任务上，wrench 策略全部匹配或优于位置基线，增益随任务对刻意力调节的需求程度变化；消融表明双边数据采集接口与 wrench 动作空间各自独立贡献性能。

Conclusion: 力域模仿学习的关键在于数据采集，力反馈遥操作可捕捉操作者的刻意力调节；该工作验证了 wrench 动作空间与纯力控制的可行性，并将发布1000+演示以促进后续研究。

Abstract: While contact-rich manipulation requires deliberate regulation of interaction forces, recent approaches to robot manipulation learning predominantly represent actions as target positions or poses. Even methods that incorporate force sensing either use it solely as an observation or, when predicting forces as part of the output, rely on a hybrid force controller. In this paper, we propose an imitation learning policy that predicts wrenches as its sole action output for direct use by a pure force controller. Our studies suggest that force-domain imitation learning depends critically on data collection, with force-feedback teleoperation improving policy performance by capturing the operator's deliberate force regulation. Using Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) as the base architecture, we train single-task models on bilateral wrench demonstrations and evaluate them on five contact-rich manipulation tasks. The wrench policy matches or outperforms position-based baselines across all tasks, with gains varying according to the degree of deliberate force regulation each task requires. Cross-condition ablations show that the bilateral data collection interface and the wrench action space each contribute independently to performance. To support further research, we will release over 1000 wrench-action demonstrations spanning these tasks on a companion website upon publication.

</details>


### [67] [RawVLA: Embodied Neural Image Signal Processor For Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37530)
*Shuhong Liu,Heng Zhou,Lingfeng Qian,Yuhao Fang,Xianbao Hou,Qianyu Zhou,Lin Gu,Wei Sui,Jianfei Yang,Ziteng Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文系统分析了相机ISP对VLA模型动作预测与操作成功的影响，并提出流式神经ISP RawVLA及RAW域基准RawVLA-Bench，以自适应RAW处理提升具身策略鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型通常直接使用固定相机ISP输出的RGB图像，使成像管线游离于学习与评估之外。作者旨在系统考察这一被忽视的设计选择对具身任务的影响。

Method: 围绕增益、传感器噪声、色度响应、色调响应和位深五个ISP维度进行系统分析；提出RawVLA，一种为冻结VLA策略自适应渲染RAW观测的流式神经ISP，并将容量集中于与具身行为相关的成像因素；同时构建RawVLA-Bench，在干净与恶劣采集条件下将图像处理作为显式评估变量。

Result: 分析表明RAW-to-RGB处理显著影响动作预测和操作成功率，且不同ISP维度影响差异明显。在RawVLA-Bench上，RawVLA在标准条件下保持性能，并在成像退化时显著提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 自适应RAW处理可作为物理相机与具身策略之间的有效接口。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models typically operate on RGB images produced by a fixed camera image signal processor (ISP), leaving the imaging pipeline outside the learning and evaluation loop. We systematically examine the consequences of this overlooked design choice across five fundamental ISP dimensions: gain, sensor noise, chromatic response, tonal response, and bit depth. Our analysis reveals that RAW-to-RGB processing materially shapes both action prediction and manipulation success, with different ISP dimensions exerting substantially different effects. Guided by these findings, we introduce RawVLA, a streaming neural ISP that adaptively renders RAW observations for frozen VLA policies while concentrating its capacity on the imaging factors relevant to embodied behavior. We further present RawVLA-Bench, a RAW-domain manipulation benchmark to expose image processing as an explicit evaluation variable across clean and adverse acquisition conditions. Experiments on RawVLA-Bench show that RawVLA preserves performance under standard conditions while substantially improving robustness under degraded imaging, establishing adaptive RAW processing as an effective interface between physical cameras and embodied policies.

</details>


### [68] [Video2STL: Grounding VLM-Generated Temporal Specifications for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37519)
*Merve Atasever,Keyan Azbijari,Cagan Bakirci,Bo-Ruei Huang,Tolga Izdas,Zahra Shahrooei,Richard Yang,Erdem Biyik,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Video2STL 将仅观测视频转换为参数化信号时序逻辑（STL）规范，并用该形式化表示驱动机器人策略学习，实现可检查、可复用且支持跨本体迁移的视频到机器人学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频策略学习无需动作标注或本体匹配演示，但核心挑战在于决定从视频迁移哪些信息。现有方法常将视觉观测转为标量相似度/价值信号，或让基础模型直接生成奖励代码，导致任务时序结构难以检查、落地和复用。

Method: 提出 Video2STL 框架：利用视觉语言模型提取与本体无关的语义事件轨迹，构建符号时序规范库；模型确定任务结构，数值谓词阈值与时序边界从成功机器人轨迹中 grounding。策略学习分离短/长时标信息：短时规范通过滚动窗口定量鲁棒性提供密集奖励，长时规范用因果监控器为有效时序前缀提供一次性进度奖励。同一表示支持从人类或动物视频到机器人控制的跨本体迁移。

Result: 四个操作任务中，Video2STL 平均 success-once 85.8%、success-at-end 67.0%，优于 native dense PPO 的 81.5%/59.5% 和 Text2Reward 的 65.0%/42.3%。四足运动任务中，基于 Qwen-3.8 与 GPT-5.6 的 Video2STL 策略在 0.3–2.1 m/s 速度范围内达到 100% 成功率，并在高速能效上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: Video2STL 通过 STL 形式化表示将视频中的任务时序结构显式化，结合语义事件与数值 grounding，实现可检查、可复用、可落地的机器人策略学习，在操作与运动任务上优于基线并支持跨本体迁移。

Abstract: Video-based policy learning is particularly promising, as it illustrates target behaviors without requiring action annotations or embodiment-matched demonstrations. A central challenge is deciding what information should be transferred from the video to the robot. Existing approaches commonly convert visual observations into scalar similarity or value signals, or ask foundation models to directly generate reward code. These approaches can make the temporal structure of a task difficult to inspect, ground, and reuse. We present Video2STL, a framework that converts observation-only videos into parametric Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications and uses the resulting formal representation for robot learning.
