A vision-language model extracts an embodiment-independent semantic event trace and constructs a bank of symbolic temporal specifications. The model determines the task structure, while numerical predicate thresholds and temporal bounds are grounded from successful robot trajectories. For policy learning, we separate short- and long-timescale temporal information: short-horizon specifications provide dense rewards through rolling-window quantitative robustness, while a causal monitor over a retained long-horizon specification provides one-time progress rewards for valid temporal prefixes. The same representation supports cross-embodiment transfer from human or animal videos to robot control. Across four manipulation tasks, Video2STL achieves $85.8\%$ average success-once and $67.0\%$ success-at-end, compared with $81.5\%/59.5\%$ for native dense PPO and $65.0\%/42.3\%$ for Text2Reward; in quadruped locomotion, Qwen-3.8 and GPT-5.6-based Video2STL policies achieve $100\%$ success across velocities from $0.3$ to $2.1\,\mathrm{m/s}$ while remaining competitive in high-speed energy efficiency. Project webpage: \href{ this https URL }{video2stl}.

</details>


### [69] [Learning Social Navigation from Internet Videos in the Policy State Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37476)
*Jiaming Wang,Duc Thang Nguyen,Jizhuo Chen,Volodymyr Shcherbyna,Diwen Liu,Zhengcheng Shen,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种高效流程，将普通单目行走视频直接转换为闭环社交导航训练环境：在策略状态空间中以度量可通行性地图表示静态场景，并回放从视频恢复的行人轨迹，无需重建或渲染照片级观测。策略在Arena基准成功率达81.2%，20次真机试验成功19次且无需微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练鲁棒的社交导航策略需要包含多样场景布局、地形和行人运动的模拟器，但人工构建此类环境并指定行人行为成本高昂，因此希望利用易获取的单目行走视频低成本生成训练环境。

Method: 核心观察是局部社交导航主要依赖两类信息：机器人可通行区域和附近行人运动。方法将静态场景表示为度量可通行性地图，并可在反事实机器人运动下进行刚体变换；同时从视频中恢复行人轨迹并随时间直接回放。该抽象允许直接在策略状态空间中定义前向动力学，并高效模拟反事实机器人状态，无需重建或渲染逼真观测。

Result: 在独立的Arena基准上，策略成功率为81.2%，优于最强基线的75.0%；在20次真实机器人试验中成功19次，且无需进行策略微调。

Conclusion: 该流程能够高效地将单目行走视频转化为闭环社交导航训练环境，在策略状态空间内模拟反事实交互，学习到的策略在仿真基准和真实机器人上均表现出较强有效性与可迁移性。

Abstract: Training robust social-navigation policies requires simulators with diverse scene layouts, terrain, and human motion, but constructing such environments and specifying pedestrian behavior is costly. We propose an efficient pipeline that converts ordinary monocular walking videos directly into closed-loop social-navigation training environments in the policy's state space. Our key observation is that local social navigation primarily depends on two types of information: where the robot can traverse and how nearby pedestrians move. We therefore represent the static scene as a metric traversability map, which can be rigidly transformed under counterfactual robot motion, while directly replaying the pedestrian trajectories recovered from the video over time. This abstraction allows us to define the forward dynamics directly in the policy's state space and efficiently simulate counterfactual robot states without reconstructing or rendering photorealistic observations. The resulting policy achieves 81.2% success in the independent Arena benchmark, compared with 75.0% for the strongest baseline, and succeeds in 19/20 real-robot trials without policy fine-tuning.

</details>


### [70] [Waggle-Dance-Inspired Multi-UAV Recruitment with RGB and Synthetic Event Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37464)
*Zhang Nengbo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文在MuJoCo中比较RGB与合成事件视觉，用于空中机器人模仿蜜蜂摇摆舞的“运动即消息”通信：一名执行者广播6位任务提议，1–3名观察者解码并选择任务；两种接收器在名义三观察者场景中均完成12次完整消息接收、6个工作单元，并在任务耗尽后重分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜜蜂摇摆舞启发的通信机制要求将另一智能体的运动转化为完整消息，并实现本地任务选择与执行。空中机器人需验证该链路在RGB与事件视觉两种接收方案下的功能可行性。

Method: 构建统一MuJoCo系统：执行者广播6位任务提议；观察者独立解码两条提议后选择任务。RGB接收器用稀疏光流提取轨迹；事件接收器仅用局部事件支持与数据包时序。两者共享运动字母表和显式空定界符。针对事件静默不能证明目标可见，事件方案加入物理获取运动与连续性限定空确认。另进行配对功能案例与事件流丢包案例，并回放42,060,633个合成事件。

Result: 名义三观察者下，RGB与事件版本均产生12次完整消息接收、6个完成工作单元，并在耗尽任务撤销后重分配。6个配对功能案例消息数与工作数一致；额外事件流丢包案例保持受影响观察者不活动。回放全部合成事件复现12条事件消息载荷及完成时间戳。名义任务总时间RGB为493.80 s，事件为517.40 s，差异主要来自事件获取调度。

Conclusion: 固定仿真结果证明两种接收器实现的功能可行性，而非传感器性能优势或硬件验证。

Abstract: The honeybee waggle dance motivates a communication mechanism in which one agent's motion guides the subsequent work of other agents. For aerial robots, realizing this mechanism requires a link from observed movement to a complete message, local task selection, and execution. This paper presents a MuJoCo system for comparing RGB and synthetic event vision within that link. One performer broadcasts six-bit task offers to one to three observers, each of which independently decodes both offers before selecting a task. The RGB receiver extracts trajectories using sparse optical flow; the event receiver uses only local event support and packet timing. Both use a common motion alphabet and explicit null delimiters. Because event silence does not establish target visibility, the event version includes a physical acquisition motion and continuity-qualified null confirmation. In the nominal three-observer case, each version produced 12 complete message receptions, six completed work units, and reassignment after withdrawal of a depleted task. Six paired functional cases produced the same message and work counts; an additional event-stream dropout case preserved inactivity of the affected observer. Replaying 42,060,633 recorded synthetic events reproduced all 12 event-message payloads and completion timestamps. Total nominal mission time was 493.80 s for RGB and 517.40 s for events, with the difference dominated by the event acquisition schedule. These fixed simulation results establish functional feasibility across two receiver implementations, rather than a sensor-performance advantage or hardware validation.

</details>


### [71] [Contact-Adaptive Robotic Ultrasound Probe Control for Tissue Exploration and Continuous Task-Relevant Visualization Using Robot-Free Image-Motion Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37456)
*Seong Jeong,Minsung Kim,Dongho Yee,Juahn Oh,Yechan Seo,Jiyul Lee,Jinseok Lee,Seonho Shim,Younghoon Noh,Youngbin Kong,Hyoun-Joong Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免机器人示教的接触自适应机器人超声监测框架：Sonologger记录临床医生操作探头时的超声与相对旋转，参数化DIMC控制器，在动态膀胱体模中实现并维持选定视图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人超声多面向标准化视图、预设扫描协议或复现专家扫描；本文针对不同角色：临床医生主导操作时，机器人助手维持其选定的视图，使组织变化仍可被观察。

Method: Sonologger将六轴惯性传感器夹持于医生操作的探头上，无需机器人、相机或追踪器，同步记录超声图像与探头坐标系相对旋转。方法不模仿轨迹，而是测量医生校正图像偏移时使用的探头轴与方向；先去除测得的图像延迟，再通过静止-移动-静止分割得到相对旋转标签，因惯性漂移排除平移控制。这些测量用于参数化可解释、非学习的Demonstration-Informed Image-Motion Controller（DIMC），并结合声束轴力调节和独立安全监督器（5 N强制回退）。

Result: 在动态膀胱体模上，DIMC在7次盲法、位姿配对试验中均成功获取并维持任务相关视图（7/7），而静态保持仅1/7（McNemar p = 0.031）；在注射器驱动的水扩张过程中也能维持视图，同时安全监督器限制力超调。

Conclusion: 该框架为接触自适应术中监测提供了一种超声上下文的可操作表示；临床验证仍属未来工作。

Abstract: Robotic ultrasound commonly targets standardized views, predefined scanning protocols, or expert-scan reproduction. We target a different role: while a clinician performs the primary procedure, a robotic assistant maintains a clinician-selected view so that changing tissue remains observable. We present contact-adaptive robotic ultrasound monitoring derived from robot-free demonstrations. Sonologger clamps a six-axis inertial sensor to the clinician-operated probe and records synchronized ultrasound and probe-frame relative rotations without a robot, camera, or tracker. Rather than imitating trajectories, the recordings measure which probe axis and sign the clinician uses to correct an image offset. A measured image latency is removed before still-move-still segmentation yields relative-rotation labels; translation is excluded from control because of inertial drift. These measurements parameterize the Demonstration-Informed Image-Motion Controller (DIMC), an interpretable, non-learned controller combined with beam-axis force regulation and an independent safety supervisor with 5 N forced retreat. On a dynamic bladder phantom, DIMC acquired and held a task-relevant view in 7 of 7 blind, pose-paired trials versus 1 of 7 for a static hold (McNemar p = 0.031), and retained the view during syringe-driven hydro-distension while the supervisor bounded the force excursion. The framework offers an operational representation of ultrasound context for contact-adaptive intraoperative monitoring; clinical validation remains future work.

</details>


### [72] [Surgical Master Console Using General-Purpose Robot Arms and a Separable Articulated Distal Interface: Porcine In-Vivo Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37455)
*Minsung Kim,Seonho Shim,Dongho Yee,Younghoon Noh,Juahn Oh,Yechan Seo,Jinseok Lee,Jiyul Lee,Seong Jeong,Hyuk Choi,Youngbin Kong,Hyoun-Joong Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种低成本腹腔镜手术主操作手：以通用机械臂为可编程基座，将手术专用交互集中在可分离远端适配器，双手机器人硬件约5500美元，并验证了仿真与RCM约束FR3平台控制及猪胆囊切除术可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能手术主控台操作直观但昂贵且难以复现；现有商用触觉设备缺乏手术腕关节 articulation 和连续抓握接口，因此需要兼顾低成本、可复现和手术专用交互的腹腔镜主操作手。

Method: 每个6自由度机械臂配备定制2自由度直驱腕、连续抓握传感和基于离合的工作空间管理；远端适配器可分离。表征电流缩放、重力和启动力摩擦，用于姿态相关重力补偿与运动门控辅助，降低持续驱动和热负荷。同一主手状态可驱动Isaac Sim或RCM约束的双FR3平台；主手状态发布至FR3目标更新延迟≤8 ms，尖端跟踪延迟对齐后平均残差0.31–0.66 mm。

Result: 在猪胆囊切除术实验中，将5项常见操作者侧指标及手/尖端速度分布，与120份Versius临床记录、99例胆囊切除术的人体在体遥测数据进行描述性比较；主控台未导致中断，术后7天观察期内未记录到异常临床体征。

Conclusion: 结果表明，可用通用机器人臂构建手术主操作手，并将手术专用交互集中于可分离远端接口，为低成本、可复现的手术遥操作主手提供可行路径。

Abstract: High-performance surgical master consoles offer intuitive articulated manipulation but are expensive and difficult to reproduce, while accessible commercial haptic devices lack built-in interfaces for surgical wrist articulation and continuous grasp. We present a laparoscopic master in which general-purpose robot manipulators provide the programmable base and surgical-specific interaction is concentrated in a separable distal adapter. Each 6-DoF arm carries a custom 2-DoF direct-drive wrist, continuous grasp sensing, and clutch-based workspace management; the complete bimanual hardware costs approximately US$5,500, below common bimanual commercial haptic references. To support sustained hand-guided use, we characterize current scaling, gravity, and break-away friction and use them for posture-dependent gravity compensation and motion-gated assistance, reducing continuous actuation and thermal loading. A common master state drives either Isaac Sim or an RCM-constrained dual-FR3 platform. Master-state publication to FR3 target update adds at most 8 ms, and tip tracking shows 0.31-0.66 mm mean delay-aligned residuals. In a porcine cholecystectomy experiment, five common operator-side metrics and hand/tip-speed distributions were compared descriptively with human in-vivo telemetry from 120 clinical Versius recordings of 99 cholecystectomy procedures; no interruption originated from the master console, and no abnormal clinical signs were recorded during the 7-day postoperative observation period. These results demonstrate a surgical master built from general-purpose robot arms with surgical-specific interaction concentrated in a separable distal interface.

</details>


### [73] [Anisotropic Representations Improve Planning in JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37441)
*Mingu Kang,Yoori Oh,Sookyung Kim,Joonseok Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AnisoWM 与 ΛReg：用可学习对角协方差替代各向同性高斯正则，使隐空间规划代价与任务代价对齐，在四个视觉控制环境中规划成功率均优于 LeWorldModel。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐世界模型在表示空间学习动作条件动力学，常以到目标表示的欧氏距离评估候选动作。联合训练中的正则化虽可防止表示坍缩，但所形成的表示几何会决定规划时终端误差的加权方式。作者指出，预测准确与表示未坍缩并不保证隐规划代价与任务对齐：各向同性高斯正则可能诱导出与任务代价排序不一致的几何。

Method: 提出 AnisoWM 及 ΛReg，将固定的各向同性高斯目标替换为在固定迹与各向异性约束下可学习的对角协方差。预测目标、预测器结构与欧氏规划器均保持不变，该目标仅在训练时使用。作者进一步分析预测驱动的目标方差分配、其对训练分布的依赖，以及诱导度量降低规划遗憾的条件。

Result: 在四个视觉控制环境中，AnisoWM 的规划成功率均超过 LeWorldModel；其隐规划代价与任务结果的一致性也更好。

Conclusion: 表示正则化的几何形状会影响隐规划代价的任务对齐性；通过可学习的各向异性协方差约束，可在不改变预测与规划框架的前提下改善隐空间规划的排序一致性与成功率。

Abstract: Latent world models learn action-conditioned dynamics in representation space and often score candidate actions by Euclidean distance to a goal representation. Joint training typically regularizes the representation to prevent collapse, but the resulting representation geometry also determines how terminal errors are weighted during planning. We show that accurate prediction and noncollapsed representations do not guarantee a task-aligned latent planning cost: isotropic Gaussian regularization can induce a geometry that ranks feasible outcomes differently from the task cost. To address this mismatch, we introduce AnisoWM with $\Lambda$Reg, which replaces the fixed isotropic Gaussian target with a learnable diagonal covariance under fixed-trace and anisotropy constraints. The prediction objective, predictor architecture, and Euclidean planner remain unchanged; the target is used only during training. Our analysis characterizes the prediction-driven allocation of target variance, its dependence on the training distribution, and the conditions under which the induced metric reduces planning regret. Across four visual control environments, AnisoWM improves planning success over LeWorldModel in all four. Its latent planning cost also shows better agreement with task outcomes. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [74] [FP2: Equipping Robotic Foundation Models with Force Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37433)
*Hongjie Fang,Shirun Tang,Junjian Hu,Shidong Zhang,Derek Zhang,Linhao Chen,Dehai Li,Mingyu Mei,Wanxi Liu,Cewu Lu,Shiquan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FP2：一种轻量级下游接口，为任务适配的机器人基础模型（RFM）引入显式力控制，同时保留其动作生成能力，以提升接触丰富场景中的物理交互可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RFM虽具备通用操作能力，但在接触丰富场景中可靠物理交互仍具挑战，亟需在保留基础模型动作生成能力的同时实现有效的力调节。

Method: FP2采用动作-调节分解：任务适配RFM作为基础策略负责任务级动作生成，高频力控制策略仅负责交互调节。FP2压缩基础策略的上下文表征，并与力/力矩及本体感知历史结合，预测结构化力控制参数。

Result: 在4种RFM骨干与4个真实接触丰富操作任务中，FP2相比对应基础策略持续提升任务性能与力调节质量，并优于代表性力感知与力控制基线。消融表明基础策略上下文与物理反馈互补；保留基础策略动作生成可提升效率和新物体泛化。

Conclusion: FP2是一种有效的轻量级下游接口，可在不牺牲RFM动作生成能力的前提下增强接触丰富操作中的力控制与整体性能。

Abstract: Robotic foundation models (RFMs) are increasingly capable of general-purpose manipulation, yet reliable physical interaction remains challenging in contact-rich settings. We present FP2, a lightweight downstream interface that equips task-adapted RFMs with explicit force control while preserving their action-generation capability. FP2 adopts an action-regulation decomposition: the task-adapted RFM serves as a foundation policy responsible for task-level action generation, while a high-frequency force control policy focuses solely on interaction regulation. To condition force regulation on the ongoing manipulation, FP2 compresses foundation-policy contextual representations and combines them with wrench and proprioceptive histories to predict structured force-control parameters. We evaluate FP2 with four RFM backbones across four real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks. FP2 consistently improves task performance and force regulation quality over the corresponding foundation policies, while comparing favorably with representative force-aware and force-control baselines. Ablations further show that foundation-policy context and physical feedback are complementary for effective force regulation, while preserving foundation-policy action generation improves both efficiency and novel-object generalization. Project website: this http URL

</details>


### [75] [Towards Spatial Perception for Heterogeneous Robot Collaboration in Subterranean Mining Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37419)
*Mario Alberto Valdes Saucedo,Akash Patel,Christoforos Kanellakis,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PERSEPHONE自主采矿任务中的机载感知流水线，连接轻量Explorer机器人与重型Inspector机器人，利用零样本视觉语言语义分割从自然语言提示检测矿藏，并生成3D场景图以规划近距离检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 废弃地下矿深部矿藏的自主开采本质上是多智能体集成问题：单一平台无法同时具备穿越数公里退化巷道的机动性和表征矿体所需的传感载荷。

Method: Explorer机器人先对未知矿井建图，并运行零样本、视觉语言语义分割栈，直接从自然语言提示检测矿藏，生成检查目标的3D场景图；随后将地图和图交给携带先进传感载荷的Inspector机器人，用于规划近距离检查视点。流水线重点在于几何抽象：单视图边界框生成、跨视图框合并、平面拟合与多边形提取，将原始检测转化为可执行的检查目标。

Result: 在地下试验设施和一座正在开采的菱镁矿中进行了广泛现场验证，覆盖铁矿脉与菱镁矿化，并在真实且感知退化的条件下测试。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了异构多机器人感知与规划流水线在退化地下环境中自主检测矿藏并支持近距离检查的可行性。

Abstract: The autonomous extraction of deep mineral deposits in abandoned underground mines is fundamentally a multi-agent integration problem. No single platform simultaneously offers the mobility to traverse kilometers of degraded drifts and the sensing payload required to characterize an ore body. This article presents the onboard perception pipeline that bridges two heterogeneous agents within the PERSEPHONE autonomous mining mission. Which consist of a lightweight Explorer robot that maps an unknown mine and generates a 3D scene graph of inspection targets, by running a zero-shot, vision-language semantic segmentation stack that detects mineral deposits directly from natural-language prompts. The map and the graph are then handed to a second Inspector robot, which carries an advanced sensing payload and uses them to plan close-range inspection viewpoints. We detail the complete pipeline, with emphasis on the geometric abstraction that turns raw detections into actionable inspection targets, spanning per-view bounding-box generation, cross-view box merging, plane fitting, and polygon extraction, and we report an extensive field validation in a subterranean test facility and in an active magnesite mine, covering both iron-vein and magnesite mineralization under realistic, perceptually degraded conditions.

</details>


### [76] [Encore: Few-Shot Agentic Discovery of Manipulation Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37359)
*Yifan Kang,Zihan Wang,Zhiwen Fan,Bangya Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ENCORE，将少量示范作为可读证据而非训练数据，让编码智能体据此编写并迭代机器人策略程序，在仿真和真实双臂机器人任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人任务通常只有一句指令，未说明抓取方式、接触顺序和期望结果，智能体只能通过试错补全细节；现有示范多被当作训练数据，未充分发挥其作为任务细节证据的作用。

Method: 确定性构建器把每个示范蒸馏为多视角关键帧、夹爪事件、帧条和完整轨迹组成的证据包。编码智能体研究该证据包，基于固定的感知与动作 API 编写策略程序，经少量开发 rollout 迭代优化，随后冻结并在不暴露成功信号的密封评估中测试。

Result: LIBERO-PRO 上，首个程序在有示范时已能成功完成一半扰动任务，无示范仅一个任务；冻结程序优于使用同一语言模型的最强先前智能体系统（96.3% vs 89.3%）。在指令未说明目标的 RoboDojo 任务上，无示范时无程序成功。真实双臂机器人上，仅用各 5 个示范学会方块交接和杯子倒置。

Conclusion: 将示范作为编码智能体可读取的证据，能有效推断隐含任务细节并生成可执行、可泛化的策略程序，减少试错，且可迁移到真实机器人。

Abstract: Coding agents can now write, run, and debug programs with little human help. Robot tasks, however, are usually specified by a sentence that leaves out how to grasp, in what order to make contact, and what the result should look like, and an agent given only the sentence must find these details by trial and error. We introduce ENCORE, which gives the agent a few demonstrations as evidence to read rather than as training data. A deterministic builder distills each demonstration into a pack of multi-view keyframes, gripper events, frame strips, and the full trajectory. A coding agent studies the pack, writes a policy program against a fixed perception and action API, refines it iteratively over a few development rollouts, and freezes it before a sealed evaluation that never reveals the success signal. On LIBERO-PRO, the agent's first program already succeeds in half of the perturbed tasks with demonstrations and in one task without them, and the frozen programs outperform the strongest prior agentic system run with the same language model (96.3% against 89.3%). On RoboDojo tasks whose instructions leave the goal unstated, no program succeeds without demonstrations. ENCORE also runs on a real bimanual robot, learning cube handover and cup inversion from five demonstrations each.

</details>


### [77] [DROM: A Language-Guided Diffusion Framework for Multi-Skill Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37348)
*Vincenzo Pomponi,Rocco Felici,Paolo Franceschi,Stefano Baraldo,Oliver Avram,Loris Roveda,Luca Maria Gambardella,Anna Valente*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DROM 是一个语言引导的扩散框架，利用动态运动基元（DMP）增强少量专家示范，在单一生成策略中学习、表示并组合多种操作技能，实现长时序任务的自主执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限示范中学习多样、长时序任务的鲁棒操作策略仍是机器人学的根本挑战；传统运动规划或硬编码控制器难以设计姿态敏感等复杂行为，且数据采集成本高。

Method: 提出 DROM：1) 用动态运动基元（DMP）将少量专家示范增广为表达力强的多技能数据集，降低数据需求并提升超出示范工作空间的空间泛化；2) 基于运动规划扩散（MPD），通过交叉注意力扩展扩散架构以支持语言条件的多技能轨迹生成，使单一模型可生成技能一致的运动；3) 用大语言模型将高层操作指令分解为可执行的技能序列，实现自然语言交互与自主执行。

Result: 在 Franka Emika Panda、FANUC CRX25ia 实机及 MuJoCo 仿真中，于多种操作任务上验证：DROM 优于 Motion Planning Diffusion 与行为克隆基线，实现鲁棒的多技能泛化，并能仅凭少量人类示范、按自然语言指令可靠组合已学技能完成长时序操作任务。

Conclusion: DROM 能够在单一生成策略中统一学习、表示与组合操作技能，支持自然语言交互与自主任务执行，为有限示范条件下的长时序操作提供有效方案。

Abstract: Learning robust manipulation policies for diverse, long-horizon tasks from limited demonstrations remains a fundamental challenge in robotics. We present DROM, a language-guided diffusion framework that enables robots to learn, represent, and compose multiple manipulation skills within a single generative policy. DROM leverages Dynamic Movement Primitives (DMPs) to augment a small set of expert demonstrations into expressive multi-skill datasets, substantially reducing data collection while improving spatial generalization beyond the demonstrated workspace. Building upon Motion Planning Diffusion (MPD), we extend the diffusion architecture to support language-conditioned multi-skill trajectory generation through cross-attention, allowing a single model to generate skill-consistent motions for a diverse set of manipulation primitives, including orientation-sensitive behaviors that are difficult to design using conventional motion planning or hard-coded controllers. For long-horizon manipulation, a large language model decomposes high-level operator requests into executable sequences of skills, enabling natural language interaction and autonomous task execution. We validate DROM on a Franka Emika Panda robot, a FANUC CRX25ia robot, and in MuJoCo simulation across a wide range of manipulation tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that DROM outperforms Motion Planning Diffusion and Behavior Cloning baselines, achieves robust multi-skill generalization, and composes learned skills to reliably execute long-horizon manipulation tasks from natural language instructions using only a limited number of human demonstrations. Datasets, simulation environments, and more at this https URL .

</details>


### [78] [Taming VLAs under Robot Execution Errors: Self-Compensation and Stress Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37334)
*Sohyun Lee,Yoonjae Baek,Jaesang Won,Jinnyeong Kim,Kang Hyunwoo,Seung-Hwan Baek,Ivan Laptev,Suha Kwak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自补偿VLA，在部署时利用指令动作与实际执行运动之间的残差在线更新策略，预补偿机器人执行误差；并构建RoboStress仿真基准。该方法在仿真和两个真实机械臂上均显著提升任务成功率，且泛化至未见物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略在机器人实际执行偏离其指令动作时容易失败，此类执行误差来自机械磨损、负载变化等机器人力学与工况因素；仅靠训练时注入鲁棒性难以覆盖物理部署中多样且动态的执行条件。

Method: 自补偿VLA是一种部署时自适应方法，无需任务奖励或标签，在线利用VLA指令动作与机器人实际执行运动之间的残差更新策略，使策略生成预补偿执行误差的命令。为压力测试VLA鲁棒性，提出RoboStress受控仿真基准，将摩擦、背隙、柔顺性和重力补偿误差等关节级模型组合为七种部署场景，其执行误差依赖机器人状态和运动历史。

Result: 在RoboStress上，自补偿VLA的平均任务成功率高于基础策略和训练时构建鲁棒性的方法；在两个使用历史不同的真实机械臂上，各将平均任务成功率提升超过30个百分点，且增益可泛化到任务演示中未出现的物体。

Conclusion: 基于部署时指令-执行残差的在线自适应能有效提升VLA对执行误差的鲁棒性，在仿真与真实机器人上均验证有效，并具备对未见物体的泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies often fail when a robot's executed motion deviates from their commanded action. Such execution errors arise from the robot's mechanics and operating conditions, such as wear and payload changes. We propose self-compensating VLA, a deployment-time adaptation method that enables a VLA policy to pre-compensate for the robot's execution errors when generating commands. Without task rewards or labels, it updates the policy online using the residual between the action commanded by a VLA and the motion executed by the robot. To stress-test VLA robustness across execution conditions that are impractical to cover with physical robots alone, we introduce RoboStress, a controlled simulation benchmark. It combines established joint-level models of friction, backlash, compliance, and gravity-compensation error into seven deployment scenarios whose execution errors depend on the robot's state and motion history. On RoboStress, self-compensating VLA achieves higher average task success than both the base policies and methods that build in robustness during training. On two physical robot arms with different usage histories, it raises the average task success rate by more than 30 percentage points on each arm, and the gains extend to objects not seen in the task demonstrations.

</details>


### [79] [Battery-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Aggressive Quadrotor Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37316)
*Alejandro Sanchez Roncero,Olov Andersson,Petter Ogren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作研究在电池电压随负载下降时，如何让学习控制器利用额外推力，同时保留飞控的电压补偿与角速率控制，从而提升敏捷飞行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机竞速和追逃等敏捷飞行需要强加速度和精确转向，但电池放电及负载下电压跌落会导致可用推力变化；保守指令限制虽更易容忍该变化，却牺牲了未使用的性能。

Method: 训练仿真器将辨识的负载瞬态电池模型与转子动力学和固件饱和耦合；前馈策略在训练和部署时均接收滤波后的电压。通过受控消融区分更大推力指令范围的收益与电压信息的收益。

Result: 在38 g Crazyflie Brushless上，相比原始权限RL，策略在3.84 m/s下将圆轨迹跟踪误差降低49%，并保持简单任务精度；平均20圈竞速时间从106.22 s降至95.24 s。与同样增加权限但电压盲的策略相比，硬件误差和竞速时间分别降低15.3%和4.5%。仿真中，将策略电压输入替换为不同电池工况的记录会恶化困难圆轨迹跟踪，对竞速影响较小且依赖电压。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在激进学习飞行中，简单电压输入可与现有执行器补偿互补，从而在利用更大推力权限的同时提升跟踪与竞速表现。

Abstract: Agile flight tasks such as drone racing and pursuit-evasion require strong acceleration and precise turns, but the available thrust changes as the battery discharges and voltage drops under load. Conservative command limits make this variation easier to tolerate, at the cost of unused performance. We investigate how learned controllers can use that additional thrust while retaining the flight controller's voltage compensation and rate control. Our training simulator couples an identified load-transient battery model to rotor dynamics and firmware saturation. The feedforward policy receives filtered voltage during both training and deployment. Controlled ablations distinguish the benefit of a larger thrust-command range from that of voltage information. On a 38 g Crazyflie Brushless, the resulting policy reduces circle tracking error by 49% relative to stock-authority RL at 3.84 m/s, while preserving easy-task precision. Mean 20-lap race time decreases from 106.22 s to 95.24 s. Compared with a voltage-blind policy with the same increased authority, hardware error and race time are lower by 15.3% and 4.5%, respectively. In simulation, replacing the policy's voltage input with a recording from a different battery condition worsens hard-circle tracking, with a smaller, voltage-dependent effect in racing. Together, these results show where a simple voltage input complements existing actuator compensation in aggressive learned flight.

</details>


### [80] [Remember What You Did: Action-History Memory with Dual-Expert Denoising for Long-Horizon Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37307)
*Yaxin Zhao,Dianye Huang,Chenwei Wang,Chenguang Yang,Zhongliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ActMem-VLA，通过冻结微调后的 VLA 并仅训练 Mamba 记忆模块与轻量 PreAction Expert，实现历史条件引导，缓解感知混叠并提升长时程操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 缺乏对交互历史的显式访问，当前观测和机器人状态相似但任务阶段不同时易产生感知混叠，导致动作歧义与成功率下降；已有引入时间或进度线索的方法常需联合微调记忆模块与基座 VLA，增加策略训练成本。

Method: ActMem-VLA 采用双专家交接架构：在冻结的微调 VLA 上增加记忆插件，包含基于 Mamba 的记忆模块和轻量 PreAction Expert (PAE)。Mamba 将已执行动作历史编码为记忆，与当前上下文共同条件化 PAE；PAE 在早期高噪声去噪阶段引导任务进展，并将部分去噪动作交给冻结的 Action Expert (AE)，在后续低噪声阶段细化动作细节。训练中仅联合优化 Mamba 模块与 PAE，基座 VLA 保持冻结。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Mem 十个任务上平均成功率达 80.8%，高于 π0.5 的 65.2% 和 MemoryVLA 的 49.5%，仅增加 3.45% 参数；在四个真实世界任务上，相较 π0.5 平均成功率提升 28.8%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将可训练的历史条件引导模块与冻结的基座策略解耦，可在低额外参数与训练成本下有效增强 VLA 的记忆与长时程操作能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have driven rapid progress in robotic manipulation, demonstrating strong fine-grained control and promising performance on long-horizon tasks. However, many existing VLAs lack explicit access to interaction history, making them vulnerable to perceptual aliasing: similar current observations and robot states at different task stages may induce action ambiguity and lower success rate. Existing methods incorporate temporal or progress cues through feature conditioning, action-prior modification, or sampling guidance. However, methods that jointly fine-tune memory modules and the base VLA incur additional policy-training costs, motivating the separation of trainable history-conditioned steering from frozen base-policy refinement. We propose ActMem-VLA, a dual-expert handover architecture that augments a frozen, fine-tuned VLA with a memory plugin comprising a Mamba-based memory module and a lightweight PreAction Expert (PAE). Specifically, Mamba encodes executed-action history into memory that conditions PAE alongside current context. With these inputs, PAE steers task progression during early, high-noise denoising, then passes the partially denoised action to the frozen Action Expert (AE) to refine action details during the remaining low-noise steps. The fine-tuned base VLA remains frozen throughout training, while only the Mamba module and PAE are jointly optimized. On LIBERO-Mem, ActMem-VLA achieves 80.8\% average success across all ten tasks, compared with 65.2\% for $\pi_{0.5}$ and 49.5\% for MemoryVLA, while introducing only 3.45\% additional parameters. Across four real-world tasks, it improves the average success rate over $\pi_{0.5}$ by 28.8\%.

</details>


### [81] [Recovering the View: Benchmarking Physical Active Vision for Occlusion Recovery in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37292)
*Kaijun Luo,Yudi Huang,Qijun Zhong,Xinshuai Song,Yang Liu,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双臂主动视觉遮挡基准 BAVO-Bench 与未来感知主动视觉策略 A-FAR，用于评估并提升操作执行中的遮挡恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有操作基准对策略在执行中遭遇遮挡后如何通过主动改变视角恢复任务观测的支持有限，因此需要系统研究主动视觉恢复。

Method: BAVO-Bench 通过 Clean、Stage Occlusion、Random-time Occlusion 控制外部可见性；A-FAR 联合视角与操作控制，将移动相机观测统一到机器人中心 3D 坐标系，并从预训练 4D 模型中蒸馏关系结构及其未来演化，提供未来感知几何引导且部署时无需未来观测。

Result: 多个操作任务实验表明，A-FAR 提升了结构化遮挡与时间偏移遮挡下的鲁棒性，同时在干净观测下保持强性能。

Conclusion: BAVO-Bench 与 A-FAR 为双臂主动视觉遮挡下的操作性能评估和主动视觉恢复提供了基准与有效策略。

Abstract: Physical active vision allows robots to change their viewpoint when task-relevant observations become unreliable, yet existing manipulation benchmarks provide limited support for studying how policies recover from occlusion during execution. We introduce BAVO-Bench (Bimanual Active Vision under Occlusion), a bimanual active-vision benchmark that systematically controls external visibility through Clean, Stage Occlusion, and Random-time Occlusion conditions, enabling evaluation of both manipulation performance and active visual recovery. Building on this setting, we present A-FAR (Active Future-Aware Recovery), an active-vision policy for joint viewpoint and manipulation control. A-FAR represents moving-camera observations in a unified robot-centric 3D frame and distills relational structure together with its future evolution from a pretrained 4D model, providing the policy with future-aware geometric guidance without requiring future observations at deployment. Experiments across multiple manipulation tasks show that A-FAR improves robustness to both structured and temporally shifted occlusions while maintaining strong performance under clean observations.

</details>


### [82] [EgoHumanoid-V2: Human-to-Humanoid Transfer of Coordinated Whole-Body Skills for Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37181)
*Jin Chen,Yiming Jiang,Chongyang Xu,Modi Shi,Shijia Peng,Li Chen,Tianyu Li,Mu Xu,Yilun Chen,Steven Hoi,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EgoHumanoid-V2，首个用于协调全身移动操作的第一人称人类到人形机器人技能迁移框架，通过粗到细动作对齐和视觉域适应实现零样本迁移，性能与遥操作数据训练相当且采集成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示可捕获多样场景和丰富全身技能，无需机器人遥操作。现有第一人称迁移工作主要关注解耦控制下移动操作中的场景泛化，对协调全身技能的直接迁移探索不足。

Method: EgoHumanoid-V2核心是粗到细动作对齐，结合运动学参考校正与动力学感知细化，在保持全身协调的同时提升末端执行器位姿精度。此外，采用机器人手臂渲染和训练时图像增强，缩小视觉具身差距并提升视角鲁棒性。

Result: 在四个真实世界任务上，使用对齐人类数据训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略无需目标任务机器人演示即可零样本技能迁移。任务得分与使用遥操作数据训练的策略相当，且数据采集成本更低。

Conclusion: 这些结果支持将人类数据作为直接的技能监督信号。

Abstract: Human demonstrations capture diverse scenes and rich whole-body skills without requiring robot teleoperation. Prior work on egocentric transfer has emphasized scene generalization in loco-manipulation under decoupled control, leaving direct transfer of coordinated whole-body skills less explored. We present EgoHumanoid-V2, the first egocentric human-to-humanoid skill transfer framework for coordinated whole-body loco-manipulation. At its core, coarse-to-fine action alignment combines kinematic reference correction with dynamics-aware refinement. It improves end-effector pose accuracy while preserving whole-body coordination. We also use robot-arm rendering and training-time image augmentation to reduce the visual embodiment gap and improve viewpoint robustness. On four real-world tasks, vision-language-action (VLA) policies trained on aligned human data show zero-shot skill transfer without target-task robot demonstrations. Task scores are comparable to those of policies trained on teleoperation data at a lower collection cost. These results support human data as direct skill supervision.

</details>


### [83] [Disentangling Spurious Correlations in Vision-Language-Action Models via Predicting Domain-Invariant Latent Lookahead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37165)
*Junghyun Kim,Ngseo Kim,ChungWoo Lee,Seoyeon Lee,Woo-Jeong Baek,Adam Zhou,Chip Huyen,Jun-Ki Lee,Gi-Cheon Kang,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DILL：一种通过域不变未来隐变量监督VLA策略的表征学习框架，以缓解视觉分布偏移下的捷径学习，并提升视觉鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在视觉分布偏移下表现脆弱，常依赖与领域特定因素相关的虚假相关，而非任务相关结构，因此需要减少捷径学习并增强视觉鲁棒性。

Method: DILL利用域变换轨迹数据学习域不变未来隐变量来监督策略。其Task-Domain Encoder通过对比目标和高斯解缠正则，分离任务相关结构与领域特定视觉变化；随后通过前瞻预测和域解缠为VLA策略学习提供未来隐变量，促使策略关注任务相关结构。

Result: 反事实任务-视角评估表明DILL降低捷径依赖；LIBERO-Plus评估显示视觉鲁棒性提升，平均成功率达69.1%，比最强基线高11.4个百分点。真实世界操作实验支持其超出受控仿真的适用性。隐空间诊断表明行为增益伴随表征更好地保留任务一致结构并抑制领域特定变化。

Conclusion: DILL能有效缓解VLA策略中的捷径学习，在仿真与真实场景中提升视觉鲁棒性和任务成功率，并产生更任务一致、域不变的隐表征。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models remain brittle under visual distribution shifts, often relying on spurious correlations tied to domain-specific factors rather than task-relevant structure. We propose Domain-Invariant Latent Lookahead (DILL), a representation-learning framework that mitigates shortcut learning in VLA policies. Our key idea is to supervise policies with domain-invariant future latents learned from domain-transformed trajectory data. A Task-Domain Encoder is trained with contrastive objectives and Gaussian disentanglement regularization to separate task-relevant structure from domain-specific visual variation. The learned encoder then provides future latents for VLA policy learning through lookahead prediction and domain disentanglement, encouraging the policy to focus on task-relevant structure rather than incidental visual factors. Counterfactual task-view evaluations show that DILL reduces shortcut reliance, while LIBERO-Plus evaluations demonstrate improved visual robustness, with 69.1% average success, 11.4 percentage points above the strongest baseline. Real-world manipulation experiments further support DILL's applicability beyond controlled simulation. Complementary latent-space diagnostics show that these behavioral gains are accompanied by representations that better preserve task-consistent structure while suppressing domain-specific variation. Our project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [84] [NIDAR: NIR-Guided Intrinsic Decomposition for Scalable Scene-Agnostic LiDAR Intensity Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36878)
*Junjie Zhang,Jie Yin,Kefei Qian,Jie Li,Mengpei Jia,Yajuan Dun,Wenbo Chu,Guofa Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NIDAR 是一个前馈框架，利用 RGB 外观与仿真器几何合成稠密类强度观测，将反射率相关图像线索迁移到仿真点云，无需目标场景强度标签或逐场景优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR 回波强度为机器人感知与状态估计提供互补的表面响应线索，但许多仿真流程忽略该模态，或依赖需要真实强度监督与逐场景优化的重建方法，导致数据采集与拟合成本高，且难以跨仿真场景复用。

Method: NIDAR 结合预训练伪近红外（pseudo-NIR）转换、层级本征分解、几何感知调制与源域分布校准，将反射率相关图像线索迁移至仿真点云。其可学习组件使用 Waymo 数据离线训练，评估时权重与校准保持固定，并在 Waymo 与 nuScenes 上部署，因此无需目标场景强度标签或基于梯度的目标场景拟合。

Result: 与所评估的重建基线相比，NIDAR 取得有竞争力的逐像素精度，并在结构与感知保真度上表现良好；受控的 pseudo-NIR 与 RGB 诊断表明，伪近红外先验在按论文所提反射率与重映射路径使用时最有益，而非作为简单直接强度回归器。作者进一步将 NIDAR 与 Unreal Engine 5、Isaac Sim 及生成式 LiDAR 流程集成，并在两个仿真室内场景中用两个强度感知 SLAM 系统展示潜在下游效用。

Conclusion: NIDAR 为所评估设置提供了可扩展的强度合成接口；当前结果基于仿真与离线评估，不构成真实机器人验证，跨波长、相机配置、嵌入式部署及真实传感器验证仍属未来工作。

Abstract: LiDAR return intensity provides complementary surface-response cues for robotic perception and state estimation, yet many simulation pipelines omit it or reproduce it using reconstruction methods that require real intensity supervision and per-scene optimization. These requirements increase data-collection and fitting costs and limit reuse across simulated scenes. We present NIDAR, a feed-forward framework that synthesizes dense intensity-like observations from RGB appearance and simulator geometry. NIDAR combines pretrained pseudo-NIR translation, hierarchical intrinsic decomposition, geometry-aware modulation, and source-domain distribution calibration to transfer reflectance-related image cues to simulated point clouds. Its learned components are trained offline using Waymo data; their weights and calibration remain fixed during evaluation on Waymo and nuScenes. Deployment therefore requires neither target-scene intensity labels nor target-scene gradient-based fitting. The reported comparisons show competitive pixel-wise accuracy and favorable structural and perceptual fidelity against the evaluated reconstruction baselines. A controlled pseudo-NIR-versus-RGB diagnostic further shows that the pseudo-NIR prior is most beneficial when used through the paper-aligned reflectance-and-remapping route, rather than as a simple direct intensity regressor. We further integrate NIDAR with Unreal Engine 5, Isaac Sim, and a generative LiDAR pipeline. Two intensity-aware SLAM systems evaluated in two simulated indoor scenes suggest potential downstream utility, but do not constitute real-robot validation. NIDAR therefore offers a scalable intensity-synthesis interface for the evaluated settings; cross-wavelength, camera-configuration, embedded, and real-sensor validation remain future work.

</details>


### [85] [PreferenceFlow: Test-Time Guidance of Flow-Matching Robot Policies from Human Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36872)
*Yiqi Tang,Diyuan Shi,Runze Li,Donglin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PreferenceFlow 是一种在测试时利用人类干预作为局部偏好监督、无需环境奖励或更新基策略来改进预训练流匹配策略的框架。在 Franka 四类真实精密插入任务上，平均成功率从冻结策略的 69% 提升到 90.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配策略能表达复杂机器人行为，但部署时在分布偏移下易产生局部错误；现实操作中强化学习所需奖励信号难以指定或获取，因此需要无需奖励的策略改进方法。

Method: 将人类干预片段与同一初始条件状态生成的机器人片段配对，训练偏好模型；推理时采用 QGF 采样更新，把值梯度替换为在估计的干净动作处评估的偏好梯度；引入梯度上限损失惩罚干预对上的过大梯度，零梯度损失抑制在专家演示动作附近的引导。

Result: 在 Franka 机器人四项真实精密插入任务中，PreferenceFlow 平均成功率为 90.5%，冻结策略为 69%；消融实验支持梯度正则化和偏好标签正确排序的作用。

Conclusion: 结果表明，人类干预可作为局部偏好监督，有效引导冻结的生成式机器人策略，在无环境奖励和策略更新条件下实现测试时策略改进。

Abstract: Flow-matching policies can represent complex robot behaviors but remain susceptible to local errors under distribution shift at deployment. Many reinforcement learning approaches to policy improvement require reward signals that are difficult to specify or obtain in real-world manipulation. We present PreferenceFlow, a framework for improving a pretrained flow policy at test time without environment rewards or updates to the base policy. Human intervention chunks are paired with robot chunks generated from the same initial conditioning state to train a preference model. During infer- ence, we adopt the QGF sampling update, replacing its value gradient with the preference gradient evaluated at an estimated clean action. A gradient-cap loss penalizes excessive gradients on intervention pairs, while a zero-gradient loss discourages guidance near actions from expert demonstrations. On four real-world precision insertion tasks with a Franka robot, Pref- erenceFlow achieves a mean success rate of 90.5%, compared with 69% for the frozen policy. Ablations support the roles of gradient regularization and correctly ordered preference labels in the evaluated settings. These results demonstrate the utility of human interventions as local preference supervision for guiding frozen generative robot policies.

</details>


### [86] [VidAct: Learning Manipulation from In-the-Wild Videos with Object-Centric 3D Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36870)
*Hang Li,Mingxin Zhang,Zihan Wu,Yang Tian,Dong Chen,Fengyi Shen,Yuan Meng,Xiangtong Yao,Heng Zhang,Ziyuan Liu,Zhenshan Bing,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VidAct 是一种从单目视频学习物体中心、3D 感知操作策略的视频到机器人框架，可实现零样本真实世界部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于重建的视频学习方法常依赖受限视角或人到机器人重定向，重建轨迹难以适应新物体配置且容易扭曲形状；同时策略缺乏精确的物体级 3D 几何感知，限制物体定位与形状理解，而这对精细操作至关重要。

Method: VidAct 包含三部分：1）从任意演示视频重建物体网格与运动，并在静态物体坐标系中规范化，避免实施例特定重定向并适应多视角；2）采用残差轨迹迁移，将重建运动适配到新物体配置，同时保持运动形状；3）策略学习时，以每帧预测仿真提供的特权完整物体点云为辅助任务，部署时仅用 RGB，从而对物体姿态和 3D 几何提供密集的物体中心监督。

Result: 在人类、机器人、生成及互联网视频上的实验表明，VidAct 具有广泛视频适用性和零样本部署能力；逐帧完整物体 3D 监督提升了策略泛化与 sim-to-real 成功率；残差轨迹迁移实现了可靠轨迹适配并更好保持形状。

Conclusion: VidAct 通过物体中心、3D 感知的视频到机器人学习，有效利用单目视频实现可泛化、可部署的操作策略，并缓解了视角限制、重定向依赖与物体级几何感知不足的问题。

Abstract: Video demonstrations offer a scalable alternative to costly robot data for learning manipulation, yet existing reconstruction-based approaches often rely on constrained camera viewpoints or human-to-robot retargeting, while the reconstructed trajectories are difficult to adapt to new objects configurations without distorting the trajectory shape. Another key limitation is that the resulting policies often lack precise object-level 3D geometry awareness, limiting object grounding and object shape awareness critical for precise manipulation. To bridge these gaps, we propose VidAct, an efficient video-to-robot framework that learns object-centric, 3D-aware manipulation policies from a single monocular video per task and enables zero-shot real-world deployment. VidAct consists of three key components. First, VidAct reconstructs object meshes and motion from arbitrary demo videos and canonicalizes the motion in the static object frame, avoiding embodiment-specific retargeting and accommodating diverse camera viewpoints. Second, VidAct employ residual trajectory transfer for adapting the reconstructed motion to novel object configurations while preserving its motion shape. Finally, as the key policy-learning component, VidAct predicts simulation-provided privileged complete-object point clouds at each frame as an auxiliary task while retaining RGB-only deployment, providing dense object-centric supervision over both object pose and 3D geometry. Experiments on human, robot, generated, and internet videos demonstrate broad video applicability and zero-shot deployment. Per-frame complete-object 3D supervision improves policy generalization and sim-to-real success, while residual trajectory transfer enables reliable trajectory adaptation with better shape preservation.

</details>


### [87] [Spotter: Let the Embodied Model Lead, and the VLM Reflect for It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36808)
*Long Li,Qichao Zhao,Yue Yang,Fan Xu,Zhe Wang,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Chao Qu,Heng Tao Shen,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Spotter，反转VLM与具身模型角色：具身模型持续执行，VLM并行监控并在检测到错误时介入反思纠正，从而提升任务成功率且额外延迟较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身模型无法响应自身失败，难以修复已知错误；仅靠随机重试、语言描述或best-of-N选择均难以成功。作者认为原因是仅训练成功演示且输入信息过窄，因此反思需来自能接收episode历史与文本等更丰富信息的VLM。

Method: Spotter让具身模型主导并连续执行，VLM在并行路径上通过轻量本地screener监控；仅在确认错误时VLM介入，反思并纠正错误，然后将控制权交回具身模型。分别使用Qwen和GPT作为VLM。

Result: 使用GPT时，Spotter在RoboCasa上使Cosmos Policy和π_0.5分别提升5.6和7.5个百分点；在RoboTwin 2.0 Hard上将π_0.5从47.2%提升至57.0%；在真实机器人上从53%提升至83%。使用Qwen时，成功episode仅比单独具身模型多13–16秒，比同模型VLM主导基线少约70%时间。

Conclusion: 反思应来自VLM；Spotter通过反转VLM主导范式，在错误确认时才介入，能在提升具身模型性能的同时控制额外延迟，验证了VLM并行监控与纠错的有效性。

Abstract: Current embodied models do not respond to their own failures, although what just went wrong could inform a small adjustment on the next attempt, the kind of reflection behind the gains of thinking in language models. We test whether they can repair a known error, which requires producing a correction and judging whether it is right. Stopped at a failure and allowed to retry, they seldom repair it through their own randomness or from a language description of the error, and best-of-N selection cannot pick the successful candidate after a failure. We attribute this to training only on successful demonstrations and to inputs too narrow to show what went wrong, and conclude that reflection must come from a vision-language model (VLM), which takes in far more information, such as the episode history and text, and is more general. Prior VLM-led work has the VLM plan every step and invoke the embodied model as a tool, placing the VLM on the critical path. We propose Spotter, which reverses the roles: the embodied model leads and executes continuously, while the VLM runs in parallel, monitors through a lightweight local screener, intervenes only when an error is detected, reflects on and corrects it, and returns control. We run Spotter with Qwen and with GPT as the VLM, and both improve the embodied models; with GPT, Spotter improves Cosmos Policy and $\pi_{0.5}$ by 5.6 and 7.5 percentage points on RoboCasa, and raises $\pi_{0.5}$ from 47.2% to 57.0% on the Hard setting of RoboTwin 2.0 and from 53% to 83% on a real robot. Because the VLM steps in only when an error is confirmed, a successful episode with Qwen takes only 13 to 16 s longer than with the embodied model alone and about 70% less time than with a VLM-led baseline using the same model. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [88] [TaRL: Learning General and Physical Rewards from Tactile Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36785)
*Po-Yi Wu,Dao-Jan Chang,Shang-Ya Hsiao,Hong-Ming Chen,Yu-Cheng Su,Tsung-Wei Ke*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TaRL 是一种基于触觉示范学习奖励的框架，用触觉形变图序列回归任务完成进度，为接触丰富操作提供塑形奖励，显著提升样本效率与成功率，并能与视觉奖励互补及跨物体泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作需要精确接触、稳定抓取和定向施力。强化学习可自动学习此类行为，但性能依赖奖励设计：稀疏奖励学习效率低，密集奖励难以指定。视觉奖励学习虽可从无动作示范中推断奖励，却仅依赖视觉观测，无法捕捉视觉目标之外的奖励。

Method: 提出 Tactile Reward Learning（TaRL）。TaRL 以触觉形变图序列为输入，利用成功与失败示范回归任务完成进度。由于捕捉局部机器人-物体交互，它能提供用于学习稳固抓取和正确施力方向的信息性反馈，同时对物体位置等场景布局变化具有鲁棒性。

Result: 在仿真中 4 个操作任务和真实世界中 2 个任务上评估。作为塑形奖励，TaRL 显著提升样本效率和最终成功率：仿真中 Nut threading 成功率从 34% 提升至 56%，真实世界中 cube pickup 从 37% 提升至 97%。触觉与视觉奖励结合可进一步提升性能。TaRL 还能跨物体实例泛化：在 box placement 上训练后直接部署到 can placement，显著改善新任务策略学习。

Conclusion: TaRL 通过学习触觉奖励有效改进了接触丰富操作中的策略学习，兼具信息性、鲁棒性和泛化能力，并可补充视觉奖励，为复杂操作任务提供更优奖励信号。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires robots to sequence precise contacts, maintain stable grasps, and apply directed forces. Reinforcement learning (RL) can acquire such behaviors automatically, but its performance hinges on reward design: sparse rewards reduce the learning efficiency, while dense rewards are hard to specify. Visual reward learning addresses this by inferring rewards from action-free demonstrations. Because it conditions only on visual observations, it fails to capture rewards beyond visual goals. We propose Tactile Reward Learning (TaRL), a framework that learns rewards from tactile demonstrations. TaRL takes a sequence of tactile deformation maps as input, and regresses task-completion progress from both successful and failed demonstrations. Because TaRL captures local robot-object interaction, it provides informative feedback to learn firm grasps and correctly directed forces; meanwhile, it is robust to changes in scene layout such as object position. We evaluate TaRL on four manipulation tasks in simulation and two in the real world. Used as a shaping reward, it substantially improves both sample efficiency and final success rate, raising success on Nut threading from 34% to 56% in simulation and on cube pickup from 37% to 97% in the real world. Combining tactile with visual rewards improves performance further. TaRL also generalizes across object instances: trained on box placement and directly deployed to can placement, it significantly improves policy learning on the new task. Project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [89] [Scale-Invariant Manipulability Shape Tracking Across Heterogeneous Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36784)
*Geunwoo Kwon,Dong-gyu Lee,Kai Li,Soonwoong Hwang,Wansoo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种尺度不变的机械臂可操作度形状跟踪方法，将仅相差正标量因子的矩阵视为等价，并用单位行列式代表在AIRM下跟踪；该方法嵌入带约束的关节速度QP，实现异构机器人及人到机器人的可操作度形状迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨不同尺寸和运动学结构的系统迁移可操作度时，若目标只是复现椭球方向和各半轴长度比例，匹配绝对尺度并非必要；传统全矩阵跟踪会同时惩罚形状和绝对尺度差异，即使只需形状匹配也会引入不必要限制。

Method: 将正标量缩放等价的矩阵类用单位行列式代表表示；推导单位行列式形状代表的微分，并在仿射不变黎曼度量（AIRM）下给出切空间跟踪残差的正交坐标表示；构造尺度不变目标，与末端位置和末端执行器方向任务共同组成带约束的关节速度二次规划（QP）。

Result: 在四种异构机器人上进行机器人-机器人和人-机器人迁移仿真。所提方法在三个跟随者上无需尺度调节即达到末端形状距离9.30×10^-5；Full方法需在运动中调节机器人特定目标尺度，且在KR500和UR20上仍有0.19-0.31的末端轴比误差。对于带并发任务的人体伸展，所提方法在四台机器人上产生沿X方向拉长的双力形状，与人体目标一致，末端位置误差为参考臂长的2.4%-5.6%，而Full方法跟踪原始人体椭球时误差最高达75%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过将可操作度矩阵的绝对尺度视为无关变量，所提尺度不变形状跟踪能在异构机器人间及人到机器人迁移中更有效地保持椭球形状与任务方向，同时显著降低末端位置误差，优于全矩阵跟踪。

Abstract: When transferring manipulability across systems with different sizes and kinematic structures, matching absolute ellipsoid scale may be unnecessary when the goal is to reproduce orientation and semi-axis length ratios. Full-matrix tracking, however, penalizes both shape and absolute-scale differences, even when only shape matching is required. We therefore propose a scale-invariant manipulability shape-tracking method that treats matrices differing only by a positive scalar factor as equivalent and uses their unit-determinant representatives. We derive the differential of the unit-determinant shape representative and an orthonormal coordinate representation of the tangent tracking residual under the affine-invariant Riemannian metric (AIRM). The resulting scale-invariant objective is integrated with position and end-effector direction tasks in a constrained joint-velocity quadratic program. Simulations with four heterogeneous robots evaluate robot-to-robot and human-to-robot transfer. On three followers, the proposed method achieves endpoint shape distances of 9.30 x 10^-5 without scale tuning. With robot-specific target scales tuned during motion, the Full method retains endpoint axis-ratio errors of 0.19-0.31 on KR500 and UR20. For human reaching with concurrent tasks, the proposed method yields dual force shapes elongated along X like the human target on all four robots, with endpoint position errors of 2.4-5.6% of reference arm length versus up to 75% for the Full method tracking the original human ellipsoid.

</details>


### [90] [DRHeC: Differentiable Rendering for Hand-Eye Calibration with RGB-Based Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36779)
*Xiaotian Zhang,Yusheng Wang,Naoya Kagawa,Noritaka Takamura,Keiji Okuhara,Hiroyasu Baba,Jun Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于RGB的可微渲染手眼标定框架，通过融合颜色与掩码几何特征并引入掩码引导的图像翻译，提高标定精度与优化稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手眼标定依赖标记物，精度受标记物精度与可观测性限制；无标记的可微渲染方法虽可解释且无需标定阶段标记物，但使用二值掩码会丢失内部轮廓细节，并存在优化不稳定和局部极小值问题。

Method: 提出RGB-based可微渲染框架，联合利用颜色和掩码几何特征提供更丰富的几何与外观线索；同时提出掩码引导的image-to-image translation方法，在翻译过程中显式保持颜色与几何一致性。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中验证了精度与鲁棒性，并优于现有可微渲染方法。UR5e真实实验中抓取成功率达88.9%，插入成功率达57.4%，分别比SOTA方法EasyHeC高46.3和48.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 该方法通过RGB可微渲染与掩码引导图像翻译，有效提升无标记手眼标定的精度、鲁棒性和优化稳定性，并在真实机器人操作任务中显著超越现有方法。

Abstract: Accurate hand-eye calibration is crucial for precision manipulation. Traditional methods rely on markers, with their precision dependent on marker accuracy and observability. In contrast, markerless methods, such as learning-based approaches, use deep neural networks to directly extract keypoints or features from images, enabling the computation of hand-eye transformation with a single image and without the need for physical markers. Recently, differentiable rendering-based methods for hand-eye calibration have leveraged physical models to render binary masks and compare them with observations, enabling hand-eye calibration without fiducial markers in the calibration stage and providing interpretable optimization. While the state-of-the-art differentiable rendering methods achieve remarkable accuracy, the use of binary masks can result in the loss of internal profile details, reducing precision. Additionally, these methods can also suffer from unstable optimization and local minima. In this study, we propose a novel RGB-based differentiable rendering framework that provides richer geometric and appearance cues by incorporating color and mask geometric features, thereby improving calibration accuracy and optimization stability. Additionally, we propose a mask-guided image-to-image translation method to ensure explicit preservation of color and geometric consistency throughout the translation. Our approach is validated through both simulation and real-world experiments, with results demonstrating strong accuracy and robustness and clear improvements over existing differentiable rendering methods. Our method achieves a grasping success rate of 88.9% and insertion success rate of 57.4% on the UR5e real-world experiment, outperforming the state-of-the-art differentiable rendering hand-eye calibration method EasyHeC by 46.3 and 48.1 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [91] [LexiconVLA: Learning Reusable Atomic Action Codebooks for Unseen Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36774)
*Zeming Wei,Jianheng Ye,Xinshuai Song,Sirui Chen,Yang Liu,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LexiconVLA 提出可检索的原子动作词典，通过全局/细节码本与视觉-原子动作对齐实现跨任务动作复用，在 RLBench 和真机上提升未见任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型难以复用未见任务中反复出现的交互；诊断研究表明，任务完成可靠并不代表其组成原子动作能在不同任务上下文中一致执行。

Method: 构建可检索的原子动作词典；全局码本与细节码本分别捕捉共享交互结构和细粒度执行变化；视觉-原子动作对齐将基于视觉状态变化的轨迹重建与基于动作码的视觉结果预测耦合，使词典扎根于运动及其效果；在 AtomAction 数据集（69 个任务、57,803 个片段）上以轨迹重建和视觉对齐学习码本；规划器与场景感知适配器将新目标转化为码条件子任务，交由共享策略执行，无需技能专用专家或部署时参数更新。

Result: 在 26 个 RLBench 任务、5 种策略骨干上，LexiconVLA 在 18 个已见任务上基本保持性能，并提升 8 个策略训练未见任务的成功率；与 BridgeVLA 结合时，未见任务成功率从 16.67% 提升至 34.17%（+17.50 个百分点），总体成功率达 71.08%，为已报告结果方法中最高；真机实验展示分步执行与故障恢复。

Conclusion: LexiconVLA 通过原子动作词典和视觉对齐实现跨任务动作复用，在不牺牲已见任务性能的情况下提升未见任务泛化能力，并支持真实机器人分步执行与失败恢复。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models struggle to reuse recurring interactions in unseen tasks. Our diagnostic study reveals that reliable task completion does not imply consistent execution of constituent atomic actions across task contexts. We present LexiconVLA, a retrievable atomic-action lexicon for cross-task reuse. Global and detail codebooks capture shared interaction structure and fine-grained execution variation, respectively, preserving both reusable patterns and execution details. Visual-Atomic Action Alignment couples trajectory reconstruction from visual state changes with visual outcome prediction from action codes, grounding the lexicon in motion and its effects. We learn these codebooks with trajectory reconstruction and visual alignment on our AtomAction Dataset of 57,803 segments from 69 tasks. A planner and scene-aware adapter translate new goals into code-conditioned subtasks for a shared policy, without skill-specific experts or deployment-time parameter updates. Across five policy backbones on 26 RLBench tasks, LexiconVLA largely maintains performance on 18 seen tasks while improving success on 8 tasks held out from policy training. With BridgeVLA, unseen-task success rises from 16.67% to 34.17% (+17.50 percentage points), and overall success reaches 71.08%, the highest among methods with reported results. Real-robot experiments demonstrate stepwise execution and failure recovery.

</details>


### [92] [Degeneracy-Orthogonal Geometric Constraints for LiDAR SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36753)
*Minseo Kim,Yina Kim,Jinhwa Hwang,Alex Junho Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DeCOD（退化正交轮廓偏移描述子），利用横截面地标为长隧道/管道等轴向均匀环境中的 LiDAR SLAM 提供退化方向上的度量约束，从而抑制纵向漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长隧道、管道等轴向均匀走廊中，LiDAR 里程计沿特征弱的前进方向存在不可约束漂移；这种结构退化无法仅靠局部扫描匹配解决。

Method: DeCOD 是一种面向横截面地标的结构对齐几何描述子：对估计边界轮廓参数化有符号法向偏差；匹配时显式解决航向歧义，并通过畸变估计解耦一阶轮廓误差；匹配地标生成几何因子，在位姿图优化中约束横截面位置与走廊轴对齐，修正纵向漂移，同时不约束绕公共轴的旋转。

Result: 在公开基准和现场实验中，DeCOD 相比标准 3D 描述子实现更鲁棒的地标检索，并能在不同里程计前端上稳定轨迹，在几何退化下可靠约束纵向漂移。

Conclusion: DeCOD 通过横截面地标匹配有效应对轴向均匀走廊中的几何退化，提升长隧道与管道场景下自主导航的定位与建图鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous robot navigation relies on simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) to estimate motion and maintain an accurate pose within an environment. However, in axially uniform corridors such as long tunnels and pipelines, LiDAR odometry is fundamentally limited by unconstrained drift along the feature-weak travel direction. This structural degeneracy cannot be resolved by local scan matching alone. To address this challenge, we propose the Degeneracy-orthogonal Contour Offset Descriptor (DeCOD), a structure-aligned geometric descriptor for cross-sectional landmarks. Cross-sectional boundaries, such as pipe joints and structural rings, provide metric constraints along this degenerate axis, but distinguishing individual landmarks requires capturing subtle surface variations across nearly identical profiles. The descriptor parameterizes signed normal deviation from estimated boundary contours, and matching explicitly resolves heading ambiguity and decouples first-order contour errors by distortion estimation. Matched landmarks yield geometric factors that enforce agreement in cross-section position and corridor axis alignment during pose-graph optimization, correcting longitudinal drift while leaving rotation about the common axis unconstrained. On a public benchmark and in field experiments, DeCOD achieves robust landmark retrieval over standard 3D descriptors and successfully stabilizes trajectories across different odometry frontends, reliably constraining longitudinal drift under geometric degeneracy.

</details>


### [93] [T$^2$Mem: Learning Test-Time Memory for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36720)
*Yize Liu,Huang Huang,Yining Hong,Zijian Du,Zhi Cao,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 T$^2$Mem，一种让预训练视觉-语言-动作策略通过测试时训练将历史观测编码为快速权重，从而在无外部记忆模块与记忆标注下实现记忆依赖机器人操作的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆依赖的机器人操作要求策略利用当前观测中已不存在的信息；仅保留历史不够，记忆必须保留并支持未来动作。核心挑战是：无记忆的基础模型能否仅从动作示范中学会保留和使用历史信息，而不依赖外部记忆支持。

Method: T$^2$Mem 在预训练 VLA 策略内发展该能力，无需外部推理模型或记忆专用标注。它使用测试时训练，通过在线自监督更新将观测历史编码为紧凑快速权重，避免重复处理完整历史；采用观测锚定接口提取视觉-语言信息以形成记忆，并向动作专家提供检索上下文；动作监督塑造记忆保留与使用内容，交替式记忆-策略学习使各组件在优化时具有固定对应部分。

Result: 在 16 个 RoboMME 任务上，T$^2$Mem 将平均成功率从无记忆基础策略的 17.93% 提升至 56.83%，优于基准中报告的循环记忆方法；受控性能分析表明，相较显式方法至少实现 3 倍推理加速。

Conclusion: T$^2$Mem 表明，无需外部记忆模块或专门标注，预训练 VLA 策略可通过测试时自监督快速权重形成有效记忆，并显著提升记忆依赖机器人操作的成功率与推理效率。

Abstract: Memory-dependent robotic manipulation requires policies to use information that is no longer available in the current observation. Retaining history alone is insufficient: memory must preserve information that supports future actions. One challenge is whether a memory-free foundation model can learn to retain and use historical information from action demonstrations alone, without external memory support. We introduce T$^2$Mem, a framework that develops this capability within a pretrained vision-language-action policy, without external reasoning models or memory-specific annotations. T$^2$Mem uses test-time training to encode observation history into compact fast weights through online self-supervised updates, avoiding repeated processing of the full history. An observation-grounded interface extracts vision-language information for memory formation and supplies retrieved context to the action expert. Action supervision shapes what the memory learns to retain and use, while alternating memory-policy learning gives each component a fixed counterpart during optimization. Across 16 RoboMME tasks, T$^2$Mem improves average success from 17.93% to 56.83% over the memory-free base policy and outperforms the recurrent-memory methods reported in the benchmark, while controlled profiling indicates at least 3x inference speedup over explicit methods. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [94] [Kinematic Nonlinear Spatio-Temporal Trajectory Warping for Contact-Rich Dexterous Manipulation Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36676)
*Hyojae Park,Arjun S. Lakshmipathy,Nancy S. Pollard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种简单有效的接触丰富灵巧操作示范复用方法：以手与物体轨迹为输入，通过基于接触分布的物体中心式变形管线生成非线性轨迹，适应中间路点、障碍、时序变化及不同起止构型；在公开人类手部运动数据集的12个变体上评估并与基线比较，且可泛化到不同机械手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有接触丰富灵巧操作示范难以直接复用到新场景；需在保持复杂接触关系的同时，适应中间路点、环境障碍、时间偏移和不同起止配置，并可靠生成高维灵巧手轨迹。

Method: 输入手和物体轨迹，输出高质量非线性轨迹变形；核心是利用接触分布，采用简单的以物体为中心的变形规格流程，从而可靠计算复杂、高维的灵巧手轨迹，并处理中间路点、环境障碍、时间偏移和不同起止配置。

Result: 在公开人类手部运动数据集上，基于4个示范生成12个变体进行评估；进行了基线对比；并展示了方法对不同机械手的泛化能力。结果与代码将在发表时提供。

Conclusion: 该方法简单有效，能够复用接触丰富灵巧操作示范，并在多种变化和不同操作器上表现出泛化性。

Abstract: We present a straightforward but effective method for repurposing existing contact-rich dexterous manipulation demonstrations. Starting from inputs of hand and object trajectories, our method outputs high-quality nonlinear trajectory warps that account for intermediate waypoints, environmental barriers, temporal shifts, and varied start/end configurations. Foundational to our method is the utilization of contact distributions, which we show allows us to reliably compute complex and high-dimensional dexterous hand trajectories following a simple object-centric warp specification pipeline. We evaluate our method across 12 variations sourced from 4 demonstrations in a publicly available dataset of human hand motion data, perform baseline comparisons, and demonstrate generalization of our approach to different manipulators. Results and code will be made available on publication.

</details>


### [95] [Where Predictive Supervision Goes Shapes What VLA Policies Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36645)
*Hanseul Kim,Jewon Yeom,Youngjoon Jeong,Minsoo Jo,Taesup Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究探讨未来预测监督何时能真正改善VLA策略的视觉表征；关键不在预测质量本身，而在于预测接口能否将监督信号传递到策略用于动作决策的表征中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来预测被广泛用于提升视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，但其前提是预测场景演化能促进控制所需的表征。然而，预测准确并不等于策略学到了对动作更有用的表征，尤其在分布偏移下，控制成功依赖于保留空间状态和合理场景变化。

Method: 通过控制变量比较不同预测接口、目标构造、预测时域和训练条件，分析预测误差如何塑造策略的视觉流，并在模拟和物理分布偏移下评估VLA策略的鲁棒性。

Result: 不同预测接口产生显著不同的预测结果和视觉表征，包括可迁移到陌生场景的空间、动力学和动作信息；更直接、与场景匹配的未来监督训练出的VLA策略在模拟和物理分布偏移下表现出更强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 未来预测应被视为一个表征学习设计问题，其对控制的价值取决于监督信号是否到达策略借以行动的表征。

Abstract: Future prediction is increasingly used to improve vision-language-action (VLA) policies, based on the premise that anticipating scene evolution encourages representations useful for control. However, forecast quality alone does not establish that a policy has learned a better representation for action. This distinction matters under distribution shift, where successful control depends on preserving spatial state and likely scene change beyond familiar configurations. We study what determines whether predictive supervision improves the visual representation used by a VLA policy. Through controlled comparisons with matched target constructions, prediction horizons, and training conditions, we find that different prediction interfaces produce markedly different forecasts and visual representations, including in the spatial, dynamics, and action information that transfers beyond familiar scenes. We trace these differences to how predictive errors shape the policy's visual stream. Consistent with this controlled finding, VLA policies trained with more direct, scene-matched future supervision show stronger robustness under simulated and physical distribution shifts. Together, our results frame future prediction as a representation-learning design problem whose value for control depends on whether its supervision reaches the representations through which the policy acts.

</details>


### [96] [Foundation-Model-Guided Topology-Aware Semantic Risk Fields for Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36640)
*Giung Lee,Weihang Guo,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种由基础模型引导、具备拓扑感知能力的语义风险场，将操作安全从单纯避碰扩展到上下文相关语义风险，并作为模块化代价用于下游运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常环境中的机器人运动规划不仅要满足硬几何约束，还需考虑依赖上下文、具有语义含义的风险，因此需要超越碰撞避免的安全表示。

Method: 针对每个被操作物体与场景物体对，利用基础模型给出六个方向的风险权重和该物体对特定的空间衰减尺度；再结合体素化3D场景几何，通过拓扑感知屏蔽与测地空间衰减构建风险场；GPU并行后端批量计算物体级距离与风险，生成稠密3D场，作为下游运动规划的模块化代价。

Result: 在完整与部分屏障下评估屏蔽行为，并与像素级语义先验基线比较3D工作空间表示；在三个家庭仿真场景中，相同几何约束下，使用该场优化的轨迹比仅避碰轨迹具有更低语义暴露；同时评估了支撑流程的计算实用性与可靠性。

Conclusion: 结果支持将该场作为一种实用的、拓扑感知的语义代价表示，用于超越碰撞避免的操作规划。

Abstract: Robot motion planning in everyday environments must satisfy hard geometric constraints while accounting for context-dependent semantic risk. We present a foundation-model-guided, topology-aware semantic risk field that extends manipulation safety beyond collision avoidance. For each manipulated-object/scene-object pair, a foundation model provides six directional risk weights and a pair-specific spatial decay scale. The method combines these priors with voxelized 3D scene geometry using topology-aware shielding and geodesic spatial decay. A GPU-parallel backend batches object-level distance and risk computations to construct a dense 3D field that serves as a modular cost for downstream motion planning. We evaluate the field's shielding behavior under full and partial barriers and compare its 3D workspace representation with a pixel-wise semantic-prior baseline. Across three household simulation scenarios, trajectories optimized with the proposed field have lower semantic exposure than collision-only trajectories under the same geometric constraints. We also evaluate the computational practicality and reliability of the supporting pipeline. Together, these results support the proposed field as a practical topology-aware semantic cost representation for manipulation planning beyond collision avoidance.

</details>


### [97] [RoboChrono: A Real Robot Benchmark for Streaming Task Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36605)
*Yuzhou Wu,Longteng Fan,Zimeng Li,Yu Wanchan,Ting Zhang,Yiyang Ma,Shihao Li,Wei Ying,Jianbin Qin,Jiajian Jing,Fangwen Chen,Yifan Wu,Zichen Zhang,Ruiqi Yang,Weibin Kong,Yihang Xu,Haoran Liu,Zonghang He,Xuyang Liu,YiFan Xiong,Siteng Huang,Tao Xu,Zhuo Xu,Long Chen,Ruoxiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出 RoboChrono 基准，用于流式机器人操作任务理解，包含39个场景、34,713个评测实例，覆盖识别、对齐与时间定位七类任务；对18个视觉语言模型零样本评测及5个开放权重模型输入消融，发现视觉匹配与时间排序能力不一致，且下一动作预测更依赖任务/动作先验而非视觉证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解机器人操作过程需结合交互历史与任务进度解释视觉观察，但现有评测常以总体分数衡量，难以诊断不同能力差异；因此需要一个面向流式任务理解的诊断性基准。

Method: 构建 RoboChrono：基于真实机器人执行与裸手人类录制，含39个场景、34,713个实例；将七项任务归为识别、对齐、时间定位三类，覆盖动作理解与预测、视觉对应、时间排序、动作定位；对18个VLM进行零样本评测，并对5个开放权重模型在匹配问题上进行输入消融。

Result: 零样本评测显示任务间差异显著：GPT-6-Astra 在Frame Matching达98.3%，Frame Ordering仅68.3%；RynnBrain1.1-122B-A10B差距更大（95.4% vs 32.9%）。消融显示移除视觉观察使Current Action Recognition下降22.1个百分点，而Next Action Prediction仅下降0.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 强视觉匹配不必然意味着强时间排序；下一动作预测可在缺乏视觉证据时由任务与动作先验支持。评估机器人操作任务理解需按能力维度进行诊断性评测，而非仅看总体分数。

Abstract: Understanding ongoing robot manipulation requires models to interpret visual observations in relation to interaction history and task progress. We introduce RoboChrono, a benchmark for streaming task understanding comprising 39 scenarios and 34,713 evaluation instances, constructed from real robot executions and complementary bare-hand human recordings. The benchmark evaluates seven tasks grouped into recognition, alignment, and temporal grounding, covering action understanding and anticipation, visual correspondence, temporal ordering, and action localization. Zero-shot evaluation of 18 vision-language models reveals substantial differences across tasks. GPT-6-Astra achieves 98.3% accuracy on Frame Matching but 68.3% on Frame Ordering, while RynnBrain1.1-122B-A10B exhibits a larger gap, reaching 95.4% and 32.9%, respectively. Input ablations on matched questions with five open-weight models further reveal distinct dependencies on visual evidence: removing visual observations reduces Current Action Recognition accuracy by 22.1 percentage points, whereas Next Action Prediction decreases by only 0.7 points. These findings show that strong visual matching does not consistently coincide with strong temporal ordering, and suggest that next-action prediction can be supported by task and action priors even when visual evidence is unavailable. RoboChrono provides a diagnostic setting for examining these differences, highlighting the need for capability-specific evaluation beyond aggregate scores when assessing task understanding in robot manipulation.

</details>


### [98] [OTRetarget: Joint Robot and Object Motion Retargeting via Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36602)
*Guillaume Besset,Erwann Carn,Timothée Carecchio,Valentin Tordjman-Levavasseur,Fabian Schramm,Yann de Mont-Marin,Justin Carpentier,Ajay Suresha Sathya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 OTR ETARGET，一种联合重定向机器人与多物体运动的方法，在保持环境交互的同时将人类 loco-manipulation 演示迁移到不同形态的人形机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将人类运动迁移到人形机器人时，需在适应机器人形态的同时保持与地面及操作对象的接触一致；仅靠骨骼运动无法完整描述交互，而固定物体轨迹又会限制对新本体的适应，因此需要联合重定向机器人及多物体运动。

Method: 用有符号距离、最近表面点和相对方向表示表面交互，并通过熵最优传输在人体、机器人和物体几何之间迁移这些量；将得到的交互目标融入约束逆问题逆运动学，平衡接触保持与运动风格，并逐帧联合优化机器人与物体位姿；无需缩放场景或演示即可处理机器人-物体及物体-物体交互。

Result: 在 OMOMO 上，机器人-物体交互 Jaccard 达到 87%，深度误差 8.7 mm，优于 OmniRetarget 的 28% 和 29.3 mm；并成功迁移到实体 G1 人形机器人，使用基于重定向参考通过强化学习训练的全身策略完成双手搬箱上桌等运动。

Conclusion: 该统一重定向框架能在适应机器人形态的同时保持多物体交互一致性，为真实人形机器人的 loco-manipulation 技能迁移提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Transferring human motion to humanoid robots requires adapting the demonstrated motion to the robot morphology while preserving interactions with the environment. This is particularly challenging for loco-manipulation tasks, where contacts with the ground and manipulated objects must remain consistent despite differences in body proportions. Yet, skeletal motion alone does not fully describe these interactions, and fixing object trajectories limits the adaptation to a new embodiment. In this paper, we introduce OTR ETARGET, a unified approach to jointly retarget robot and multi-object motion from human demonstrations. Our approach represents surface interactions through signed distances, closest surface points, and relative directions, and uses entropic optimal transport to transfer these quantities across human, robot, and object geometries. We incorporate the resulting interaction targets into a constrained inverse kinematics formulation that balances contact preservation with motion style and jointly optimizes robot and object poses at each frame. This formulation accommodates robot-object and object-object interactions without rescaling the scene or the demonstration. We validate the proposed approach on OMOMO, where it achieves a robot- object interaction Jaccard score of 87% and a depth error of 8.7 mm, compared with 28% and 29.3 mm for OmniRetarget. Finally, we demonstrate transfer to a physical G1 humanoid using whole-body policies trained with reinforcement learning on the retargeted references, across motions including two-handed box pick-and-place onto a table.

</details>


### [99] [Executor-aware Candidate Selection via a Feasibility Certificate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36597)
*Sooin Choi,Soonwoong Hwang,Wansoo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于证书的候选选择框架：在预测轨迹状态上由执行器硬约束构造命令见证，并对照原始约束验证，以筛除几何有效但执行器不可行的候选，且不改变候选生成、排序与执行器。在 5085 次几何有效评估中，795 次无执行器可行命令；证书充分但保守，未认证这 795 次，另有 7.09% 参考可行案例未被认证。FR3 与固定基座 RB-Y1 仿真显示认证常改变候选选择，但关联更低规划器-命令覆盖率和更高标称跟踪误差，且无一致的交互储备优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化机器人常将运动规划与下游执行器分离，执行器施加状态相关硬约束。几何上有效的候选可能无法由执行器命令集实现，因此需要在候选选择阶段验证执行可行性，同时尽量不侵入现有候选生成、排序和执行器流程。

Method: 提出证书式候选选择框架：在预测 rollout 状态上从执行器硬集合构造命令见证，并针对原始约束验证该见证；保持候选生成、排序和执行器不变。评估包括两个机器人模型上的 5085 次几何有效数值评估、FR3 与固定基座 RB-Y1 受控仿真、事后精确 LP 准入基线，以及 MoveIt/OMPL 规划器生成的外部候选池研究。

Result: 5085 次几何有效评估中，795 次未找到执行器可行命令。证书充分但保守：795 次均未被认证，且 7.09% 参考可行案例未获认证。FR3 和固定基座 RB-Y1 仿真中，证书准入频繁改变候选选择；事后精确 LP 基线显示保守性依赖平台。相比几何选择，证书准入关联更低规划器-命令覆盖率与更高标称跟踪误差，在到达状态交互储备上无一致优势。MoveIt/OMPL 研究进一步在外部候选池上评估同一准入规则。

Conclusion: 证书准入可安全排除部分执行器不可行候选，但当前形式过于保守，并可能牺牲覆盖与跟踪性能；其效果具有平台依赖性，需进一步降低保守性以兼顾可行性、覆盖率和跟踪性能。

Abstract: Modular robotic systems often separate motion planning from a downstream executor that enforces state-dependent hard constraints. A candidate that is geometrically valid may therefore be incompatible with the executor's available command set. We present a certificate-based candidate-selection framework that constructs a command witness from the executor hard set at predicted rollout states and verifies it against the original constraints, without changing candidate generation, ranking, or the executor. Across 5,085 geometry-valid numerical evaluations on two robot models, 795 admitted no executor-feasible command. The certificate is sufficient but conservative: none of the 795 was certified, while 7.09\% of reference-feasible cases remained uncertified. In controlled FR3 and fixed-base RB-Y1 simulations, certificate admission frequently changed candidate selection, and a post-hoc exact linear-programming (LP) admission baseline revealed platform-dependent conservatism. Relative to geometry-based selection, certificate admission was associated with lower planner-command coverage and higher nominal tracking error, without a consistent advantage in reached-state interaction reserve. A planner-generated MoveIt/OMPL study further evaluates the same admission rule on externally generated candidate pools.

</details>


### [100] [HACo: Learning Haptic Active Compliance for Force-Aware Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36596)
*Naisheng Ye,Yinzhe Zhou,Junkai Zhao,Yuhang Lu,Checheng Yu,Zhenjie Yang,Pengwei Wang,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 HACo：一种从触觉反馈直接学习力调节动作的主动柔顺策略，融合指尖触觉与关节力矩，并通过门控触觉交叉注意力实现闭环力调节；在真实多接触灵巧操作基准上平均成功率达 83%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的灵巧操作需要策略将物理反馈转化为运动命令，并在多接触交互演化中调节负载；现有策略常忽略指尖触觉与关节力矩的互补反馈，且动作目标要么导致过大加载，要么忽略受物体约束的运动。

Method: 通过柔顺调节遥操作将操作者输入转换为控制器可执行的柔顺动作，在保留运动意图的同时调节负载；HACo 直接学习这些动作，并以命令-状态差异作为辅助柔顺意图监督。策略结合局部指尖触觉响应与关节力矩反馈，后者可捕捉负载在 articulated hand 中的传递及触觉覆盖之外的接触。合规接地模块使用门控触觉交叉注意力，将动作生成扎根于演化的触觉状态，从而无需显式在线接触建模即可闭环调节力。

Result: 在覆盖多接触摩擦、切向交互、易碎曲面接触、旋转力矩和可变形物体操作的真实世界基准上，每个任务进行 20 次试验；HACo 平均成功率为 83%，而最强基线为 35%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，HACo 能在多样化的力敏感灵巧操作任务中实现主动柔顺。

Abstract: Contact-rich dexterous manipulation requires policies that translate physical feedback into motion commands while regulating interaction loads across evolving multi-contact interactions. This requires haptic observations of contact state and action supervision showing how commands should adapt. Existing policies often overlook complementary fingertip tactile and joint-torque feedback, while common action targets either encode excessive loading or omit motion constrained by the object. We introduce HACo, a Haptic Active Compliance policy that learns force-regulating actions directly from haptic feedback. Compliance-regulated teleoperation converts operator inputs into controller-executable compliant actions that preserve motion intent while regulating loads. HACo learns these actions directly, using command-state discrepancy as auxiliary compliant-intent supervision. It combines local fingertip tactile responses with joint-torque feedback capturing load transmission through the articulated hand, including contacts beyond tactile coverage. A Compliance Grounding Module uses gated haptic cross-attention to ground action generation in the evolving haptic state, enabling closed-loop force regulation without explicit online contact modeling. We evaluate HACo on a real-world benchmark covering multi-contact friction, tangential interaction, fragile curved-surface contact, rotational torque, and deformable-object manipulation. Across 20 trials per task, HACo achieves an 83% mean success rate, compared with 35% for the strongest evaluated baseline. These results demonstrate active compliance across diverse force-sensitive dexterous manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [101] [Simple Agentic Memory for Generalist Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36595)
*Yuyou Zhang,Yunbei Zhang,Miao Li,Janet Wang,Zijian Jin,Shilong Liu,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练记忆层SimpleARM，为冻结的通用机器人策略提供基于交互历史的任务状态记忆，并在RoboMME上显著优于非oracle基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人控制不仅需要保留或压缩过去视觉观测，还需要单个帧无法显式表示的交互派生状态，如持久身份关系、累积进度和有序流程；现有视觉记忆系统对此不足。

Method: SimpleARM是面向冻结通用机器人策略的免训练记忆层：根据任务指令确定监控内容；用冻结感知工具在线维护紧凑的类型化状态；仅当候选子目标依赖历史时，通过结构化访问检索状态；执行前用当前视角 grounding 解析被回忆实体。在RoboMME记忆依赖操作基准上评估。

Result: 在全部16个任务和3个策略随机种子上，SimpleARM平均成功率为67.17%，最强非oracle基线为44.51%。匹配消融显示机制特异性：移除关系、引用、进度或路线状态，会在控制中需要相应状态的任务上造成大幅性能下降，而对其他任务影响较小。

Conclusion: 结果支持基于状态的机器人记忆观点：用于控制的有效记忆并非简单保留的视觉历史，而是从交互历史中提取的紧凑、任务相关状态。

Abstract: Visual-memory systems commonly retain or compress past observations. Robot control additionally requires interaction-derived state that no individual frame may explicitly represent, such as persistent identity relations, accumulated progress, or ordered procedures. We introduce Simple Agentic Robot Memory (SimpleARM), a training-free memory layer for frozen generalist robot policies. From the task instruction, SimpleARM specifies what to monitor; frozen perceptual tools maintain compact typed state online; structured access retrieves that state only when a proposed subgoal depends on history; and current-view grounding resolves recalled entities before execution. We evaluate SimpleARM on RoboMME, a benchmark of memory-dependent robot manipulation tasks that require history information no longer available in the current observation. Across all 16 tasks and three policy seeds, SimpleARM achieves 67.17% mean success, compared with 44.51% for the strongest non-oracle baseline. Matched ablations show mechanism specificity: removing relation, reference, progress, or route state produces large losses where the affected state is retrieved for control, while largely sparing other tasks. These results support a state-based view of robot memory: effective memory for control is not simply retained visual history, but compact task-relevant state derived from the interaction history.

</details>


### [102] [Cooperative Multi-Agent Vision-Language-Action Models via Reinforced Fine Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36588)
*Ruixiao Xu,Wong Lik Hang Kenny,Zhiqian Liu,Jianing Guo,Hanxiao Li,Kejian Shi,Shuning Zhang,Pu Feng,Yongjia Ma,Yuqing Ma,Kai Chen,Qi Dou,Yaodong Yang,Xianglong Liu,Simin Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出三阶段强化微调流水线，使预训练VLA具备多机器人协作能力，并在仿真与真实任务中显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练VLA基于大规模单智能体数据，缺乏多机器人细粒度协调技能；多机器人示范SFT受数据限制且无法从自身经验改进；硬多智能体任务中现有在线RL因协同探索噪声和更新不稳定效果不佳。

Method: 三阶段RFT：1）初始化感知数据采集，扫描初始配置，仅在预训练VLA反复失败时调用人类示范，兼顾初始化偏移鲁棒性并降低人力成本；2）离线信用过滤微调，将信用分配给各智能体，仅用正优势的每智能体轨迹微调，而非联合轨迹；3）在线潜在空间微调，冻结VLA，在其潜在噪声空间进行RL，避免噪声协同探索和不稳定更新。

Result: 以π0和π0.5为骨干，在RoboTwin、RoboFactory和双Franka真实操作共11个任务上评估，平均成功率分别提升+23.1%、+16.4%、+44%。

Conclusion: 该多智能体VLA强化微调框架有效提升协作任务性能，缓解示范数据瓶颈与在线RL不稳定问题，并已开源代码。

Abstract: We study reinforcement learning (RL) methods for cooperative multi-agent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. This problem is challenging because VLAs are pretrained on large-scale single-agent data and therefore lack the fine-grained coordination skills required for inter-robot collaboration. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on multi-robot demonstrations partially bridges this gap, but its performance is bounded by the demonstration data and cannot improve from its own experience. We present a three-stage reinforced fine-tuning (RFT) pipeline for multi-agent VLAs. First, initialization-aware data collection sweeps over initial configurations and invokes human demonstrations only when the pretrained VLA repeatedly fails, yielding robustness to initialization shift with reduced human cost. Second, offline credit-filtered tuning assigns credit to individual agents and fine-tunes on per-agent trajectories with positive advantage rather than on entire joint rollouts.
