Third, we find existing online RL for VLAs are less effective for hard multi-agent tasks, which we attribute to noisy co-exploration and unstable updates. We instead use online latent-space fine tuning, which freeze the VLA and perform RL in its latent noise space. We evaluate our multi-agent VLA with both $\pi_0$ and $\pi_{0.5}$ backbones across 11 tasks in RoboTwin, RoboFactory and real-world manipulation with two Franka robots. Our multi-agent VLA improves the average success rate by $+23.1\%$, $+16.4\%$, and $+44\%$ on RoboTwin, RoboFactory, and real-world tasks, respectively. Code available at this https URL .

</details>


### [103] [Inferring Soil Friction Angle from Robot Foot-Ground Force Histories: A Bayesian Inverse Approach to Proprioceptive Soil Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36582)
*Dawei Xu,Zhijie Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究验证了四足机器人旋转腿的力历史能否用于反演无黏性土的内摩擦角 φ，并在匹配模型实验中取得高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人足-地交互信号有望实现空间分布式的原位土体强度表征；作为第一步，需要检验简化旋转腿的力历史是否足以识别无黏性土的内摩擦角 φ。

Method: 采用基于物质点法（MPM）的二维连续体模型，并以实测旋转腿力历史进行基准校验；由该模型生成训练数据，并构建两个高斯过程代理模型，对完整力历史进行贝叶斯反演。

Result: 在匹配模型实验中，该框架反演了 14 个非网格化摩擦角，中位绝对误差约 0.1°（最大约 0.7°）；所有情况下报告的置信区间均包含真值。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当前向模型正确设定时 φ 是可识别的；这支持进一步发展本体感知式土体传感以应对空间变化地形，并可应用于机器人训练中的物理驱动世界模型及野火后边坡评估等场景。

Abstract: Foot-ground interaction signals recorded by quadruped robots may enable spatially distributed, in situ characterization of soil strength. As a first step, we test whether the internal friction angle $\phi$ of cohesionless soil can be identified from the force history of a simplified rotating leg. A two-dimensional continuum model implemented with the material point method, benchmarked against measured rotating-leg force histories, generates the training data, and two Gaussian-process surrogates support Bayesian inversion of the full histories. In matched-model experiments, the framework recovers 14 off-grid friction angles with a median absolute error of approximately $0.1^\circ$ (maximum $\sim 0.7^\circ$); the reported credible intervals contain the true value in every case. These results establish that $\phi$ is identifiable when the forward model is correctly specified, and support further development of proprioceptive soil sensing for spatially variable terrain, with applications from physics-grounded world models for robot training to post-wildfire slope assessment.

</details>


### [104] [EquivDP3: A SIM(3)-Invariant Point-Cloud Encoder for Data-Efficient Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36575)
*Abu Hanif Muhammad Syarubany,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EquivDP3 将 SIM(3) 等变 VNN 编码器引入面向 Unitree G1 人形机器人的分层扩散策略，在少样本下显著提升全身移动操作的数据效率，且额外延迟极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形 loco-manipulation 的视觉运动策略需仅凭少量示教泛化到不同物体位姿与光照；DP3 使用 PointNet 编码器，不具备操作任务所需的 SIM(3)（旋转、平移、缩放）等变性，EquiBot 已为轮式机械臂解决该问题，但尚未扩展到更复杂的人形本体。

Method: 提出 EquivDP3：两阶段策略，高层扩散规划器采用 SIM(3) 等变 VNN 编码器，以 6 Hz 输出全身命令块；低层由冻结的预训练 RL 运动策略与手臂微分逆运动学模块以 50 Hz 执行；通过行为克隆端到端训练。在 IsaacLab 两个仿真基准上，与四个非等变基线在 5–100 个示教、分布内/外条件下对比。

Result: 优势集中在低数据区间：5–10 个示教时成功率为 67.1%，基线为 38.2–52.4%；50–100 个示教时所有编码器收敛至 74.3–85.2%，排序不再有意义。仅本体感觉控制验证该差距确为感知性：移除点云后，5 个示教下成功率从 60%（EquivDP3）降至 31%，10 个示教下从 99% 降至 78%，但 50–100 个示教时差距消失，表明高数据平台期是基准上限而非各编码器学到相同不变性。编码器相较被替换的 PointNet 每个动作块仅增加 0.8 ms 延迟。

Conclusion: 将几何对称性嵌入分层扩散策略的感知骨干，是在示教稀缺时提升人形 loco-manipulation 数据效率的实用且近乎零成本的方法。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies for humanoid loco-manipulation must generalize across object poses and lighting from only a handful of demonstrations. 3D Diffusion Policy (DP3) conditions a diffusion-based action generator on point-cloud features, but its PointNet-style encoder has no built-in equivariance to the rotations, translations, and scalings (SIM(3)) that manipulation tasks respect. EquiBot closed this gap for wheeled manipulators with a SIM(3)-equivariant Vector Neuron Network (VNN) encoder. We extend this to a substantially more complex embodiment, the 43-joint Unitree G1 humanoid, and propose EquivDP3: a two-stage policy where a high-level diffusion planner with a SIM(3)-equivariant VNN encoder emits 6 Hz whole-body command chunks, executed at 50 Hz by a frozen, pre-trained RL locomotion policy and a differential inverse-kinematics module for the arms, trained end-to-end by behavior cloning. Across two simulated IsaacLab benchmarks and four non-equivariant baselines (5-100 demonstrations, in- and out-of-distribution), EquivDP3's advantage concentrates in the low-data regime: at 5-10 demonstrations it reaches 67.1% success versus 38.2-52.4% for the baselines, while by 50-100 all encoders converge (74.3-85.2%) and the ordering is no longer meaningful. A proprioception-only control confirms this gap is genuinely perceptual: with the point cloud removed, success drops to 31% vs. 60% (EquivDP3) at 5 demonstrations and 78% vs. 99% at 10, but vanishes by 50-100, showing the high-data plateau reflects a benchmark ceiling, not five encoders learning the same invariance. The encoder costs only 0.8 ms of extra latency per action chunk over the PointNet encoder it replaces. Baking geometric symmetry into a hierarchical diffusion policy's perception backbone is a practical, nearly free way to improve data efficiency for humanoid loco-manipulation when demonstrations are scarce.

</details>


### [105] [Riemannian Splat Regression Models for Learning Time Fields on Arbitrary Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36561)
*Anastasios Manganaris,Rohit Banerjee,Gokul Prabhakaran,Ahmed H. Qureshi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需坐标投影即可在任意黎曼流形上学习函数的模型，将splat回归与由wrapped Gaussian分布定义的splats结合，并用于学习到达时间场，与适配各流形的MLP比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意黎曼流形上的运动规划重要且困难，欧氏空间规划方法难以直接适用；NTFields等神经时间场方法必须借助临时的坐标投影到更高维空间，无法直接使用。作者希望无需投影，以最小模型容量获得高维流形上最优规划的最低运行时方法。

Method: 将splat回归模型与由wrapped Gaussian分布定义的splats相结合，构建无需坐标投影、可在黎曼流形上学习任意函数的模型，并将其用于多个黎曼流形上的到达时间场学习。

Result: 成功在多个黎曼流形上学习了到达时间场，并与针对每个流形分别适配的多层感知机（MLP）比较了准确率和模型大小。

Conclusion: 该模型无需坐标投影即可直接在黎曼流形上学习函数，为高维流形上的运动规划提供了一种更高效且模型容量需求更低的潜在方案。

Abstract: Motion planning on arbitrary Riemannian manifolds is an important and difficult problem that frustrates typical planning methods for Euclidean spaces. In particular, motion planning methods that approximate optimal time-to-go functions with neural networks, e.g., Neural Time Fields (NTFields), cannot be directly applied without using ad-hoc coordinate projections into higher dimensions. Using these methods directly without such projections is desirable, as it promises to provide the lowest-possible-runtime method for obtaining optimal plans on high-dimensional manifolds while using minimal model capacity. In this work, we develop a model that requires no coordinate projection and can learn arbitrary functions on Riemannian manifolds by combining splat regression models with splats defined by wrapped Gaussian distributions. We successfully apply this model for learning arrival time fields on several Riemannian manifolds, and we compare the accuracy and model size of this approach with multi-layer perceptrons adapted to work on each manifold individually.

</details>


### [106] [A robust single-sensing-element tactile sensor for concurrent pressure and tackiness detection with real-time signal decoupling capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36558)
*Ying Yang,Mingwei Gu,Jia-Sen Xie,Xingyu Ma,Yan-Na Lu,Lin Zheng,Jinhui Gu,Junshuai Chen,Yunjie Lu,Denys Makarov,Jin Ge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种表面柔软触觉传感器，将霍尔效应传感器与软磁复合材料集成于坚固弹性框架中，通过受压凹陷和脱粘凸起动态改变霍尔传感器—磁体间距，实现压力与拉脱力的实时双模区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将粘性/黏附感知集成到人形机器人人工皮肤中，可显著提升其认知与操作能力；但现有触觉传感器面临多模态信号解耦困难和稳定性不足的挑战。

Method: 设计表面柔软触觉传感器：霍尔效应传感器与软磁复合材料置于稳健弹性框架内。受压时表面凹陷，从粘性表面拉脱时显著凸起，动态改变霍尔传感器与磁体距离。该机制产生全流程可追溯且基线分离的信号，可在单一传感元件、同一接触点上实时区分压力与拉脱力，并消除应力串扰；坚固框架与磁—机传感机制结合提升可靠性和信号基线稳定性。

Result: 实现压力与拉脱力的实时区分和同一接触点双模感知，提高传感精度与灵敏度，并获得优异可靠性和基线稳定性。该传感器可用于评估粘附性能、监测橡胶老化、精确操作轻质物体以及认知自然物体表面特性。

Conclusion: 该触觉传感器在提升机器人黏附评估、物体操作和表面特性认知等能力方面具有重要应用潜力。

Abstract: Integrating tackiness sensation into the artificial skin of humanoid robots significantly enhances their cognitive and operational capabilities. However existing tactile sensors face challenges in decoupling of the multimodal signal and stability. Here we present a surface-soft tactile sensor that incorporates a Hall effect sensor and a soft magnetic composite within a robust elastic framework. The sensor surface indents under pressure and bulges prominently when retracted from sticky surfaces dynamically altering the Hall sensor-magnet distance. This generates whole-process-traceable and baseline-separated signals enabling real-time differentiation between pressure and pull-off force. This single-sensing-element design facilitates bimodal sensing at the same contact spot while eliminate stress cross-talk enhancing both accuracy and sensitivity. The fusion of a robust framework and magneto-mechanical sensing mechanism equips the sensor with exceptional reliability and excellent signal baseline stability. This tactile sensor holds substantial potential for advancing robotic capabilities in evaluating adhesive properties monitoring rubber aging precisely handling lightweight objects and cognizing natural objects surface characteristics.

</details>


### [107] [Learning to Explore Hidden Kinematics for Articulated Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36553)
*Ruiyao Liu,Boshu Lei,Zhuoyang Pan,Kostas Daniilidis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出将主动感知的动作选择摊销到训练中：用贝叶斯滤波维护关节类型与参数信念，将其渲染为逐点铰接流场来条件化策略，并以强化学习奖励不确定性降低，从而在铰接物体操作中超越先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅从视觉估计铰接物体运动学常存在歧义，交互可消歧；但现有主动感知方法逐步贪心搜索最锐化信念的单个动作，若缺少接触与惯性动力学前向模型，则难以扩展到长时程，而这些模型本身未知。

Method: 维护关节类型和参数的信念分布，由生成先验初始化，并基于观测到的部件运动进行贝叶斯滤波更新；将信念渲染为逐点铰接流场，即当前后验对物体每点运动的预测，并以此条件化策略。该表示利用铰接运动归纳偏置，优于信念的潜在编码或从观测追踪的流。用强化学习训练策略，奖励每次交互从后验中移除的熵，使信息性探索成为学习行为而非每步搜索。

Result: 在PartManip基准的门和抽屉操作上优于先前方法；在ArticuRiddle新数据集（外观暗示错误铰接）上达到61.7%成功率，最佳先前方法为44.4%。

Conclusion: 表明摊销式主动感知与基于铰接流场的信念条件化能有效学习信息性探索，提升铰接物体操作在运动学歧义和误导性外观下的鲁棒性与成功率。

Abstract: The kinematics of an articulated object is often ambiguous from vision alone. Interaction resolves the ambiguity, and active perception methods exploit this by searching for the single action that most sharpens a belief over the kinematic parameters at each step. Such greedy search cannot be extended over a horizon without forward models of the contact and inertial dynamics, which are themselves unknown. We instead amortize action selection into training. We maintain a belief distribution over joint type and parameters, initialized from a generative prior and updated by Bayesian filtering on the observed part motion. To condition the policy on this belief, we render it as a per-point articulation flow field, the motion that the current posterior predicts for every point on the object. Carrying the inductive bias of articulated motion, this representation generalizes better than a latent encoding of the belief or flow tracked from observation. We train the policy with reinforcement learning, rewarding the entropy that each interaction removes from the posterior, so that informative exploration becomes learned behavior rather than a search at every step. Our method outperforms previous approaches across door and drawer manipulation on the PartManip benchmark, and reaches 61.7% success on ArticuRiddle, a new dataset of objects whose appearance implies the wrong articulation, against 44.4% for the best previous method. Project Website: this https URL

</details>


### [108] [Planning Oriented 3D Scene Completion via Coupled TUDF Occupancy Representation Learning from Partial Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36543)
*Tianyou Yu,Pengfei Zhao,Chao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向路径规划的3D场景补全框架，联合预测TUDF连续几何表示与体素占用图，并通过双向耦合学习提升几何重建与下游规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人导航中部分可观测性导致传感器覆盖有限和遮挡，环境中大量区域未被观测；现有场景补全方法多关注补全建图或重建部分观测的3D结构，较少研究如何设计场景补全以服务于路径规划等下游任务。

Method: 以部分LiDAR观测为输入，所提框架联合预测补全后的基于TUDF的连续几何表示和体素级占用图；引入双向耦合学习方案，使TUDF特征为占用重建提供密集几何指导，占用特征为距离场估计提供互补结构约束；网络直接输出完整占用和TUDF表示，可将TUDF无缝集成到轨迹规划中而无需后处理。

Result: 在未见环境上的大量实验表明，该方法在几何重建质量和下游规划性能方面均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 面向路径规划的耦合几何场景补全能有效利用TUDF与占用表示之间的协同关系，在提升场景补全质量的同时改善下游路径规划表现。

Abstract: Partial observability remains a fundamental challenge in robotic navigation, where limited sensor coverage and occlusions leave large portions of the environment unobserved. Existing scene completion methods primarily focus on improving incomplete mapping or reconstructing partially observed 3D structures, but rarely investigate how scene completion can be designed to benefit downstream tasks such as path planning. In this work, we propose a path-planning-oriented 3D scene completion framework that moves beyond pure occupancy modeling toward a coupled geometric formulation. Specifically, given partial LiDAR observations as input, the proposed framework jointly predicts completed Truncated Unsigned Distance Field (TUDF)-based continuous geometric representations and voxel-wise occupancy maps. This coupled representation allows the network to better reason about obstacle boundaries and free-space geometry. To fully exploit the synergy between the two representations, we introduce a bidirectionally coupled learning scheme, where TUDF features provide dense geometric guidance to improve occupancy reconstruction, while occupancy features in turn offer complementary structural constraints that refine distance-field estimation. Consequently, the proposed network directly predicts complete occupancy and TUDF representations, allowing seamless integration of TUDF into trajectory planning without post-processing. Extensive experiments on unseen environments demonstrate that the proposed method consistently improves both geometric reconstruction quality and downstream planning performance.

</details>


### [109] [Reactive Real-Time Flow Policies via Asynchronous Distribution Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36540)
*Moritz Zoellner,Reece O'Mahoney,Ioannis Havoutis,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究VLA异步执行是否保持原策略的动作分布，发现非马尔可夫演示下分布会显著偏移并限制反应性；提出递归流场蒸馏与Propose-Resolve来对齐分布，从而恢复反应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA等通用机器人策略泛化能力强，但推理延迟与实时控制需求冲突。异步执行虽可避免动作块之间的停顿，却可能改变原VLA的动作分布，尤其在非马尔可夫演示下会限制策略反应性。

Method: 通过两种机制将异步策略的动作分布对齐原VLA：1）Recursive Flow-Field Distillation，利用VLA的动作生成流训练异步策略，并理论刻画所学分布；2）Propose-Resolve，异步生成多个动作序列，再用最新观测基于VLA动作分布似然的轻量近似进行选择。

Result: 递归流场蒸馏使异步策略几乎覆盖原VLA的全部动作范围，而现有异步方法仅恢复一部分；整体方法在LIBERO上匹配原VLA成功率，在RoboMimic上保留约80%成功率，比现有异步方法高约30个百分点。

Conclusion: 异步执行未必保持原VLA动作分布，需显式对齐；所提方法能恢复反应性并显著缩小与同步VLA的性能差距。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies such as vision-language-action models (VLAs) have achieved remarkable generalization, but their inference delays can conflict with the demands of real-time control. Asynchronous execution avoids pauses between action chunks by predicting the next sequence of actions while the robot carries out the previous one. In this paper, we study whether asynchronous execution produces the same action distribution as the original VLA. We find that, for non-Markovian demonstrations, asynchronous execution can produce a fundamentally different action distribution, which can limit the policy's reactivity. In our method, we seek to restore this reactivity by aligning the asynchronously produced action distribution with that of the original VLA through two complementary mechanisms. First, Recursive Flow-Field Distillation trains the asynchronous policy using the VLA's action-generation flow. We characterize the learned distribution theoretically and show experimentally that our asynchronous policy can generate nearly the full range of actions the original VLA would produce, while existing asynchronous methods recover only a fraction of that range. Second, Propose-Resolve prepares multiple action sequences asynchronously and uses the latest observation to select among them based on a lightweight approximation of their likelihood under the VLA's action distribution. Our resulting method matches the original VLA's success on LIBERO and retains about 80% of its success on RoboMimic, about 30 percentage points more than existing asynchronous methods.

</details>


### [110] [Asymmetric Scout-Worker Reconnaissance for Route Validation in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36537)
*Kashif Khurshid Noori,Jaskrit Singh,Athanasios Ch. Kapoutsis,Jing Xiao,Constantinos Chamzas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种共生式侦察者框架，利用小型敏捷侦察机器人探索未知环境中连接有序目标点的大型工作机器人可行路径段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有探索与重规划方法通常假设单一通行性模型，并为同一机器人寻找最优路径，无法直接处理侦察者可行但工作机器人不可行的非对称通行能力场景。

Method: 将问题建模为非对称侦察者-工作者侦察；侦察者凭借更小 footprint 和更高机动性，仅探索识别连接有序目标点所需的工作机器人可行路径段，并依据侦察观测推断工作机器人可行性。

Result: 仿真与真实环境实验表明，该方法能验证可行路线、用经验证的工作机器人可行绕行修复阻塞路段，并相对基线探索与规划方法显著减少侦察者行程；真实室内部署中，侦察者穿越狭窄走廊识别出工作机器人可行路线。

Conclusion: 共生式侦察框架可有效应对通行能力非对称的未知环境路线规划，减少不必要探索，并为大型工作机器人提供可行路径。

Abstract: This paper studies asymmetric scout-worker reconnaissance in unknown environments, where a small, agile autonomous scout explores routes for a larger worker robot that must visit an ordered sequence of goal locations. Because the scout has a smaller footprint and greater mobility, a scout-traversable route may be infeasible for the worker; worker feasibility must therefore be inferred from scout observations. This setting is not explicitly addressed by existing exploration and replanning methods, which typically assume a single traversability model and seek optimal paths for the same robot performing the exploration. We introduce a symbiotic scout-based framework that exploits the scout's superior mobility to explore only the portions of the unknown environment needed to identify worker-feasible path segments connecting the ordered goals. Evaluations in simulated and real-world settings demonstrate that the proposed approach validates feasible routes, repairs blocked segments with validated worker-feasible detours, and substantially reduces scout travel compared to baseline exploration and planning methods. A real-world indoor deployment further demonstrates the scout navigating narrow corridors to identify a worker-feasible route.

</details>


### [111] [Trajectory-Level Mode Guidance for Controllable Diffusion-Based Multi-Robot Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36530)
*Tianyou Yu,Shengze Cai,Chao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种在干净轨迹空间中进行时间步相关引导的扩散规划框架，可在融入粗略/部分轨迹先验与规划代价的同时保留轨迹生成的多模态性，并扩展到多机器人协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动规划通常存在多个可行解，扩散模型适合学习多模态轨迹分布；但如何在不过度限制生成的情况下引入粗略或部分轨迹先验仍具挑战。此类先验通常指示期望的解空间区域而非单一解，因此需要保持多模态的条件生成方法。

Method: 在干净轨迹空间引导生成，并在反向扩散过程中逐步注入轨迹先验。每个反向步骤重建干净轨迹，作为统一空间整合规划代价与部分轨迹先验：规划代价通过梯度精修引入；部分先验按对应噪声层级逐步注入，且引导强度随时间递减。早期引导生成靠近先验，后期逐渐释放约束以保留扩散模型固有模态。多机器人规划通过加入机器人间碰撞代价自然扩展。

Result: 在单机器人和多机器人规划任务上的实验表明，该方法可实现可控轨迹合成、生成多样可行解，并实现安全的多智能体协调。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在保持多模态性的同时有效融合规划代价与部分轨迹先验，实现可控扩散轨迹生成，并可扩展至多机器人协同规划。

Abstract: Motion planning often admits multiple feasible solutions, making multimodal generation valuable, particularly for flexible multi-robot coordination. Diffusion models naturally learn such trajectory distributions, yet incorporating coarse and partial trajectory priors without restricting generation remains challenging. Such priors indicate a desirable region of the solution space rather than a single solution, motivating conditioned generation that preserves multimodality. In this paper, we guide trajectory generation in the clean trajectory space and progressively incorporate trajectory priors with a timestep-dependent guidance strength. At each reverse diffusion step, the reconstructed clean trajectory provides a unified space for integrating planning costs and partial trajectory priors. Planning costs are incorporated through gradient-based refinement, while the partial prior is progressively injected at the corresponding noise levels with decreasing guidance strength. This guides generation toward the prior in early stages while gradually releasing the constraint to preserve the inherent multimodality of the diffusion model. The framework naturally extends to multi-robot planning by incorporating inter-robot collision costs. Experiments on single- and multi-robot planning tasks demonstrate controllable trajectory synthesis, diverse feasible solutions, and safe multi-agent coordination.

</details>


### [112] [Distilling Privileged Control Barrier Functions into RGB-Only Safety Filters for Dynamic Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36520)
*Seungyeon Yoo,Gawon Lee,Seungwoo Jung,Inkyu Jang,H. Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种教师-学生视觉蒸馏框架，将特权CBF教师的安全行为迁移到仅用RGB的学生安全滤波器，用于动态环境机器人导航避障，部署时无需3D重建或在线渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅RGB端到端视觉导航策略在真实动态环境中易发生碰撞，需要专门安全层；现有视觉CBF方法多依赖实时渲染或显式场景重建，且主要面向静态场景，难以机载部署。

Method: 采用教师-学生视觉蒸馏：教师在real-to-sim动态Gaussian Splatting环境中利用机器人与障碍物真值状态构建特权CBF；学生以短时RGB历史、机器人速度和名义控制动作为输入，直接输出安全动作。为缩小信息差距，教师仅基于学生RGB历史中可观测的障碍物构建安全约束，并建模障碍物速度不确定性以增强对运动变化的鲁棒性；同时通过动作增强让学生接触多样安全/不安全名义动作，更好学习安全边界。

Result: 实验表明，该方法优于视觉CBF基线，并在动态障碍物运动下提升基于RGB导航策略的安全性。

Conclusion: 该框架部署时仅需RGB观测和机器人速度，无需显式3D重建或在线渲染，为动态环境下RGB视觉导航提供了实用安全层。

Abstract: RGB-only end-to-end visual navigation policies remain vulnerable to collisions in real-world dynamic environments, motivating a dedicated safety layer. Existing visual Control Barrier Function (CBF) approaches seek to provide safety from RGB observations, but often rely on real-time rendering or explicit scene reconstruction and are primarily designed for static scenes, limiting their practicality for onboard deployment. We propose a teacher-student visual distillation framework that transfers the safety behavior of a privileged CBF teacher to an RGB-only student filter for dynamic environments. The student maps a short RGB history, robot velocity, and a nominal control action directly to a safe action, while the teacher uses ground-truth robot and obstacle states in a real-to-sim dynamic Gaussian Splatting environment. To reduce the teacher-student information gap, the teacher constructs safety constraints only from obstacles observable within the student's RGB history. It also accounts for obstacle-velocity uncertainty to improve robustness to motion variations, while action augmentation exposes the student to diverse safe and unsafe nominal actions to better capture the safety boundary. At deployment, the student requires only RGB observations and robot velocity, without explicit 3D reconstruction or online rendering. Experiments show that the proposed method outperforms visual CBF baselines and improves the safety of RGB-based navigation policies under dynamic obstacle motion. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [113] [LIBERO-MAX: Do Robot Policies Adapt When the World Changes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36518)
*Yunbei Zhang,Zijian Jin,Yuanzhe Liu,Janet Wang,Xilun Zhang,Yuyou Zhang,Zhenyu Zhang,Daoan Zhang,Shuaicheng Niu,Gen Li,Jianfei Yang,Jihun Hamm,Ismini Lourentzou,Weirui Ye,Bo Liu,Peter Stone,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 LIBERO-MAX 基准，用 8,000 个配对案例评估机器人在任务执行中途发生环境变化时的鲁棒性，发现 14 种当前策略成功率下降 11.0–25.7 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人常需在目标移动、视角变化或障碍出现后继续任务，但已有仿真鲁棒性基准多在重置时固定外部条件，忽视执行中途变化带来的时序挑战。

Method: 构建 LIBERO-MAX，覆盖几何、观测、外观、杂乱度和路径等八类变化，共 8,000 个配对案例；每对固定任务、初始状态、策略种子和事件前动作序列，对比有/无中途事件的任务执行；评估 14 种 VLA、混合及 world-action 策略，并分析相机控制与查询频率影响。

Result: 中途事件使成功率下降 11.0–25.7 个百分点；事件画像显示对几何和观测变化的共同脆弱性，而不同策略家族排名交错；相机控制表明鲁棒性既取决于变化条件下的能力，也取决于遭遇变化时所处轨迹，调整查询频率不能消除差距。

Conclusion: 配对协议与时序诊断使 LIBERO-MAX 成为可复现测试平台，用于诊断执行中途变化下的失败，并衡量机器人策略在世界变化时保持有效的进展。

Abstract: Robots must often continue a task after a target moves, the viewpoint shifts, or an obstacle appears, even though their earlier observations and committed actions reflect the previous scene. Many simulation robustness benchmarks fix external conditions at reset, leaving this temporal challenge underexamined. We introduce LIBERO-MAX, a benchmark of 8,000 paired cases spanning eight types of changes to geometry, observations, appearance, clutter, and paths. Each pair compares task execution with and without a mid-task event, holding the task, initial state, policy seed, and pre-event action sequence fixed. This controlled comparison distinguishes event-associated regressions from failures already present without the change. Across fourteen current VLA, hybrid, and world-action policies, events reduce success by 11.0-25.7 percentage points. Event profiles reveal shared vulnerabilities to geometry and observation changes, while policy-family rankings interleave. Camera controls show that robustness reflects both competence under the changed conditions and the trajectory from which they are encountered; varying query cadence does not eliminate the gap. Together, the paired protocol and temporal diagnostics establish LIBERO-MAX as a reproducible testbed for diagnosing failures under mid-execution changes and measuring progress toward robot policies that remain effective as the world changes.

</details>


### [114] [Closed-Form Cartesian Forward Kinetostatics for Spatial Multi-Segment Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36495)
*Ke Wu,Fangju Yang,Xiaohui Zhang,Junda He,Guanjun Bao,Jingang Yi,Jian S. Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向空间多段腱驱动连续机器人的力到笛卡尔构型正运动静力学模型，具有求积形式的闭式解，可避免逐致动输入的迭代平衡求解，并在高精度下显著提升计算速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腱驱动连续机器人的正运动静力学通常需要对每个致动输入进行非线性平衡求解，计算成本高，难以满足高效力到构型映射需求。

Method: 以笛卡尔骨干中心线和累积材料扭转作为广义坐标，推导应变度量与腱几何；通过变分平衡得到显式轴向与弯曲关系，并在所提模型的可容许纵向非螺旋布线条件下证明零平衡材料扭转。解按段传播，无需迭代平衡求解，同时保留轴向变形、空间变化的轴向与弯曲刚度、腱布线直径及分段腱参与。

Result: 与全应变几何变应变模型（GVS）对比，单段与三段机器人最大长度归一化末端位置偏差分别为 8.91×10^-6 和 1.01×10^-5；平均评估时间为 1.52 µs 和 2.94 µs，相对基线加速约 1864 倍和 3348 倍。

Conclusion: 该显式力到构型映射在保持高精度的同时消除了迭代平衡求解，为空间多段腱驱动连续机器人正运动静力学提供了显著的计算优势。

Abstract: Forward kinetostatics of spatial tendon-driven continuum robots typically requires a nonlinear equilibrium solve for each actuation input. This paper develops a force-to-Cartesian-configuration model with a closed-form solution in quadratures for spatial multi-segment robots under tendon actuation. The Cartesian backbone centerline and accumulated material twist serve as generalized coordinates, from which the strain measures and tendon geometry are derived. Variational equilibrium yields explicit axial and bending relations and establishes zero equilibrium material twist within the proposed model for admissible longitudinal non-helical routing. The solution is propagated segment by segment without an iterative equilibrium solve, while retaining axial deformation, spatially varying axial and bending stiffnesses and tendon-routing diameter, and segment-dependent tendon participation. Numerical comparisons with a full-strain geometric variable-strain model (GVS) yield maximum length-normalized tip-position discrepancies of 8.91 x 10^-6 and 1.01 x 10^-5 for the single- and three-segment robots, respectively. Mean evaluation times of 1.52 {\mu}s and 2.94 {\mu}s, with corresponding speedups of approximately 1864x and 3348x over the baseline, demonstrate the computational advantage of the explicit force-to-configuration mapping in the reported benchmark.

</details>


### [115] [DQ-MPCC: Dual-Quaternion MPCC for Quadrotor Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36482)
*Bryan S. Guevara,Luis F. Recalde,Guanrui Li,Tiago Nascimento*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于四旋翼竞速的对偶四元数模型预测轮廓控制（DQ-MPCC），将位姿建模为单位对偶四元数，并在对偶四元数流形切空间、期望体坐标系中表达轮廓误差，以统一位姿-旋量坐标实现与常规模型相同的刚体动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼竞速要求高速穿越每个门时进行激进的姿态与进度控制；传统MPCC的预测模型建立在惯性坐标系，姿态误差则在体坐标系中定义，存在坐标与误差表达不统一的问题。

Method: 将位姿表示为单位对偶四元数，把轮廓误差投影到对偶四元数流形的切空间并表达在期望体坐标系；采用体坐标系下的统一位姿-旋量坐标描述与常规模型相同的刚体动力学。通过蒙特卡洛软件在环仿真与11×4.5×3.65 m八门赛道实机竞速，将DQ-MPCC与基线MPCC对比，仿真和硬件使用相同增益。

Result: DQ-MPCC在完成飞行中每次过门均保持在规定几何裕度内，而基线超出；基线最差门偏移中位数从仿真到实机增长71.5%，DQ-MPCC则下降10.1%。在所有仿真过门均满足几何裕度的配置中，DQ-MPCC将最小单圈时间缩短6.7%（仿真）和10.5%（实机），并以100 Hz机载运行。

Conclusion: DQ-MPCC通过在统一对偶四元数位姿-旋量框架下进行轮廓控制，在保持过门几何裕度、缩小sim-to-real差距和提升竞速单圈时间方面优于传统MPCC，并满足机载实时性。

Abstract: Quadrotor racing demands aggressive attitude and progress control while passing through every gate, and conventional quadrotor MPCC formulations state the prediction model in inertial coordinates and the attitude error in the body frame. We present a Dual-Quaternion Model Predictive Contouring Control (DQ-MPCC) for quadrotor racing in which the pose is a unit dual quaternion and the contouring errors are projected onto the tangent space of the dual quaternion manifold, expressed in the desired body frame: the same rigid-body dynamics as the conventional model, in unified pose-twist coordinates in the body frame. We compare DQ-MPCC against a baseline MPCC through Monte Carlo software-in-the-loop simulations and real-world racing on an eight-gate circuit of 11x4.5x3.65 m. With the same gains in simulation and hardware, DQ-MPCC keeps every crossing of its completed flights within the prescribed geometric margin, whereas the baseline exceeds it, its median worst-gate offset growing by 71.5% sim-to-real against a 10.1% decrease for DQ-MPCC. Among the configurations that keep every simulated gate crossing within the geometric margin, DQ-MPCC also reduces the minimum lap time by 6.7%, and by 10.5% in the real-world flights, while running onboard at 100 Hz.

</details>


### [116] [Staircase Policy: Streaming Inference for World-Action Models with Large Action Chunks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36471)
*Guoheng Sun,Chen Chen,Jin Wang,Ang Li,Teresa Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 STAIRCASE POLICY（S-WAM），将 flow-matching VLA 转为 JEPA 风格 WAM，通过流式推理与交错去噪的子块划分，兼顾长时程操作精度与推理吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型（WAM）通过预测未来观测来条件化动作生成，可提升机器人操作性能，但未来预测会加剧原本昂贵的迭代动作生成开销；动作分块虽能摊销成本，却因长执行时程中后续动作仍依赖过时观测而导致性能下降。

Method: 将大动作块划分为处于不同去噪阶段的子块，近期动作一可用即执行，后续动作继续精炼；在每个子块边界，从最新观测重新预测未来潜变量，并更新所有未执行动作，从而无需重复完整策略推理即可支持长时程执行；未来预测误差还可作为自适应分块的信号。

Result: S-WAM 在 LIBERO 上达到 97.7%，在 LIBERO-Plus 上达到 87.9%，并在多种策略骨干和真实机器人任务上取得提升；执行吞吐达 292.7 动作/秒，在精度相当下约为传统执行的 3.62 倍，首动作时延从 123.6 ms 降至 73.3 ms；加入额外推理优化后吞吐进一步提升至 642.9 动作/秒。

Conclusion: 该框架有效缓解长时程执行中的观测陈旧问题，实现高精度、低延迟、高吞吐的机器人操作策略，并展现出跨策略骨干的泛化能力与基于预测误差的自适应分块潜力。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) improve robotic manipulation by conditioning action generation on predicted future observations, but future prediction adds further inference overhead to already expensive iterative action generation. Action chunking can amortize this cost over multiple actions, yet performance degrades over long execution horizons because later actions remain conditioned on stale observations. We introduce STAIRCASE POLICY, a streaming inference and training framework that turns a flow-matching VLA into a JEPA-style WAM and partitions a large action chunk into sub-chunks at staggered denoising stages. Near-term actions are executed as soon as they become available, while later actions continue to be refined. At each sub-chunk boundary, the future latent is re-predicted from the latest observation and used to update all unexecuted actions, enabling long-horizon execution without repeated full policy inference. The resulting future-prediction error can further serve as a signal for adaptive chunking. S-WAM achieves 97.7% on LIBERO and 87.9% on LIBERO-Plus, and improves performance across multiple policy backbones and real-robot tasks. It reaches 292.7 executed actions per second, $3.62\times$ the throughput of conventional execution at comparable accuracy, while reducing time-to-first-action from 123.6 to 73.3 ms. With additional inference optimizations, throughput further increases to 642.9 actions per second.

</details>


### [117] [World4Scorer: Outcome-Grounded World Modeling for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36438)
*Jieyuan Pei,Meiyi Lu,Sining Ang,Yubo Zhao,Zhangyi Hu,Mingwei Xu,Haokai Ding,Wei Li,Zihan You,Jianwei Zheng,Li Yu,Yifeng Pan,Ji Tao,Rongjunchen Zhang,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: World4Scorer 将生成-选择规划器的评分器建模为轨迹条件化的 JEPA 风格预测器，用仿真结果标签监督所有候选轨迹状态，并用真实执行轨迹的未来锚定共享表示，从而在 NAVSIM-v2、Bench2Drive 和 OGBench-Cube 上取得领先或验证有效的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需随交通演化选择安全高效方案。生成-选择规划器会提出多条轨迹并打分，但其评分器必须比较从未执行的候选计划。驾驶日志只记录已执行轨迹的未来，因此仅匹配日志未来会使替代候选的预测缺乏约束；而仿真器可为每个候选标注结果。

Method: 提出 World4Scorer：轨迹条件化的 JEPA 风格预测器为每个候选预测一个状态，并从该状态读取候选分数。仿真器结果标签监督所有候选状态，已执行轨迹的观测未来则锚定预测器以学习真实场景演化。由于同一预测器产生所有候选状态，该锚定可约束用于评分未执行计划的共享参数，且未来信息仅在训练时需要。针对生成候选多数得分较高的问题，使用场景匹配的候选库加入低分计划以增强结果监督；针对逐帧选择可能冲突的问题，采用惯性重排序保持连续选择一致。

Result: World4Scorer 在 NAVSIM-v2 上达到最先进性能，并在闭环 Bench2Drive 上取得较强的适配系统结果。在固定 LeWM 世界模型与规划预算时，基于结果的评分也提升了 OGBench-Cube 上的操作规划表现。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过轨迹条件化潜在预测读取评分，并联合仿真结果监督与真实执行未来锚定，可有效训练能比较未执行计划的评分器；辅以低分候选增强和惯性重排序，方法在自动驾驶与操作规划任务中均展现良好有效性和泛化性。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires choosing a safe and efficient plan as surrounding traffic evolves. Generate-and-select planners propose multiple trajectories and score them for execution, and they have outperformed representative direct-prediction baselines on NAVSIM. Their scorer must compare plans that were never executed. Driving logs record the future of only the executed trajectory, so matching the logged future can leave predictions for the alternatives unconstrained; a simulator, in contrast, can label the outcome of every candidate. We introduce World4Scorer, which builds the scorer as a trajectory-conditioned JEPA-style predictor: it predicts a state for each candidate and reads the candidate's scores from that state. Simulator outcome labels supervise the states of all candidates, and the observed future of the executed trajectory anchors the predictor to real scene evolution. Because one predictor produces every candidate's state, the anchor can constrain shared parameters used to score unexecuted plans, while the future itself is needed only during training. Generated candidates mostly score well, so a scene-matched bank adds low-scoring plans to the outcome supervision; framewise choices can conflict, so inertial re-ranking keeps consecutive selections consistent. World4Scorer achieves state-of-the-art NAVSIM-v2 performance and a strong adapted-system result on closed-loop Bench2Drive. With the LeWM world model and planning budget fixed, outcome-based scoring also improves manipulation planning on the OGBench-Cube benchmark.

</details>


### [118] [Losing the name before the box: measuring and repairing what narrow fine-tuning costs a detector outside its deployment vocabulary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36426)
*Trung Minh Bui,Jongsul Moon,YoungOuk Kim,Jung-Hoon Hwang,Dongin Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文揭示检测器在窄域微调后域内精度提升，但对预训练已覆盖、词表未命名目标的开放世界覆盖会显著下降，且该退化无法由域内指标或检测AP反映；通过回混少量预训练权重可低成本恢复覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在障碍物检测与巡检等场景中，词表未命名的目标仍可能构成风险；然而窄域微调通常只关注域内精度提升，且域内测试集不含这类目标，因此需要研究微调对开放世界或未命名类别覆盖的隐性损害。

Method: 提出纵向协议：将同一预训练检查点与其微调后代对比，跟踪留出的top-K候选框覆盖率C_tau，即在预训练覆盖但词表省略的类别中，检测器top-K区域仍覆盖的框比例；在4种架构、3个域上评估，并对比检测AP、冻结阶梯，以及无训练修复（回混四分之一预训练状态，含归一化统计量）。

Result: C_tau随域内精度上升而下降，在大于1024 px^2的框上降幅为5.12至63.35个百分点；域内指标和检测AP均无法识别该下降。在一个同时报告AP的架构上，自适应损失87%的AP却只损失约五分之一覆盖率，且命名能力在各冻结深度均最先退化。退化具有结构性：无共同预训练的三架构对哪些类别失去覆盖一致，未学过的类别不下降。回混四分之一预训练状态可在所有扫描单元提升覆盖，域内精度最多损失2.47个百分点，仅需额外一次评估。

Conclusion: 窄域微调会系统性削弱对未命名但预训练已知类别的覆盖，这是标准域内评估和AP掩盖的风险；简单的预训练权重回混无需训练即可在很小精度代价下恢复开放世界覆盖，并建议将此类纵向覆盖评估纳入部署流程。

Abstract: A detector pretrained on a broad corpus is fine-tuned on a narrow domain, its in-domain accuracy improves, and it ships. We ask what happens meanwhile to its coverage of objects the vocabulary never names, which in obstacle detection and inspection carry the risk. No in-domain test set holds an example of one. We give a longitudinal protocol: one pretrained checkpoint against its own fine-tuned descendants. It tracks held-out top-$K$ proposal coverage $C_\tau$: of categories pretraining covered and the vocabulary omits, the share of boxes a detector's top $K$ regions still cover. The quantity is the open-world proposal literature's; the longitudinal reading is not. $C_\tau$ falls while in-domain accuracy rises, on four architectures and three domains, by $5.12$ to $63.35$ points on boxes above $1024$ px$^2$. No in-domain number identifies the fall, and neither does detection average precision, which charges a missed and a misnamed box alike. On the one architecture scoring both, adaptation costs $87\%$ of the AP against a fifth of the coverage, and the naming goes first at all six depths of its freeze ladder, every run. What breaks is structured: three architectures sharing no pretraining run agree on which categories lose coverage, and those a model never learned do not lose any. A repair follows and needs no training: mixing a quarter of the pretrained state back, normalisation statistics included, raises coverage on every cell swept for at most $2.47$ points of in-domain accuracy. Seeing it costs one extra evaluation pass.

</details>


### [119] [FineART: Fine-grained Annotated Robotic Trajectory Dataset and Vision-Language-Action Model for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36416)
*Jade Choghari,Pepijn Kooijmans,Mansi Agarwal,Yusuf Umut Ciftci,Aseem Doriwala,Catherine Weaver,Mouli Sivapurapu,Kai Yang,Jackson Lee,Thomas Wolf,Pragna Mannam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出密集标注的双臂操作数据集 FineART 及可预测下一子任务的视觉-语言-动作策略 FineART-VLA，通过子任务预测中训练显著提升长时程双臂操作的成功率、数据效率与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境中的机器人需要执行长时程、多步骤的双臂任务，而非孤立单步动作；现有操作数据集难以支撑该能力：单臂数据集虽达数十万条轨迹，但通常每个 episode 仅有一个高层指令，少数双臂数据集即便标注子任务也只覆盖部分时长。

Method: 构建 FineART：包含 40,543 个 episode、1,718 小时、533,913 个子任务，覆盖 151 个任务的密集标注双臂操作数据集。提出 FineART-VLA：一种视觉-语言-动作策略，能够预测自身的下一子任务，并采用该方式进行中训练（mid-training）。

Result: 空间消歧任务成功率从 32.0% 提升至 100.0%；在未见长时程任务中，逐步人类子任务引导将成功率从 16.0% 提升至 76.0%；在新机器人上仅需少量微调，所需数据为未中训练基线的十分之一，并可零样本泛化到新硬件上完全未见的任务。

Conclusion: 密集子任务标注与下一子任务预测式中训练能有效增强双臂长时程操作性能，并带来更高的数据效率与跨机器人、跨任务泛化能力；数据集、模型权重和训练代码均已开源。

Abstract: Robots operating in real-world environments must execute complex, multi-step bimanual tasks over long horizons rather than single, isolated actions. Current manipulation datasets struggle to support this capability: although single-arm datasets reach hundreds of thousands of trajectories, they typically provide only one high-level instruction per episode while the rare bimanual effort that does label subtasks annotates only a fraction of its hours. We present FineART, a densely annotated bimanual manipulation dataset of 40,543 episodes, 1,718 hours, and 533,913 subtasks across 151 tasks. We also introduce FineART-VLA, a vision-language-action policy that predicts its own next subtask, and show that mid-training it this way yields substantial gains. Specifically, success on a spatial disambiguation task increases from 32.0% to 100.0%, and step-by-step human subtask guidance lifts success on an unseen long-horizon task from 16.0% to 76.0%. Furthermore, after minimal fine-tuning on a new robot, the policy requires one-tenth the data of baselines without mid-training and generalizes zero-shot to completely unseen tasks on the new hardware. We open-source the full dataset, model weights, and training code.

</details>


### [120] [One from Infinity: Actualizing Futures from Pretrained World Models into Robot Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36413)
*Bang Du,Yichen Xie,Shuqi Zhao,Yuxin Chen,Menglin Wu,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboActualizer：冻结预训练视频世界模型，仅训练约60M参数的小型 actualizer，学习任务条件下的未来选择与动作读出，在多个仿真和真实机器人任务上以低延迟实现实时控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视频世界模型已包含多样潜在未来，但机器人需要实现任务条件下的确切未来。现有方法通常需微调庞大的世界模型骨干，并依赖大规模机器人数据和算力；本文认为昂贵的表示学习已在预训练完成，真正剩余的是选择可达成的未来并读出相应动作。

Method: 将问题形式化为 actualization：在冻结世界模型提供的先验上，学习任务条件的选择与实现。实现 RoboActualizer，基于冻结世界模型编码器，仅约60M可训练参数，由两个轻量 DiT 专家组成，通过 flow matching 联合预测未来 latent 与动作；训练仅需单张32GB GPU。

Result: 可训练参数比现有 WAMs 和 VLAs 少至100倍，在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin 2.0 等仿真基准以及两个真实平台上的五个任务中取得优异性能，推理延迟低至39 ms，支持实时控制。

Conclusion: 无需微调重型世界模型，冻结其表示并训练轻量 actualizer 即可高效地将世界模型转化为可执行机器人策略，显著降低训练成本与参数量，同时保持高性能和实时性。

Abstract: A pretrained video world model admits many plausible futures for a scene, but a robot must realize the exact task-conditioned one. To turn world models into executable robot policies, existing methods fine-tune the heavy world model backbone using large-scale robot data and computational resources. Challenging this status quo, we argue that the expensive part has already been paid in the world model pretraining since the representation space of a video world model lays out the diverse potential futures. In this case, what remains is to select the future that accomplishes the task and to read out the actions that realize it. We formalize this task as actualization, which learns a task-conditioned selection and realization on top of a prior supplied by a frozen world model. This can be solved by a tiny actualizer model. We implement RoboActualizer with as few as 60M parameters on top of a frozen world model encoder. The actualizer is composed of two lightweight DiT experts that jointly predict future latents and actions by flow matching. The model can be trained entirely on a single GPU with 32 GB peak memory. With up to 100x fewer trainable parameters than existing WAMs and VLAs, RoboActualizer reaches great performance on simulation benchmarks including LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, RoboTwin 2.0 and five tasks on two real-world platforms, with a low latency of 39 ms that allows real-time control.

</details>


### [121] [DORA: Divergence-Oriented Data-Relay Algorithm for Partially Connected Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36395)
*Jonathan Diller,Fernando Cladera,Camillo Jose Taylor,Vijay Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究部分连接无人机集群在搜索与监测中的探索-中继权衡，形式化为异构任务感知覆盖（HMAC）问题，并提出面向信息价值的DORA数据中继算法，以降低任务相关目标（MRT）的解析延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机团队在搜索与监测任务中常处于部分连接网络，机器人需在探索环境与向队友中继信息之间权衡；当机器人语义异构时，某机器人认为无用的观测可能对具备互补检测能力的队友至关重要。

Method: 作者将问题形式化为HMAC：在间歇通信下，耦合多机器人对区域的完整覆盖与能力约束下的任务相关目标发现；提出DORA，一种以团队信息价值而非仅发现事件驱动通信的分歧导向数据中继算法，通过量化机器人当前信息状态与对每个队友知识估计之间的任务相关分歧，综合任务相关性、发现新颖性、传感器不确定性和信息年龄。

Result: 在四种不同物体密度与空间结构的仿真环境中评估，并在实体无人机平台上验证；结果显示，相比传统基于时间的通信调度方法，DORA将MRT解析延迟最多改善74.8%。

Conclusion: DORA通过面向团队信息价值的中继策略，能有效缓解异构无人机团队在间歇通信下的探索-中继权衡，显著提升任务相关目标的解析效率。

Abstract: Teams of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) deployed for search and monitoring missions frequently operate as partially connected networks, forcing each robot to trade off exploring the environment against relaying information to teammates. This tradeoff is especially acute when robots are semantically heterogeneous: an observation that appears uninformative to the robot that made it may be critical to a teammate with complementary detection capabilities. In this work, we formalize this setting as the heterogeneous mission-aware coverage (HMAC) problem, which couples complete multi-robot coverage of an area with capability-constrained mission-relevant target (MRT) discovery under intermittent communication. We then present DORA, a divergence-oriented data-relay algorithm that drives communication by the value of information to the team rather than by discovery alone. DORA quantifies the mission-relevant divergence between a robot's current information state and its estimate of each teammate's knowledge, capturing mission relevance, discovery novelty, sensor uncertainty, and the age of information. We evaluate DORA in simulation across four environments with differing object densities and spatial structure, and validate it on a physical UAV platform. Our results show that DORA improves MRT resolution delay by up to 74.8% over traditional time-based communication scheduling methods.

</details>


### [122] [ATLAS: Aligned Transport of Latent Structure for Reliable World Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36333)
*Ke Fang,Yupu Yao,Lu Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 ATLAS，通过从信息量丰富的编码器表征向规划潜在迁移归一化成对结构，并用 Wasserstein 嵌入匹配校准潜在边缘分布，从而在潜在世界模型中保留对规划重要的关系几何，提升目标达成成功率与规划稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型依赖表征几何进行规划，但仅正则化潜在边缘分布无法决定动作选择所需的状态间关系。这可能导致在表征被变换为规划器最终使用的潜变量时，与规划相关的新颖性结构被削弱。

Method: 提出 Aligned Transport of Latent Structure（ATLAS）训练目标：将来自信息量丰富编码器表征的归一化成对结构迁移到规划潜在中，以显式保留关系几何；同时使用 Wasserstein 嵌入匹配（WEMReg），通过一维 Wasserstein-2 传输校准规划潜变量的全局边缘分布。分析表明，关系保持与边缘校准提供非冗余约束，并将有限候选规划稳定性与关系失真、潜在尺度失配和预测误差联系起来。

Result: 在 LeWM 中实例化 ATLAS 后，在 PushT、TwoRoom 和 OGBench-Cube 的低新颖性与高新颖性评估子集上均提升了平均目标达成成功率，其中在高新颖性 TwoRoom 回合中增益最大。表征与 rollout 诊断进一步显示，规划潜在中与新颖性相关的结构更强、边缘校准更好、多步预测误差更低。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，保留对规划相关的潜在几何是可靠世界模型规划的重要要素。

Abstract: Latent world models rely on representation geometry for planning, yet regularizing the latent marginal alone does not determine the state-to-state relationships used for action selection. We show that this can cause planning-relevant novelty structure to be weakened as representations are transformed into the final latent used by the planner. We introduce Aligned Transport of Latent Structure (ATLAS), a training objective that explicitly preserves relational geometry while calibrating the global latent distribution. ATLAS transfers normalized pairwise structure from an informative encoder representation to the planning latent and uses Wasserstein embedding matching (WEMReg) to calibrate its marginal through one-dimensional Wasserstein-2 transport. Our analysis shows that relational preservation and marginal calibration impose non-redundant constraints, and connects finite-candidate planning stability to relational distortion, latent-scale mismatch, and prediction error. Instantiated in LeWM, ATLAS improves mean goal-reaching success across PushT, TwoRoom, and OGBench-Cube on both lower- and higher-novelty evaluation subsets, with the largest gain on higher-novelty TwoRoom episodes. Representation and rollout diagnostics further show stronger novelty-related structure in the planning latent, improved marginal calibration, and lower multi-step prediction error. Together, these results highlight preservation of planning-relevant latent geometry as an important ingredient for reliable world-model planning. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [123] [Bilinear World Models: Learning Representations with Structured Dynamics for Efficient Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36305)
*Antonio Pariente,Ignacio Boero,Nikolai Matni,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种 JEPA 风格世界模型，将潜在动力学限制为双线性参数化，以结构化方式实现高效规划与控制、保证动作可恢复性并防止表示坍塌；理论上广泛非线性系统可变换为双线性，实验中将规划时间降低近三个数量级并支持长时程与实时控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型需联合学习潜在表示与动力学，以预测高维观测在动作下如何演化。任意潜在动力学可能导致规划低效与表示坍塌。作者希望用双线性结构把建模负担转移到编码器，促使表示暴露系统可控几何，并通过结构约束保证动作可恢复性。虽然双线性看似受限，但广泛非线性动力学系统存在变换使其变为双线性。

Method: 提出 JEPA 风格世界模型，不学习任意潜在动力学，而是强制其遵循双线性参数化。该结构支持高效规划与控制，并可结构化地强制动作可恢复性，从而按构造防止表示坍塌。理论上展示广泛非线性动力学系统可通过变换化为双线性形式。编码器从高维观测直接学习表示，双线性动力学在潜在空间中建模。

Result: 在标准 2D 与 3D 控制任务中，可直接从高维观测学习具有双线性参数化动力学的表示；规划时间减少近三个数量级，同时保持甚至提升控制精度。在更长时程规划与实时控制等更 demanding 设定下，方法均成功。

Conclusion: 双线性参数化的 JEPA 风格世界模型可同时提升表示质量与规划效率，并将 JEPA 风格世界模型从短时程离线规划推进到长时程规划与实时控制；结构约束并非过度限制，而是有效利用可控系统几何与动作可恢复性的归纳偏置。

Abstract: World models jointly learn latent representations and dynamics that predict how high-dimensional observations evolve under actions. In this work, we propose a JEPA-style world model in which, rather than learning arbitrary latent dynamics, we restrict them to follow a bilinear parameterization. This structure enables efficient planning and control while shifting the modeling burden onto the encoder, encouraging richer representations that expose the controllable geometry of the system. In particular, this structured parameterization allows us to structurally enforce action recoverability, thereby preventing representation collapse by construction. Although prescribing a bilinear parametrization may appear restrictive, we show that a broad class of nonlinear dynamical systems admits a transformation under which the dynamics become bilinear. Empirically, we show across standard 2D and 3D control tasks that representations with bilinear-parameterized dynamics can be learned directly from high-dimensional observations, reducing planning time by nearly three orders of magnitude while retaining or even improving control accuracy. We also propose more demanding regimes of longer-horizon planning and real-time control, and demonstrate that our method succeeds in both, moving JEPA-style world models beyond short-horizon offline planning.

</details>


### [124] [Design and Validation of an Antagonistic Tendon-Driven Dexterous Robotic Hand with Bidirectional Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36241)
*Chunghyeon Lee,Hyukjun Kwon,Sungeon Kim,Saehyun Moon,Seokhwan Jeong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于双向拮抗腱驱动机构的拟人化17自由度灵巧手，可在掌侧和背侧双向闭合抓取，并在紧凑人体尺度平台上验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拟人灵巧手虽复现人手形态，但继承单侧抓取工作空间，需通过腕或臂重定向才能从对侧抓取；现有可反转手多依赖非拟人、软体或任务特定结构，而五指拟人手仍主要面向掌侧抓取。

Method: 设计了一只人体尺度（长200 mm）、轻量（220 g）、3D打印的17自由度机械手，采用双向拮抗腱路由机制；除耦合关节外，各关节的屈曲/伸展和收展均由主动驱动，无需被动回位弹簧。该路由使所有自由度可越过中性位，从而在掌侧或背侧形成抓取闭合。

Result: 实验测得平均电机-关节传动误差3.0%，平均关节传动带宽13.2 Hz，最大指尖力29 N，定位重复性达0.15 mm；Kapandji评分为8，可完成GRASP Taxonomy全部33种抓取类型，并实现掌侧与背侧抓取任务。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该紧凑人体尺度平台能够实现双向操作，为拟人灵巧手扩展双侧抓取工作空间提供可行方案。

Abstract: Dexterous robotic hands typically reproduce human hand morphology but inherit its one-sided grasping workspace, requiring wrist or arm reorientation to grasp from the opposite side. Existing reversible hands generally rely on non-anthropomorphic, soft, or task-specific finger arrangements, whereas conventional five-digit anthropomorphic hands remain designed primarily for palmar-side grasping. This paper presents an anthropomorphic, human-scale (200 mm length), lightweight (220 g), 3D-printed, 17-DoF robotic hand built on a bidirectional antagonistic tendon-routing mechanism, in which flexion/extension (except the coupled joint) and abduction/adduction at joints are actively driven without passive return springs. The proposed routing mechanism allows all degrees of freedom to cross their neutral configuration and form grasp closures on either the palmar or dorsal side. Experimental evaluation demonstrates an average motor-to-joint transmission error of 3.0%, an average joint transmission bandwidth of 13.2 Hz, a maximum fingertip force of 29 N, and a positioning repeatability up to 0.15 mm. The hand further achieves a Kapandji score of 8, successfully performs all 33 GRASP Taxonomy grasp types, and performs palmar- and dorsal-side grasping tasks, validating bidirectional operation in a compact, human-scale platform.

</details>


### [125] [Test-Time Adaptation of Manipulation Policies Under Actuator Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36182)
*Som Sagar,Ransalu Senanayake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TeAR：一种策略无关的动作校正方法，利用实时执行器遥测将冻结的机器人操作策略变为遥测条件化策略，以应对电机发热、电流饱和和电压跌落等硬件退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作策略通常假设同一动作在训练后始终产生相同运动，但真实硬件会因电机逐渐发热、接触附近电流饱和、负载下电压下降而产生更弱、延迟或噪声更大的运动。关节温度、电机电流、供电电压等遥测信号本已可测，却通常仅用于日志或安全检查，未用于策略自适应。

Method: TeAR在动作到达底层控制器前进行校正，不修改原始策略。它学习一个轻量Transformer，将策略提出的动作与实时执行器遥测结合，对动作的各个分量进行放大、阻尼或偏置，从而生成遥测条件化动作。

Result: 在18个策略-任务对、8个策略家族和5个操作任务上评估。在存在退化模型不匹配的额外配对评估中，TeAR成功率为31.8%，基线策略为25.6%，假定模型逆校正为30.6%。在物理机械臂上，TeAR在发热条件下将成功率提高10-15%，且无需在机器人上微调。

Conclusion: 利用现有遥测信号进行动作校正可提升操作策略对真实硬件退化的鲁棒性，且方法即插即用、策略无关，无需重新训练策略或进行真机微调。

Abstract: Robot manipulation policies are usually trained under the assumption that a commanded action produces the same motion as it did during training even after hours of operation. Real hardware violates this assumption as the motors gradually heat up, current saturates near contact, voltage sags under load, thus the same policy action can produce a weaker, delayed, or noisier motion. These conditions are already measured by onboard telemetry, such as joint temperature, motor current, and supply voltage, yet this signal is typically used only for logging or safety checks rather than policy adaptation. We introduce Telemetry-Aware Action Rectification (TeAR), a policy-agnostic method that turns a frozen manipulation policy into a telemetry-conditioned policy by rectifying its outgoing action before it reaches the low-level controller. TeAR learns a lightweight Transformer that combines the proposed action with live actuator telemetry and amplifies, damps, or biases individual action components. We evaluate TeAR across 18 policy-task pairs spanning 8 policy families and 5 manipulation tasks. In an additional paired evaluation with degradation-model mismatch, TeAR achieves 31.8% success, compared with 25.6% for the base policy and 30.6% for an assumed-model inverse. On a physical arm, TeAR improves success under heating by 10-15% without on-robot fine-tuning.

</details>


### [126] [SkillWeaver: Agentic Exploration over Neural Interaction Skills for Scalable Robot Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36171)
*He Zhu,Lusen Zhao,Kwan Man Cheng,Su Li,Katerina Fragkiadaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SkillWeaver，一种基于神经交互技能（NIS）的智能体框架，可自主探索并生成机器人经验，以替代昂贵且难扩展的遥操作数据采集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习依赖大规模演示，但遥操作数据成本高、难以扩展到多样环境和长时程任务；仿真虽可扩展，现有数据生成流程多依赖开环控制器、脚本化技能序列或任务专用程序。

Method: SkillWeaver 让 VLM 智能体在仿真中推理下一步，调用并参数化可复用、参数化、闭环的 NIS；NIS 被实例化为强化学习策略，用于闭环、接触丰富操作。探索组织为验证器引导的树搜索，并生成验证、反思与记忆来指导后续探索，无需预设执行流程。

Result: 自主扩展至 14.1K 场景中的 39.1K 条演示，并蒸馏为视觉运动策略。在仿真基准和真实机器人操作中，使用 SkillWeaver 生成经验训练显著提升对新颖物体、空间配置、任务和环境的泛化，并支持零样本/少样本 sim-to-sim 与 sim-to-real 迁移。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于神经交互技能的智能体探索是一种可扩展的机器人数据生成替代方案。

Abstract: Large-scale demonstrations have driven unprecedented progress in robot learning, yet collecting robot data through teleoperation is expensive and difficult to scale to diverse environments and long-horizon tasks. Simulation offers a scalable alternative, but existing data-generation pipelines often rely on open-loop controllers, scripted skill sequences, or task-specific programs. We introduce SkillWeaver, an agentic framework that autonomously generates robot experience by exploring over Neural Interaction Skills (NIS): reusable, parameterized, closed-loop policies that expose learned physical interaction capabilities to a reasoning agent. Given a task and a simulated environment, a VLM agent reasons about what to do next, invokes and parameterizes NIS to interact with the environment, observes their outcomes, and generates verification, reflection, and memory to guide subsequent exploration. We instantiate NIS as reinforcement-learned policies for closed-loop, contact-rich manipulation and organize exploration as verifier-guided tree search, enabling the agent to discover successful long-horizon behaviors without relying on predetermined execution pipelines. SkillWeaver scales autonomously to 39.1K demonstrations across 14.1K scenes, which we distill into visuomotor policies. Across simulation benchmarks and real-world manipulation, training on SkillWeaver-generated experience substantially improves generalization to novel objects, spatial configurations, tasks, and environments, and enables zero- and few-shot sim-to-sim and sim-to-real transfer. Our results suggest agentic exploration over neural interaction skills as a scalable alternative for robot data generation.

</details>


### [127] [KPI: A Promptable Kernel for Physical Interaction on Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36151)
*Yikai Wang,Honghao Zhu,Xiao Hu,Hao Zhang,Zelin Wang,Yip Fun Yeung,Ding Zhao,Lingfeng Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KPI 是一种可提示内核，位于轨迹源与未修改的全身跟踪器之间：轨迹源为每个方向发送“跟踪、顺应或保持力范围”的契约，KPI 根据跟踪误差与力/力矩估计在线调节手臂刚度、阻尼、参考和前馈，从而在接触时产生所需物理交互。配合视觉-语言代理，可从单条指令生成参考轨迹与契约，实现绞盘操作、开门、搬箱等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用人形全身跟踪策略虽能实现行走、平衡与到达，但其泛化性主要体现在轨迹中，而轨迹本身无法充分指定接触时应产生的交互；单任务策略通常在仿真中联合优化轨迹与控制器，通用栈则常依赖预设或手工选择的控制器。

Method: 提出 KPI：一个插入轨迹源与未修改 whole-body tracker 之间的可提示内核。轨迹源发送按方向定义的契约（跟踪、顺应或保持力范围）；KPI 利用跟踪误差和 wrench 估计，以接触频率在线调节双臂的刚度、阻尼、参考轨迹与前馈，使执行控制器趋向契约要求。进一步构建 agentic framework：由视觉-语言代理根据一条指令同时编写参考轨迹和契约，无需任务专用代码。

Result: 在指令驱动下演示了绞盘操作、开门和箱子运输，并进行了脚本化的表面交互实验；其中绞盘演示中，人形机器人能够转动曲柄，将第二个机器人完全吊离地面。

Conclusion: KPI 使通用人形全身跟踪器具备可提示、面向接触的物理交互能力，可由视觉-语言代理从自然语言指令端到端部署，无需任务专用代码，并在多种真实接触任务中验证了有效性。

Abstract: Humanoids now walk, balance and reach with remarkable generality: one whole-body tracking policy follows references from a human, or from an end-to-end policy. That generality travels in the trajectory, and a trajectory alone carries limited information about the interaction it should produce: at contact, the executing controller determines how the robot behaves. Single-task policies usually reach hard interactions by optimising trajectory and controller together in simulation; general stacks usually assume a preset or hand-chosen controller. We present KPI, a promptable kernel for physical interaction between the trajectory source and an unmodified whole-body tracker. Instead of a controller fixed before the task, the trajectory source sends a contract: per direction, track, comply, or hold a force range. From tracking error and a wrench estimate, the kernel adapts the arms' stiffness, damping, reference and feedforward toward it at contact rate. We demonstrate KPI through an agentic framework: from one instruction, a vision-language agent writes both the reference trajectory and the contract, with no task-specific code. We demonstrate instruction-driven winch operation, door opening, and box transport, alongside scripted surface-interaction experiments. In the winch demonstration, the humanoid is able to turn a crank to hoist a second robot fully off the ground.

</details>


### [128] [Scouting the Dynamics Gap: Test-Time Policy Adaptation via Action-Outcome Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36107)
*Yishu Li,Liyuan Geng,Xinyi Mao,Amber Li,David Held*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCOUT是一种动力学感知的元学习框架，通过共享信念隐空间耦合动作预测策略与前向动力学模型，利用动作-结果误差在线更新隐信念，使操作策略快速适应未知物理动力学，并在仿真基准上加速在线适应、实现鲁棒的sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人策略在受控环境中表现优异，但部署时存在未观测的物理属性和动力学，要求策略快速适应；现有测试时适应方法通常依赖稀疏标量奖励，未能充分利用物理交互中丰富的几何与动力学反馈。

Method: 提出SCOUT框架：将动作预测策略与前向动力学模型通过共享信念隐空间耦合。元训练时，内循环通过最小化对观测动作结果的动力学预测误差来更新共享信念隐变量，外循环优化动作选择网络；部署时，智能体依据动作-结果不匹配更新隐信念，在线推断并适应未知物理动力学，且避免灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在 simulated manipulation benchmarks 上显著加速在线适应，并在真实世界中实现鲁棒的 sim-to-real transfer。

Conclusion: SCOUT表明，将策略与动力学预测通过共享隐信念耦合，并基于动作-结果误差进行测试时适应，能够有效提升机器人操作策略对未知动力学的快速在线适应能力与真实世界泛化性。

Abstract: While pretrained robotic policies exhibit impressive capabilities in controlled environments, unobserved physical properties and dynamics require these policies to rapidly adapt during deployment. Existing test-time adaptation methods typically rely on sparse scalar rewards, failing to exploit the rich geometric and dynamic feedback from the environment during physical interaction. To address this challenge, we propose SCOUT, a dynamics-aware meta-learning framework that enables manipulation policies to rapidly adapt by continuously revising their internal beliefs about environment dynamics. Our approach couples an action-prediction policy with a forward dynamics model via a shared belief latent space. During meta-training, an inner loop updates this shared belief latent by minimizing the dynamics prediction error against the observed action outcome, while the outer loop optimizes the network for action selection. At deployment, this structure allows the agent to infer and adapt to unknown physical dynamics on the fly. By updating its latent belief based on action-outcome mismatches, the policy automatically adapts without risking catastrophic forgetting. We demonstrate that SCOUT significantly accelerates online adaptation across simulated manipulation benchmarks and achieves robust sim-to-real transfer in the real world. Project webiste can be found here: this https URL

</details>


### [129] [MagNav: A Dual-Core Magnetic Track Guidance Framework for Lighting-Invariant Navigation in Two-Wheeled Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36091)
*Arpita Kumari,Anupam Chatterjee,Richa Srivastava*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于五通道模拟霍尔传感器阵列与串级PID的磁轨道导引自平衡两轮倒立摆机器人，实现不受光照影响的自主循线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两轮倒立摆机器人转向与平衡强耦合，现有红外/光学循线方案易受阳光或阴影等光照变化干扰，通常仅适用于室内，因此需要一种光照无关且可靠的循线导航方案。

Method: 采用磁轨道导引系统，使用五通道模拟霍尔效应传感器阵列感知路径；控制上采用串级PID：内环基于IMU与互补滤波器维持平衡，外环根据磁传感器读数调节转向；使用步进电机实现精确力矩控制且无需额外旋转编码器；并加入光学传感器模块作为对比。

Result: 测试表明，磁导引系统在超过10,000 Lux的强光下仍能准确跟踪，而光学系统会丧失精度并有时失效。

Conclusion: 该设计为工厂、仓库和室外路径等场景提供了一种可靠且不受光照影响的自主导航解决方案。

Abstract: Two-Wheeled Inverted Pendulum (TWIP) robots are useful for studying how to control systems that are naturally unstable and have fewer actuators than degrees of freedom. Adding autonomous line-following to these robots is challenging because steering and balancing are closely linked. Most existing systems use infrared sensors, which can be affected by changes in lighting, such as sunlight or shadows, making them reliable only indoors. This paper presents a self-balancing robot that can follow a line using a magnetic track guidance system. By using a five-channel analog Hall-effect sensor array, the robot is not affected by optical interference. The control system uses a cascaded PID structure: the inner loop keeps the robot balanced using data from an inertial measurement unit with a complementary filter, while the outer loop adjusts steering based on the magnetic sensor readings. Stepper motors provide precise torque control without needing extra rotary encoders. For comparison, an optical sensor module was also included. Tests show that the magnetic guidance system keeps accurate tracking even in very bright lighting, over 10,000 Lux, while the optical system loses accuracy and sometimes fails. This design provides a reliable, lighting-independent solution for autonomous navigation in places like factories, warehouses, and outdoor paths.

</details>


### [130] [SAKI: Skill Assembly and Kinematic Imitation from Human Videos for Long-Horizon Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36031)
*Yijie Lu,James Zhao,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAKI 是一个连接人类视频技能获取、跨演示技能组装与闭环全身执行的框架，用于长时程移动操作中的可复用物体中心技能学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频提供了获取多样操作技能的途径，但将其从桌面场景扩展到长时程移动操作，需要适应变化场景与机器人构型，并组合独立演示的交互。

Method: SAKI 准备可复用的物体中心技能，保留任务关键交互并允许迁移路径自适应；根据目标和任务依赖选择、排序技能，把物体角色绑定到当前场景，并在技能间传递场景估计与机器人配置；通过全身运动学模仿生成基座、机械臂和夹爪的协调运动；执行中利用持久物体估计维持跨视角任务参考，并用视觉反馈更新剩余轨迹。

Result: 真实机器人实验表明，技能可跨布局复用，并能将独立演示的交互组合为连续移动任务，如整理和擦拭。消融实验显示，在全身优化与视觉反馈固定时，任务条件参考准备显著提升长时程任务完成率。

Conclusion: SAKI 将人类视频技能获取、跨演示组装和闭环全身执行统一起来，是实现长时程移动操作中技能复用与组合的有效框架。

Abstract: Learning from human videos offers a promising route to acquiring diverse manipulation skills. Extending this capability beyond tabletop settings to long-horizon mobile manipulation requires adapting and composing demonstrated interactions across changing scenes and robot configurations. We present Skill Assembly and Kinematic Imitation (SAKI), a framework connecting human-video skill acquisition, cross-demonstration assembly and closed-loop whole-body execution. SAKI prepares reusable object-centric skills that preserve task-critical interactions while allowing transfer paths to adapt. Given a goal and supplied task dependencies, it selects and orders skills, binds their object roles to the current scene, and carries scene estimates and robot configuration between successive skills. Whole-body kinematic imitation generates coordinated base, arm and gripper motion. During execution, persistent object estimates maintain task references across viewpoint changes, while visual feedback updates remaining trajectories. Real-robot experiments demonstrate skill reuse across layouts and the composition of independently demonstrated interactions into continuous mobile tasks, including tidying and wiping. Ablation results show that task-conditioned reference preparation substantially improves long-horizon task completion with whole-body optimisation and visual feedback held fixed. Check this https URL for video demos!

</details>


### [131] [In-Context Learning for Robots: Methods and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36012)
*Haojian Huang,Zexi Li,Junhao Guo,Yehang Zhang,Wenxuan Peng,Bohan Zhou,Weilin Ruan,Leyi Wu,Chenxu Wang,Jianchong Su,Binghui Xie,Wosong Chen,Yingjie Xu,Tianhao Zhou,Suzeyu Chen,Pukun Zhao,Jiaqi He,Xinyi Li,Runze Li,Peiran Dong,Shaoxiang Dang,Jing Huang,Yingbing Chen,Yifan Chang,Tianyi Zhang,Shiyuan Deng,Haozhi Wang,Yangkai Wei,Wenqian Li,Han Yang,Kaiwen Zhou,Huaping Liu,James Cheng,Rui Shao,Donglin Wang,Yaochu Jin,Jianye Hao,Ying-Cong Chen,Yinchuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述围绕机器人上下文学习（ICL），按“上下文证据到执行”的接口将文献分为四类，并梳理其迁移假设、评估实践及通向物理递归自我改进的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人必须推断新任务要求并转化为合适的物理动作；机器人ICL通过演示与交互引导已有能力，且部署时保持神经参数固定，因此需要系统理解上下文证据如何连接到执行，以及如何评估其迁移效果。

Method: 以连接上下文证据与执行的接口为主线，区分四类方法：上下文条件策略、几何演示迁移、基于世界模型的控制、技能与智能体执行；比较其迁移假设及训练、对应关系和记忆的作用，并在操作与导航任务中考察物体、环境和执行条件变化时如何保持所教要求。

Result: 分析将方法设计同评估实践联系起来，区分对教学的响应性、物理迁移能力以及从保留经验中获益；据此提出研究议程，将组合式任务获取、忠实迁移与物理递归自我改进相连接。

Conclusion: 机器人ICL需在对象、环境和执行条件变化下保持教学意图并实现可靠物理迁移；未来应让积累的经验提升后续任务学习能力，推动组合式任务获取、忠实迁移与物理递归自我改进的统一。

Abstract: General-purpose robots must infer what a new task requires and translate that understanding into appropriate physical action. In-context learning (ICL) for robots supports this process by using demonstrations and interaction to direct existing competence with neural parameters held fixed during deployment. We organize this literature review around the interfaces connecting contextual evidence to execution, distinguishing four families: context-conditioned policies, geometric demonstration transfer, world-model-based control, and skill- and agent-based execution. Comparing these interfaces clarifies their transfer assumptions and the roles of training, correspondence, and memory in making context useful. Across manipulation and navigation, we examine how these mechanisms preserve taught requirements as objects, environments, and execution conditions change. This analysis links method design to evaluation practices that distinguish responsiveness to teaching, physical transfer, and benefits from retained experience. The resulting agenda connects compositional task acquisition and faithful transfer with physical recursive self-improvement, in which experience improves the ability to learn subsequent tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [Multi-Agent Flow Matching with Decoupled Generative Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38133)
*Ruoyu Lin,Magnus Egerstedt,Fabio Pasqualetti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DeGG-Flow：一种带解耦生成引导的多智能体流匹配框架，能为共享与私有耦合需求提供可行性条件、有限时域收敛保证及 Wasserstein 偏差界，并直接生成满足硬约束的多智能体/多对象结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型虽能建模复杂多模态分布，但通常无法形式化保证生成对象满足硬约束；在多智能体生成中，硬需求可能依赖多个智能体共同满足，且每个智能体需独立确定自身引导输入，不能依赖其他智能体同时计算的引导输入。

Method: 将生成过程建模为控制仿射动力系统，针对两类耦合需求设计引导条件：依赖多智能体共同满足的共享需求，以及每个智能体依赖邻居的私有需求。为两类需求建立可行性条件与有限时域收敛保证，并推导刻画引导引起分布偏移的 Wasserstein 界。

Result: 在多机器人协作通过重构环境跨越空间间隙任务，以及带可供性要求的多对象场景生成任务上验证 DeGG-Flow；两种应用中均直接生成满足所有对应硬需求的对象，并可扩展到训练时未见的团队规模。

Conclusion: DeGG-Flow 为多智能体流匹配提供了解耦生成引导与形式化保证，能够处理共享和私有耦合硬约束，并在未见团队规模下保持约束满足能力。

Abstract: Generative modeling is widely used for producing diverse objects from complex, multimodal distributions. However, its expressivity does not, in general, come with formal guarantees that the generated objects satisfy hard constraints or requirements. In multi-agent generation, this problem becomes more challenging because a hard requirement can depend on multiple agents, while each agent may need to determine its own guidance input without relying on the simultaneously computed guidance inputs of other agents. To this end, we introduce DeGG-Flow, a general framework for multi-agent flow matching with decoupled generative guidance. By representing the generative process as a control-affine dynamical system, we develop guidance conditions for two classes of coupled requirements: shared requirements whose satisfaction depends on multiple agents together, and private requirements associated with each individual agent dependent on its neighbors. For both classes, we establish feasibility conditions and finite-horizon convergence guarantees. We further derive a Wasserstein bound that characterizes the distributional deviation induced by the guidance. We demonstrate DeGG-Flow on multi-robot collaboration for crossing a spatial gap by reconfiguring the environment, and on multi-object scene generation with affordance requirements. Across both applications, DeGG-Flow directly generates objects that satisfy all corresponding hard requirements, including at team sizes unseen during training.

</details>


### [133] [Diffusion Policy Improvement with Proposal-Conditioned Refinement Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36812)
*Junhyun Ha,Juho Lee,Byungwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Proposal-Conditioned Refinement Flows（PReFlow），一种结合 critic 引导的 proposal 选择与条件 refinement flow 的离线 RL 策略提取方法；在 OGBench 50 个任务上离线性能有竞争力，在线微调后 500K 环境步达到 91% 聚合得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散/流策略虽能建模复杂行为，但对其与行为策略的 KL 散度进行惩罚，会抑制行为密度低但 critic 值高的动作；直接精炼行为提议虽可替代，但高斯或确定性编辑器难以表达同一提议对应的多个分离高价值模式。

Method: PReFlow 将基于 critic 的 proposal 选择与条件 refinement flow 联合优化。其 KL 正则化目标的最优解诱导出在高斯平滑行为先验下的 Gibbs 策略：refinement flow 可表达多个高价值模态，而以 proposal 为中心的高斯参考项约束动作大幅偏移。该高斯参考还支持从采样端点与 critic 梯度得到无模拟、闭式的伴随匹配目标，从而仅用单一速度回归损失训练，无需反向伴随求解。

Result: 在 50 个 OGBench 任务上，PReFlow 的离线性能与对比方法相比具有竞争力，并在在线微调后取得最高聚合分数，500K 环境步达到 91%。

Conclusion: PReFlow 通过 proposal 条件化流精炼，在保持行为先验约束的同时提升对高 critic 值多模态动作的建模能力，并获得优异的离线与在线微调表现。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow policies can model complex behaviors in offline reinforcement learning (RL). However, penalizing their KL divergence from the behavior policy can discourage actions having high critic values with low behavior density. Directly refining behavior proposals may be an alternative, yet Gaussian or deterministic editors limit expressiveness to represent multiple separated modes for the same proposal. In this work, we introduce Proposal-Conditioned Refinement Flows (PReFlow), a policy extraction method combining critic-based proposal selection with a conditional refinement flow. To optimize proposal selection and refinement together, we formulate a KL-regularized objective whose optimum induces a Gibbs policy over final actions under a Gaussian-smoothed behavior prior. The refinement flow can represent multiple high value modes, while a proposal-centered Gaussian reference regulates large action changes. This Gaussian reference further enables us to make use of simulation-free, closed form adjoint matching targets from sampled endpoints and critic gradients, yielding a single velocity regression loss without a backward adjoint solve. On 50 OGBench tasks, PReFlow achieves competitive offline performance and the highest aggregate score among the compared methods after online fine-tuning, reaching 91\% after 500K environment steps.

</details>


### [134] [Action Chunking Proximal Policy Optimization with Feedback Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36250)
*Sanghyun Hahn,Jonghyun Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出动作分块 PPO（ACPPO）及其带闭环校正版本 ACPPO-Corr，用分块 actor 配合标准状态价值 critic，避免学习高维动作块 Q 函数，并通过逐步反馈校正器提升接触丰富任务中的反应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作分块可在强化学习中提供时间抽象，但现有方法在高维机器人控制中常受限于两点：动作块价值函数随动作维度和块长度增长而难以学习；开环执行动作块缺乏块内反馈，降低接触丰富任务中的反应性与适应性。

Method: 提出 ACPPO：一种 PPO 扩展，使用 chunked actor 规划短动作序列，但保留标准 state-value critic，从而避免 chunked Q-functions。进一步提出 ACPPO-Corr：在动作块规划器上加入 stepwise feedback corrector，在每个块内在线调整已规划动作，实现块级规划与闭环局部反馈结合。

Result: 在 IsaacGym 和 Bi-DexHands 的 25 个仿真机器人任务上评估，涵盖 locomotion、arm manipulation 和 dexterous hand-object interaction。ACPPO-Corr 在总体聚合性能上优于所评估方法，并在决策频率敏感与决策频率中性两个任务子集上均表现最佳。消融实验表明中等 chunk 长度效果最好，且 corrector regularization 对平衡块级规划与局部反馈很重要。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在在线 PPO 中，当块级规划与闭环校正相结合时，动作分块可以有效应用于高维机器人控制，并提升对决策频率和接触丰富任务的适应能力。代码已开源。

Abstract: Action chunking provides temporal abstraction in reinforcement learning by selecting short action sequences instead of individual actions, but many existing approaches face two limitations in high-dimensional robotic control. First, many rely on value functions over action chunks, which can be difficult to learn as action dimensionality and chunk length grow. Second, executing chunks open-loop removes within-chunk feedback, limiting reactivity in contact-rich tasks. We present Action Chunking PPO (ACPPO), a PPO extension that uses a chunked actor while retaining a standard state-value critic, thereby avoiding chunked Q-functions. We further propose ACPPO-Corr, which augments the chunk planner with a stepwise feedback corrector that adjusts planned actions online within each chunk. Across 25 simulated robotics tasks from IsaacGym and Bi-DexHands, spanning locomotion, arm manipulation, and dexterous hand-object interaction, ACPPO-Corr achieves the strongest aggregate performance among evaluated methods and performs best on both decision-frequency-sensitive and decision-frequency-neutral task subsets. Ablations show that moderate chunk lengths work best and that corrector regularization is important for balancing chunk-level planning with local feedback. These results suggest that action chunking can be effective in online PPO when chunk-level planning is paired with closed-loop correction. The code is available at: this https URL .

</details>
